## Einträge

### 2026-10-20 · Release State Metadata for <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834051*

Users can review release state information for SAP Datasphere views. The release states help catalog users understand the view's lifecycle stage. They can also filter to find views at different release states in their lifecycle.  
See [Catalog Asset Details](afccc581146542c485a52563167e23cc.html).  

### 2026-10-20 · Monitor Task Chain Status with a Graphical Display

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834055*

The new task chain monitor graphical view feature provides a visual view of a task chain that is representative of the data builder editor.  
This helps identify the flow of task chains and provides color coded object level status of tasks in the task chain.  
See [Monitoring Task Chains](4142201ec1aa49faad89a688a2f1852c.html).  

### 2026-10-20 · Expression Evaluator Task for Dynamic Task Chain Calculation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834096*

With the new Expression Evaluator task you can evaluate expressions using input and output parameters within task chains. Use it to transform data, perform calculations, and create conditional values that can be reused as input parameters in subsequent tasks.  
See [Run Expression Evaluator Tasks in a Task Chain](5064ede215654f2eb6e18b86a29dfab5.html).  

### 2026-10-20 · Define a Time Zone When Deleting Records With Filters on Date or Datetime Columns

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834072*

If your filter is defined on a column where values are of data type "Date" or "Datetime", you can add a time zone using the selector. A suffix with the time zone in brackets is then added to the column name in the deletion filter.  
See:  
- [Controlling Deletion of Local Table Records](2a59b7142e1b4d478b0bf4063084261e.html)  
- [Delete Data From Your Local Tables (File)](872ad509995a451890bf8b80b73ec0e6.html)  

### 2026-10-20 · Connect to External REST APIs via Custom Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834054*

You can now connect SAP Datasphere to external applications that offer a REST API by creating custom connection types.  
A custom connection type is a reusable blueprint that maps external API resources to structured entities, allowing you to load compatible JSON-based data from third-party systems or customer-built REST APIs into the SAP Datasphere object store using replication flows.  
Custom connection types extend standard SAP Datasphere-managed connectivity when predefined connection types do not exist. They are available in spaces with storage type SAP HANA Data Lake Files (file spaces). Connections created from a custom connection type are independent of the connection type, meaning that later changes to the connection type do not affect existing connections.  
See:  
- [Creating a Custom Connection Type](9c582ca295164a538cb61b7e7beb6719.html)  
- [Create a Connection from a Custom Connection Type](d0653d37167a49679d0a3e909547a674.html)  
- [Rest API Custom Connection Sources for Replication Flows](cc0fad818e9b4d409c43969195d3fd82.html)  

### 2026-10-20 · Automatic Data-Product Provider Connection During Content Transport

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834056*

The BD Administrator can now define system mappings to help your transported content automatically connect to the correct data products. When you import a content package installed from an SAP Business Data Cloud data product into an SAP Datasphere tenant, SAP Datasphere will automatically connect to the correct data-product provider system instance.  
For more information, see [System Mapping for Content Transport Between SAP Datasphere Tenants](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BUSINESS_DATA_CLOUD/f7acf8c9dad54e99b5ce5ebc633ed8e1/4a4024c409ef427ebe285b8e60a9e58a.html) in SAP Business Data Cloud documentation.  

### 2026-10-20 · Assign More Than 100 Spaces to Users

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834046*

There is no longer any limit to the number of spaces you can assign a user to via scoped roles.  
See [Create a Scoped Role to Assign Privileges to Users in Spaces](b5c4e0b6c462414783ebbfc053815521.html).  

### 2026-10-20 · AI SQL Code Generation in Transformation Flows on File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834099*

You can now generate SQL code in transformation flows on file by prompting the built-in SAP Business AI. You can create a view from scratch by specifying your sources and what you want to output or modify existing SQL code. CTEs (Common Table Expressions) WITH clauses are supported.  
See :  
- [Generate SQL Code with AI in a File Space](15452a78b52749b59b0f133c981f17ab.html)  
- [Create a SQL View in a Transformation Flow on File](06c4e72bcbee46568be499c77a3a0d00.html)  

### 2026-10-20 · <span class="ph uicontrol">Data Pipeline Analyzer</span> Displays Impact on related <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span> Stories

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.22 · SAP-ID 590834059*

When issues occur in analytical models or exposed views, related SAP Analytics Cloud stories can be displayed in the impact summary and pipeline graph. This enables you to identify affected analytics content, navigate directly to impacted objects, and gain visibility into downstream business impact from a single location.  
See [Viewing SAP Analytics Cloud Objects in the Data Pipeline Analyzer](55d92f3d718d4246bca5782325b078b2.html).  

### 2026-10-07 · Restart Replication Flow Objects via API

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.21 · SAP-ID 590834057*

You can now restart replication flow objects via API.  
See [Controlling Replication Flows via API](5e60730b5b4c45f49e261af6fdaad72b.html).  

### 2026-10-07 · Manual Check for Data Product Schema Changes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.21 · SAP-ID 590834052*

Data products sometimes have schema changes that aren't updated in the installed versions. As a result, installed data products can have inconsistencies when compared to the corresponding catalog version.  
You can check for schema inconsistencies on the Data Product Access page. Once you identify the affected data products, you can resolve the inconsistencies by updating their installations.  
See [Resolving Schema Inconsistencies](f4d469b644af4c30b8cd071d07a2babc.html).  

### 2026-10-07 · Deprecation of the Business Builder

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Announcement · Version: 2026.21 · SAP-ID 590834047*

The Business Builder is deprecated and will not receive further updates. We recommend that you migrate your consumption models to analytic models, which are now the preferred way to expose data to SAP Analytics Cloud and other clients, tools and apps.  

### 2026-10-07 · Consistent Size Display Across All Monitors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.21 · SAP-ID 590834060*

We've standardized size displays across all monitors using a dynamic decimal format for improved readability and consistency.  
With dynamic scaling, values are automatically adjusted so they remain between 1 and 1,000. Instead of displaying very large or very small numbers, the unit changes automatically:  
- KB for sizes greater than 1,000 bytes  
- MB for sizes greater than 1,000,000 bytes  
- GB for sizes greater than 1,000,000,000 bytes  
- TB for sizes greater than 1,000,000,000,000 bytes, etc...  
This makes size values easier to read and compare across SAP Datasphere.  

### 2026-09-28 · Use Short-Lived Access Tokens for Google BigQuery Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.20 · SAP-ID 590834067*

You can now use the Workload Identity Federation authentication type to let replication flows securely access Google BigQuery with short-lived access tokens.  
See:  
- [Google BigQuery Connections](30ed77de13864368bdc596099b37ed70.html)  
- [Configuring Workload Identity Federation for Google BigQuery Connections](7962a49923d84644910b1c6001df0145.html)  

### 2026-09-28 · Use OAuth 2.0 Authentication with X.509 Client Certificate for ABAP-based Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.20 · SAP-ID 590834068*

You can now use authentication type OAuth 2.0 with the Client Credentials with X.509 Client Certificate grant type for SAP ABAP (on-premise), SAP S/4HANA On-Premise, SAP ECC, and SAP BW connections. This certificate-based grant type is the most secure option for the connection types and is selected by default when you choose OAuth 2.0 as the authentication type.  
This authentication type is available for connections that use Cloud Connector. For SAP ABAP (on-premise) and SAP S/4HANA On-Premise, the OAuth 2.0 authentication type with client credentials was introduced in an earlier version of SAP Datasphere. For SAP BW and SAP ECC connections, OAuth 2.0 authentication is new and supports both grant types: client credentials and client credentials with X.509 client certificate.  
Note the following for all connection types:  
- Remote tables are not supported with the OAuth 2.0 authentication type. This also applies to model import for SAP S/4HANA connections.  
- For SAP BW and SAP ECC connections, only replication flows are supported.  
- For SAP ABAP and SAP S/4HANA On-Premise connections, replication flows and data flows are supported.  
- When you change an existing connection from basic authentication to authentication type OAuth 2.0, remote tables are disabled. For SAP BW and SAP ECC connections, data flows are also disabled.  
See:  
- [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html)  
- [SAP BW Connections](e589041e80264f43b6c209c407336376.html)  
- [SAP ECC Connections](e546ccd61af54bf49a0f531a43fe0961.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Connections](a49a1e3cc50f4af89711d8306bdd8f26.html)  

### 2026-09-28 · Tags for Sensitive Data in Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.20 · SAP-ID 590834097*

Information-sensitive data is now visually highlighted in the analytic model with the tags Sensitive Personal Data and Personal Data.  
This improves data security awareness and compliance.  

### 2026-09-28 · Simplified Metric Names for Transformation Flow Monitoring

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.20 · SAP-ID 590834033*

Metric names and values in transformation flow monitoring Run Details now display in simplified format instead of technical wording. For example, EXECUTION_MODE: 0 now shows as Execution Mode: Sequential. You can immediately understand run results without referencing internal documentation.  
See [Metrics for Transformation Flows](b42fa5b6f4e04a9491efa9bf7dab0929.html).  

### 2026-09-28 · New Replication Flow Monitoring APIs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.20 · SAP-ID 590834058*

You can now start, stop, pause, and resume replication flows (and pause and resume individual replication flow objects) via API.  
See [Controlling Replication Flows via API](5e60730b5b4c45f49e261af6fdaad72b.html).  

### 2026-09-28 · New API Endpoint and New Command for Data Providers

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.20 · SAP-ID 590834092*

The API endpoint /dsc/providers/:providerIdentifier/assignUsers is available to assign and activate data provider memberships.  
For more information, see the API documentation on the [SAP Business Accelerator Hub](https://api.sap.com/api/DataSharingCockpit/overview).  
You can also use the command datasphere marketplace providers assign-users in the SAP Datasphere command line interface.  
See [Managing Data Marketplace Data Providers via the Command Line](ca9f910607da4a9e8a2385f655edab95.html).  

### 2026-09-28 · Export Data of Data Preview

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.20 · SAP-ID 590834098*

In the data preview of an analytic model, you can export the data as an Excel or CSV file. This enables you to share the intermediate results with colleagues, and to validate the calculations and measures.  
See: [Preview Data in an Analytic Model](9f1fa73a33424cbe9bac3064702c0dbd.html)  

### 2026-09-28 · Data Protection: Tracking of Personal and Sensitive Data in Transformation Flows in <span class="ph uicontrol">SAP HANA</span> Runtime or <span class="ph uicontrol">Apache Spark</span> Runtime

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.20 · SAP-ID 590834101*

Trace personal and sensitive personal data installed from an SAP BDC data product as it is processed in transformation flows. Flow operators display the personal data tags on relevant columns and these tags are inherited by target tables to which personal data is written.  
See :  
- [Creating a Transformation Flow](f7161e6c20204672ac4a6d90c81762e4.html)  
- [Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html)  
- [Modeling with Personal Data](fd0d4e63b73c4b8bb2efbd2399a9f9b9.html)  

### 2026-09-09 · Transporting Runtime Settings for Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834065*

You can now select which runtime settings are included when a replication flow is exported using the Transport app. The following settings can be marked for transport: Delta Load Run, Delta Load Frequency, Thread Limit for Initial Load, Email Notification Configuration, and Capture Source Schema Changes.  
See [Working With Existing Replication Flow Runs](da62e1ee746448e8bc043e1be4377cbe.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Support for Schema Changes from ABAP SLT Sources to Local Table File Targets

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834064*

Replication flows now support compatible "add column" schema changes from ABAP SLT sources to Local Table (file) targets. You can apply new nullable non-key columns to the target without reinitializing the replication flow.  
See [Modify Replication Flow Object Schemas and Settings](a24c71f3ba7548909534d4cb52cefbfc.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Side-by-side Major Version Installation of Intelligent Content

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834100*

If your SAP Datasphere is part of an SAP Business Data Cloud formation, you can now install multiple major versions of intelligent content on the same source system. Use this to safely test and migrate workflows to new major versions without disrupting existing processes.  
All major versions use the source system's ingestion and preparation spaces, but each version creates its own model space to contain its data products. The spaces are identified by version numbers.  
See [Installing Intelligent Content](344999cbd4ff4d8188fb67b4ca6ea33f.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Release Status Indicators for Data Product Objects and Columns

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590833479*

The data product details page provides improved visibility of object (or entity) and column release statuses. Consumers can use this information to identify deprecated or decommissioned data objects and their recommended successors. This enables proactive migration planning and helps avoid dependencies on obsolete data during modeling and integration activities.  
See [Data Product Details](71f4d1599dba4aa59e6682a314650cf7.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Object Store Requests Consumed by Category

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834034*

Object Store Requests are now metered and consumed in three separate categories: Control Plane, Read, and Write. Each category has its own price per 1,000 requests. Previously, all request types were charged at a single blended rate.  
You can view your consumption per category in the Capacity Monitor and in the consumption cards on the Tenant Configuration screen.  
See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html)  

### 2026-09-09 · MySQL Sources for Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834066*

You can now use MySQL and the corresponding connection type to add sources to replication flows.  
See:  
- [MySQL Sources for Replication Flows](4fe921866ee44d6e8bb13d92f378d3a2.html)  
- [MySQL Connections](520a2601fd7a45f084e5ed1d30c6ebfa.html)  

### 2026-09-09 · Initial and Delta and Delta Only Load Types Now Supported for Local Table (File) as Source

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834061*

When using a local table (file) as the source in a replication flow, you can now use the Initial and Delta and Delta Only load types in addition to the already supported Initial Only load type. To use delta load types, delta capture must be enabled on the source table.  
See [SAP Datasphere Local Table (File) Sources for Replication Flows](bdff6b91cf4b44f895429c23566b3ea0.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Generate a Business Summary and an Errors and Impacts Summary With AI

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834082*

Leverage AI to gain deeper insights into your data pipeline. You can generate a Business Summary to quickly understand how your pipeline is structured, or create an Errors and Impacts Summary to identify issues and pinpoint any blocked data movements.  
See [Generate Summaries of the Data Pipeline Analyzer With AI](1e72ea2b540f412c8bcd5d4751560ebc.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Fallback Text for Attribute Translation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834104*

When creating a text association, you can now specify a text column from your fact or dimension to provide Fallback Text in the event that no translation is available for the selected record/language in the text entity.  
See [Create an Association to Define a Semantic Relationship Between Entities](66c6998af9974dac8f54a46515777560.html)  

### 2026-09-09 · Data Validation: Text Entities

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834108*

New validation rules can help optimize and ensure the completeness of your translations.  
See [Validating View Data](ed4063dc7f53436d806ea48f2fd3949a.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Data Product Life Cycle Status

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590833478*

The data product details page shows the lifecycle status. Consumers can filter by lifecycle status, such as active, provisioning, or data-loading. This information helps data stewards track and manage data products throughout their operational lifecycle.  
See [Data Product Details](71f4d1599dba4aa59e6682a314650cf7.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Data Product Access Request Management

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834053*

The workflow for accessing data products has been streamlined across SAP Business Data Cloud. You'll now be able to send access requests for data products, track their statuses, and receive timely notifications when a request is approved or rejected.  
See [Obtaining Access to Data Products](998fb5f447d04021baf497375261c9d1.html)  

### 2026-09-09 · Certificate-Based Authentication for Database Analysis Users

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834036*

X.509 certificate-based authentication is now available for database analysis users. This enhancement strengthens security for technical users by reducing reliance on passwords, while maintaining password-based authentication as the default method.  
See [Create a Database Analysis User to Debug Database Issues](c28145bcb76c4415a1ec6265dd2a4c11.html).  

### 2026-09-09 · Automatic Extraction of Account Dimension Members from <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span> Classic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590833477*

When account dimension members are added, deleted, or updated for classic models, the catalog automatically extracts the latest metadata from the SAP Analytics Cloud source system. This ensures that the catalog assets are current with account dimension member changes.  

### 2026-09-09 · Add or Clear Filters on Columns in Data Integration Monitor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834073*

You can now apply and clear column filters on Data Integration Monitor overview pages. Active filter conditions are displayed directly in the user interface for improved visibility.  

### 2026-09-09 · AI SQL Code Generation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2026.19 · SAP-ID 590834123*

You can now generate SQL code by prompting the built-in SAP Business AI. You can create a view from scratch by specifying your sources and what you want to output or modify existing SQL code.  
See [Generate SQL Code with AI](d1530656d42f44f68b6265666ec35531.html).  

### 2026-08-25 · Use Input and Output Parameters in API Tasks

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834062*

You can now define and use input and output parameters in API tasks. This provides more flexibility and allows for better reuse when working with API tasks. In this context, we've also added the GET and DELETE methods for API tasks in synchronous mode and the use of an ID output parameter for status retrieval for API tasks in asynchronous mode.  
See:  
- [Run API Tasks in a Task Chain](9a8489ed7443436197fbd8b8ffba61ab.html)  
- [Using Parameters in API Tasks](6b6d6004c2c94a71a8a732718fed100c.html)  

### 2026-08-25 · Protect Data Export for Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834102*

You can now change the protect data export configuration for analytic models that are part of Intelligent applications.  

### 2026-08-25 · Monitor File Space Storage Usage

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834128*

You can now view detailed storage usage for each file space (spaces with a storage type of SAP HANA Data Lake Files) directly from the space page.  
See [Monitor Your Space Storage Consumption](94fe6c13f6a340288cd50ee355566591.html).  

### 2026-08-25 · Improved User Experience Through Clear Differentiation Between Local Tables and Local Tables (File)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834105*

You can now clearly differentiate in the different areas of SAP Datasphere if your object is a local table (stored in a standard space) or a local table (file) stored in a file space.  
See :  
- [Creating a Local Table](2509fe4d86aa472b9858164b55b38077.html)  
- [Creating a Local Table (File)](d21881b121bc4703861be6ead4aea2ab.html)  

### 2026-08-25 · Flatten Operator for Transformation Flows on File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834103*

Simplify complex star-schema data models with the new Flatten operator in Apache Spark transformation flows. The operator automatically joins tables to create flattened views, streamlining data preparation for machine learning, AI, and analytics use cases.  
See [Creating a Flatten Operator](34f48faf744a429f9db581e5b43b920a.html).  

### 2026-08-25 · Extended Access to Monitoring Task Logs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834035*

More users can now monitor task logs. Access is no longer restricted to global roles. Users with scoped roles may can also monitor task logs when granted access to a space with the required scoped privileges: Data Warehouse General (-R------); Data Warehouse Data Integration (-R------) to view data integration task logs in the Task Logs app; Data Warehouse Data Integration (--U-----) to cancel a task run. The DW Integrator role template, for example, grants these privileges.  
See [Reviewing Task Logs](399e52f2b3824026a2c427a346fa792d.html).  

### 2026-08-25 · Enhanced Capacity Unit Monitoring

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590833454*

The Capacities Monitoring application has been enhanced with resource-specific consumption cards and charts. These insights help you analyze capacity unit consumption by service, monitor usage trends, and identify major contributors to overall capacity utilization.  

### 2026-08-25 · Enable Delta Vector for Your Local Table (File)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834110*

In the table editor, you can enable deletion vectors to optimize how delete and update operations are handled in local tables (file).  
See [Creating a Local Table (File)](d21881b121bc4703861be6ead4aea2ab.html)  

### 2026-08-25 · Data Preview in Business Builder

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834048*

The runtime of the data preview in the Business Builder has been changed to align with the data preview of the analytic model.  
This gives you all the options that you have in the data preview of the analytic model.  
The preview is available for consumption models, but not for perspectives anymore. Also, the preview of business entities and authorization scenarios is no longer available. You also can't save your data preview anymore.  

### 2026-08-25 · <span class="ph uicontrol">Initial Only </span>Load Type for Local Table (File) Targets

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.18 · SAP-ID 590834070*

You can now use the Initial Only load type when replicating data to a local table (file) target with delta capturing switched off. This applies to object store sources (Amazon Simple Storage Service, Google Cloud Storage, Microsoft Azure Data Lake Gen2, SAP HANA Cloud Data Lake Files), HANA views, and all other sources that support initial-only load.  
See [SAP Datasphere Targets for Replication Flows](12c45eb2659f4069b29ef4d69bdd9070.html).  

### 2026-08-11 · Side Bar Navigation Reorganized

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834127*

The navigation in SAP Datasphere has been redesigned, with applications now reorganized based on their functions. Additionally, the About dialog has now moved under the user profile:  
See :  
- [Navigating in SAP Datasphere](bd79b74face14b17a4cfa844a2fd36c7.html)  
- [Changing SAP Datasphere Settings](1084796d09464e78870f32cab8584dfc.html)  

### 2026-08-11 · Resolve Erroneous Records with the Data Remediation Table

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834107*

Automatically flag and correct erroneous records without interrupting your transformation flow. When Support Data Validation is enabled on a Python operator, flagged records are captured in a dedicated Data Remediation table. Review issues in the Data Integration monitor, correct values individually or in bulk, and reprocess corrected records in the next run. The system tracks record status and automatically merges corrections back into your target table.  
See [Resolving Erroneous Records Using the Data Remediation Table](0cbcc49d36f345208ffd97d3695a141e.html).  

### 2026-08-11 · Performance Improvement for Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834069*

To improve overall performance of task chain runs, we have reduced the idle times between task runs.  

### 2026-08-11 · Oracle Sources Now Support Delta Load Types

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834074*

You can now use the Initial and Delta and Delta Only load types for Oracle sources. Previously, Oracle sources supported only the Initial Only load type.  
See [Select Source and Target Connections for Replication Flows](10891192186c4920b08939a7b46adc79.html).  

### 2026-08-11 · New Partition Type Available For Your Local Tables: Dynamic Range

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834111*

To improve scalability and reduce operational overhead, you can now select a new partition type: Dynamic Range. This option monitors incoming data in the “OTHERS” partition and automatically creates new partitions as needed. It ensures efficient data distribution, maintains optimal query performance, and seamlessly adapts to increasing data volumes.  
See [Partitioning Local Tables](03191f36e9144b2aaa47b8c9eea039c1.html)  

### 2026-08-11 · Display Your System Information

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834037*

The System Information Bar and Favicon settings have been relocated to the System  Administration  Default Appearance tab.  
See [Display Your System Information](6bdd79878afa4ec5bcd9d3502158a06e.html).  

### 2026-08-11 · Data Privacy and Protection－Personal Data and Sensitive Personal Data Tagging in Monitors and Task Chain Editor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834075*

You can now trace personal and sensitive personal data in your objects. Objects containing personal data are automatically tagged in the task chain editors, and are displayed in a new column "Tags" in the Local Tables, Views and Remote Tables monitors.  
See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html), [Monitoring Remote Tables](4dd95d7bff1f48b399c8b55dbdd34b9e.html), [Monitoring Local Tables](374046156e5b47599fc9b96c8c3a4dce.html), [Persisting and Monitoring Views](9af04c990f294fd28c00f46763dd8b0d.html).  

### 2026-08-11 · Create up to 20 File Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834129*

You can now create up to 20 file spaces (spaces with a storage type of SAP HANA Data Lake Files), compared to the previous limit of 5.  
See [Create a File Space to Load Data in the Object Store](947444683e524cfd9169d7671b72ba0c.html).  

### 2026-08-11 · Copy Spaces and their Contents

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.17 · SAP-ID 590834130*

When copying a space, you can now select some or all of its source spaces (which share data to it), and some or all of its consumer spaces (to which it shares data), in order to create a new stack of spaces sharing data between them, independently of the original spaces.  
See [Copy Spaces and their Contents](73068ac8e1934615b419d8c6c4095a9a.html).  
This feature is available for all spaces, but can be particularly useful for copying the preparation and model spaces installed as intelligent content, in order to extend them to incorporate custom fields.  
See [Extending Intelligent Content](3c158685865d4b408938a148e828e21f.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · SAP BW/ECC Sources Now Support Delta Load Types

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834079*

You can now use all supported load types when replicating data from SAP BW/ECC sources. In addition to Initial Only, SAP BW/ECC sources now support Initial and Delta and Delta Only load types.  
See [Select Source and Target Connections for Replication Flows](10891192186c4920b08939a7b46adc79.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Personal Data and Sensitive Personal Data Tagging

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834124*

You can now trace personal and sensitive personal data installed from an SAP BDC data product across the entities that consume it.  
Columns containing personal data are automatically tagged in the Catalog, and these tags are visible in the table and view editors. As subsequent entities consume this data, they inherit these tags, which are also visible in the Data Viewer and in the impact and lineage analysis diagram.  
See: [Modeling with Personal Data](fd0d4e63b73c4b8bb2efbd2399a9f9b9.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · OData Consumption API: Partial String Filtering

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834115*

New filter options now allow you to filter your text data based on partial matches:  
- startswith(<field>,'<value>') - String value starts with  
- endswith(<field>,'<value>') - String value ends with  
- contains(<field>,'<value>') - String value contains  
See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Multiple Scoped License Installations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834093*

You can now install the same data product into multiple target spaces using different scoped licenses, each with its own activation key.  
See [Changing License Scope](7890c5b332504a4b954c981b5f871e49.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Monitor Your Partition Details

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834080*

From the Local table monitor, you can display the partitioning details of your local tables.  
See [Monitoring Your Partitions](3c9de9eb77e649a98450f4fd67a5f7ba.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Manage Packages via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834109*

You can now list, create, read, update, delete, and export packages via the datasphere command line interface.  
See [Managing Packages via the Command Line](9dfeea1833ce4b61a3304e0dbb4468b8.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Local Table (Files) as Replication Flow Sources

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834077*

You can now use Local Table (Files) as source objects in replication flows. This allows you to replicate data from a Local Table (File) to supported SAP and non-SAP target systems without re-extracting the data from the original source.  
See [SAP Datasphere Local Table (File) Sources for Replication Flows](bdff6b91cf4b44f895429c23566b3ea0.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Intelligent Content that Combines Data Products from Multiple Systems

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834106*

If your SAP Datasphere is part of an SAP Business Data Cloud formation, you can now install intelligent content that combines data products from multiple systems. Each source system creates (or re-uses) its own ingestion and preparation spaces to contain its data products, and an additional cross-source space is created that combines data from all the source systems. The source-specific and cross-source spaces are identified by aliases.  
See [Reviewing Installed Intelligent Content](644648756d334daaaf35d4fc9a0feeda.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Dynamically Generate SAML Assertions for SAP SuccessFactors Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834076*

When using the OAuth 2.0 authentication type with SAP SuccessFactors connections, you can now use automated, dynamic generation and rotation of SAML assertions to avoid manual efforts that have been required before.  
For dynamically generating SAML assertions, SAP Cloud Identity Services (IAS) is used as Identity Provider.  
See:  
- [SAP SuccessFactors Connections](39df02030d4b411487bacecf9afea4e8.html)  
- [Configuring Dynamic Generation of SAML Assertions for SAP SuccessFactors Connections](63c8260a7f574e6e8e3c386656c93b00.html)  
Note  
Access to APIs based on HTTP Basic Authentication will be deleted on November 12, 2027. We strongly recommend to adopt the OAuth 2.0 authentication type for your connections.  
For more information, see:  
- [Deprecation of Basic Authentication for APIs](https://help.sap.com/doc/62fddbd651204629b46bbccbabf886ba/cloud/en-US/fcc05a902b4140e585d968c2fe4a96bc.html) in SAP SuccessFactors What's New Viewer  
- SAP Note [3774454](https://me.sap.com/notes/3774454)  

### 2026-07-28 · Delta Capture Flag Handling for Transformation Flows on File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834113*

- Insert records from delta-enabled source tables to non-delta targets.  
- For initial-only loads, automatically clear the target table data before each run by enabling Delete All Before Loading.  
Transformation flows on file support two new features. You can now: See [Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Delete Logs Associated With Apache Spark Tasks

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834038*

In Configuration  Task, you can monitor the size of logs generated by Apache Spark tasks, and configure a deletion task to free up space in your tenant.  
See [Delete Task Logs to Reduce Storage Consumption](c6902024ecd74956b4ba2d1c67ccb073.html)  

### 2026-07-28 · Cloud Connector Support for Cloud Object Store and Microsoft Azure SQL Database Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834084*

You can now use Cloud Connector for object store connections and Microsoft Azure SQL Database.  
Cloud Connector acts as a reverse invoke proxy and routes traffic through a TLS tunnel, allowing you to use replication flows to replicate data from or, if applicable, write data to cloud object stores and Microsoft Azure SQL Database without having to expose public endpoints.  
See:  
- [Amazon Simple Storage Service Connections](a7b660a0a4ef4a4fbee57b44f5b2147d.html)  
- [Google Cloud Storage Connections](aec242c29188408c9ebe1a3ab63ce28b.html)  
- [Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen2 Connections](cd06b3c5ab5147c0905e3fa8abd13eb1.html)  
- [Microsoft Azure SQL Database Connections](46343fc1c4544fa9a075d97f84d39826.html)  
- [Microsoft OneLake Connections](057fa4b51c734e679dd70eec9514839d.html)  

### 2026-07-28 · Centralized Reusable Mail Lists for Task Chain and Replication Flow Notifications

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834071*

You can now centrally per tenant create and manage reusable mail lists for email notifications across task chains, notification tasks, and replication flows. This feature streamlines notification management and enhances data protection by preventing exposure of personal email addresses to unauthorized users.  
Existing lists of recipient email addresses in your tenant have been migrated to corresponding reusable mail lists. These lists are assigned to the task chains or replication flows for which they were originally created. The names of the new mailing lists are generated as follows:  
- For task chains and notification tasks: <space_technical_name>:<task_chain_technical_name>  
- For replication flows: <space_technical_name>:<replication_flow_technical_name>  
See:  
- [Configure Email Notification for Task Chains and Notification Tasks](7ff6a4e584a345a88d002c18c1fc321e.html)  
- [Configure Email Notification for Replication Flow Failure at Object Level](5dc4db23d3894b10aca6ade3c666554d.html)  

### 2026-07-28 · Automated Error Tracking for Transformation Flows on File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834112*

Track and resolve errors automatically in transformation flows on file by enabling Support Data Validation on the Python operator to generate an error tracking table during deployment. The system captures failed records with error status and detailed error messages, enabling iterative data quality improvement and seamless reprocessing of erroneous records without manual intervention.  
See [Creating a Python Operator](a747acf9d96b450da4b802b80e05a896.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · Apply Compatible Source Schema Changes Without Reinitializing Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834063*

You can now detect and apply compatible source schema changes for replication flows that use SAP ABAP (SLT) sources targets without reinitializing the replication flow. Supported changes include adding nullable non-key columns. When schema changes are detected, review and selectively apply them in the replication flow editor before resuming replication. See:  
- [Configure the Run Settings of a Replication Flow](3f5ba0c5ae3944c1b7279bb989a2a5b5.html)  
- [Modify Replication Flow Object Schemas and Settings](a24c71f3ba7548909534d4cb52cefbfc.html)  

### 2026-07-28 · Apache Spark Metrics Tracking for Resource Consumption

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834039*

A new billable metric is available to track Apache Spark resource consumption. Apache Spark compute block-hours measures the allocated memory capacity provisioned for Apache Spark tasks, expressed in compute block-hours.  
See [Reviewing Task Logs](399e52f2b3824026a2c427a346fa792d.html).  

### 2026-07-28 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP HANA Cloud</span> Connections: DigiCert TLS RSA4096 Root G5 Certificate Required

*Action: Required · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Announcement · Version: 2026.16 · SAP-ID 590834078*

SAP HANA Cloud migrates its server certificates to DigiCert TLS RSA4096 Root G5 by end of November 2026 and will switch exclusively to a G5 certificate chain in all productive landscapes following a dedicated migration schedule.  
Action: If you have connections pointing to SAP HANA Cloud, you must ensure that the new DigiCert TLS RSA4096 Root G5 (DigiCertTLSRSA4096RootG5.crt.pem) is uploaded to the SAP Datasphere trust store (under  (System)     (Configuration)   Security  Certificates).  
See:  
- SAP Note [3399573](https://me.sap.com/notes/3399573)  
- Prerequisites for SAP HANA Cloud in [SAP HANA Connections](e6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9.html#loioe6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9)  

### 2026-06-30 · Work with File Space Objects via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.14 · SAP-ID 590833490*

You can now import and export objects in file spaces via the command line.  
See:  
- [Manage Spaces and Space Access via the Command Line](5eac5b71e2d34c32b63f3d8d47a0b1d0.html)  
- [Manage Modeling Objects and Tasks via the Command Line](6f5c65f209004751aa48f9682ee2ec45.html)  
- [Manage Connectivity via the Command Line](3918242966c74f11858db39c8e5228e4.html)  

### 2026-06-30 · SQL View Transform in Transformation Flows on File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.14 · SAP-ID 590833519*

Create a SQL view transform to combine and transform data using a SQL editor using Apache Spark SQL. Use this feature to build complex data transformations with auto-complete suggestions, drag-and-drop source objects, real-time validation and data preview capabilities.  
See [Create a SQL View in a Transformation Flow on File](06c4e72bcbee46568be499c77a3a0d00.html).  

### 2026-06-30 · New Supported Data Types for Custom Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.14 · SAP-ID 590834094*

You can use hana.SMALLINT and hana.TINYINT data types in your custom data products for SAP Business Data Cloud.  
See :  
- [Creating Data Products for SAP Business Data Cloud](b07e95d07a1e4569b87d9bb57b732bcf.html)  
- [Data Types Supported By Local Tables (File)](2f39104e5bd847919b8daee1580c4f68.html)  

### 2026-06-30 · Install Intelligent Content that Combines Data Products from Multiple Source Systems

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.14 · SAP-ID 590834114*

If your SAP Datasphere is part of an SAP Business Data Cloud formation, you can now install intelligent content that combines data products from multiple systems. Each source system creates (or re-uses) its own ingestion and preparation spaces to contain its data products, and an additional cross-source preparation space is created that combines data from all source systems. The source-specific and cross-source spaces are identified by aliases.  
See [Reviewing Installed Intelligent Content](644648756d334daaaf35d4fc9a0feeda.html).  

### 2026-06-30 · Different Purposes Introduced for Certificate Upload

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.14 · SAP-ID 590834040*

To enable X.509 client certificate-based authentication for Open SQL database users, you can now upload the required X.509 root certificate (root CA) to SAP Datasphere.  
We have introduced the option to select a Purpose when uploading certificates to the SAP Datasphere trust store (under  (System)     (Configuration)   Security  Certificates). For using certificate-based authentication when creating a database user, select X.509 Client (Open SQL). To secure connections, select TLS Server.  
See:  
- [Manage Certificates](46f5467adc5242deb1f6b68083e72994.html)  
- [Create a Database User](798e3fd6707940c3bd2219b2d1ebaac2.html)  

### 2026-06-30 · Certificate-Based Authentication for Database Users

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.14 · SAP-ID 590834131*

X.509 certificate-based authentication is now available as the default method for database users. This enhancement provides stronger security for technical users by reducing reliance on passwords, while still allowing password-based authentication as an alternative. See [Create a Database User](798e3fd6707940c3bd2219b2d1ebaac2.html).  
This authentication method is also available via the Command Line. See [Managing Database Users via the Command Line](cd3b314dd33f407f9ba507dd870ba1f1.html).  

### 2026-06-30 · Additional Replication Flow Actions on the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.14 · SAP-ID 590834081*

You can now pause and resume replication flows and individual replication objects as well as other actions already available via the command line.  
See: [Running and Managing Tasks via the Command Line](2b26a31f197444dea314495bc0008eae.html).  

### 2026-06-16 · Use Snowflake as a Source in Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590833497*

Snowflake is now available as a source connection in replication flows with support for the Initial Only load type.  
See: [Snowflake Sources for Replication Flows](af67fc6241d445e9b49ccd2f03d72f9e.html)  

### 2026-06-16 · Use Oracle as a Source in Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590833494*

You can now use Oracle as a source connection in replication flows to replicate data into supported targets with the Initial Only load type.  
See:  
- [Select Source and Target Connections for Replication Flows](10891192186c4920b08939a7b46adc79.html)  
- [Oracle Connections](c73ae0601d364f47830d339b6e86b7e8.html)  

### 2026-06-16 · Oracle Connections: Enhanced Authentication Option with Oracle Wallet for Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590833493*

You can now use your Oracle wallet to support encrypted communication using SSL and certificate-based authentication via mTLS. SSL support is available both for basic authentication and certificate-based authentication. When using the Wallet authentication type, you can use the connection for replication flows. Remote tables and data flows are not supported with Oracle Wallet.  
To be able to use Oracle wallet, a new section Authentication to select the authentication type and an additional field in the Security section to upload the required server certificate when using SSL have been introduced.  
If you want to use an existing connection for replication flows with Oracle wallet, you need to edit the connection and complete the SSL properties and credentials.  
See [Oracle Connections](c73ae0601d364f47830d339b6e86b7e8.html).  

### 2026-06-16 · Intelligent Content

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590833520*

Intelligent applications are renamed to intelligent content.  
Intelligent content includes prebuilt SAP Analytics Cloud stories and MyMetrics (KPIs) for various SAP lines of business or solution areas, along with flows, views, analytic models and other SAP Analytics Cloud objects to prepare data for them.  
Intelligent content is built on data products, supports analytics, planning, and AI and machine learning use cases, and can be simply and seamlessly installed from the SAP Analytics Cloud cockpit.  
For more information, see [Installing Intelligent Content](344999cbd4ff4d8188fb67b4ca6ea33f.html).  

### 2026-06-16 · Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram Includes Target Systems for Shared Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590833480*

The Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram now includes the target system in the impact of a shared data product. This enhancement provides a clearer view of data impact across all affected systems. You can see how data products are consumed beyond SAP Datasphere installations, which improves impact assessment and strengthens data governance across the entire data ecosystem.  
See [Data Product Details](71f4d1599dba4aa59e6682a314650cf7.html).  

### 2026-06-16 · Enhanced Capacity Unit Monitoring

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590833455*

The Capacity Unit Monitoring tool is enhanced with a customizable date and time range filter, enabling analysis beyond the current month. A new Spaces tab provides detailed consumption insights by space for Premium Outbound Integration, object store usage, and total capacity unit consumption.  
See [Monitoring Capacity Unit Consumption](ba3d05baac854171914c09d64bed7202.html)  

### 2026-06-16 · Dynamic Workload Memory Management to Strengthen Database Resilience

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590833456*

The total statement memory limit for workloads generated by spaces or groups is now dynamically adjusted based on your tenant’s available free memory. You can still set limits, but the system automatically reduces them when insufficient memory is available. This improves system resilience by preserving sufficient capacity during peak usage without requiring manual intervention.  
See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  

### 2026-06-16 · Connection Type Snowflake

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590834087*

You can now create connections to Snowflake and use them as source for replication flows.  
You can configure Cloud Connector for Snowflake with Cloud Connector acting as a reverse invoke proxy routing traffic through a TLS tunnel. This allows you to replicate data from Snowflake databases that don't necessarily have public endpoints.  
See [Snowflake Connections](d9ead8bb44ac456d99a141b4e3f5e973.html).  

### 2026-06-16 · Cloud Connector Support for Confluent Cloud Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.13 · SAP-ID 590833491*

You can now configure Cloud Connector for Connections to Confluent Cloud. Cloud Connector acts as a reverse invoke proxy and routes traffic through a TLS tunnel, allowing you to use replication flows to replicate data from or write data to Apache Kafka hosted on Confluent Cloud if it doesn't necessarily have public endpoints.  
See [Confluent Connections](d83c08ad4eaf49dba9602b1d51c07a52.html#loiod83c08ad4eaf49dba9602b1d51c07a52).  

### 2026-06-03 · Use Object Store Sources in Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.12 · SAP-ID 590833495*

You can now create replication flows from object store sources such as Amazon S3, Microsoft Azure Data Lake Gen2, Google Cloud Storage, SFTP, SAP Hana Data Lake Files, and Microsoft OneLake into local table files.  
See [Cloud Storage Provider Sources for Replication Flows](4d481a2c620f4b52ba65b360299d7719.html).  

### 2026-06-03 · Update Listed Data Product Tables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.12 · SAP-ID 590833515*

Add new columns to the existing tables of a listed SAP Business Data Cloud data product.  
See [Updating Artifacts for Listed Data Products for SAP Business Data Cloud](689aa9d06a494b828ec90b8dc0537c8e.html).  

### 2026-06-03 · Enhanced <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span> Asset Type Support

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.12 · SAP-ID 590833481*

The catalog can now extract metadata for the following SAP Analytics Cloud asset types:  
- Add-in Workbook  
- Analysis Workbook  
- Composite  
- Content Link  
- Dataset  
- Uploaded File  
The metadata extracted includes the asset's name, the dates when it was created and changed, its container name, path, description, and other. The impact and lineage analysis diagram will be available for these objects in a future release.  
See [Connecting to SAP Analytics Cloud Systems](27a4f9620761458dbc60cf9cf853989d.html).  

### 2026-06-03 · Enable System Notifications

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.12 · SAP-ID 590833554*

You can now configure in the user profile settings area if you want to receive system notifications. For example, while creating replication flows or task chains you can configure email notifications to receive a notification when a task is completed.  
See [Changing SAP Datasphere Settings](1084796d09464e78870f32cab8584dfc.html).  

### 2026-06-03 · Direct Delta Sharing Client Integration for Transformation Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.12 · SAP-ID 590834083*

Transformation flows load data without intermediate data copying when accessing delta share tables from BDC Foundation Services or Databricks. You benefit from improved performance and reduced storage overhead by reading data directly from delta sharing sources through the native client instead of copying to a file space.  

### 2026-05-20 · View Performance Summary for Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.11 · SAP-ID 590834085*

You can now view a summary of your replication flow performance, including data volume and transfer speed, to better monitor and understand the performance of your replication flow.  
See [Understanding Replication Flow Metrics](39c116da4e5a41629040f38a907091ce.html).  

### 2026-05-20 · Manage Task Chains with REST APIs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.11 · SAP-ID 590833521*

You can now retry failed task chains or cancel running task chains with REST APIs. You can also use new CLI commands to retry and cancel task chains. For more information,  
See :  
- [Managing Tasks via the REST API](274f2736465c4c48a091c675880502a2.html)  
- [Running and Managing Tasks via the Command Line](2b26a31f197444dea314495bc0008eae.html)  

### 2026-05-20 · Joule Natural Language Capabilities in SAP Datasphere

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.11 · SAP-ID 590834041*

Joule is now integrated with SAP Datasphere, allowing you to use natural language to access system information, manage users and spaces, and navigate data assets and administration tools. This capability simplifies everyday administrative and discovery tasks while respecting existing authorizations, security, and governance settings.  

### 2026-05-20 · Include Transformation Flow Runtime Settings in Transport Packages

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.11 · SAP-ID 590833496*

Data Integrators and Modelers can export runtime settings (including SAP HANA run modes, batch configurations, and Spark application configuration) alongside transformation flows during transport operations, ensuring consistent runtinme behavior across environments.  
See [Change Transformation Flow Settings](f7da0290f5084e3db17c65b3d7c3140a.html).  

### 2026-05-20 · Include Persisted View Runtime Settings in Transport Packages

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.11 · SAP-ID 590833492*

You can export runtime settings for persisted views included run mode and partitions during transport operations. This will ensure consistent runtime behavior across environments.  
See [Change Persisted View Settings](d04f5dd6bbda44448407d54d2a7979be.html).  

### 2026-05-20 · Grant Automated Predictive Library (APL) and Predictive Analysis Library (PAL) Access

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.11 · SAP-ID 590833556*

Using the new With Grant Option property, a database user can now grant access to the APL and PAL libraries to space users who need to run procedures in the Open SQL schema via a task chain.  
See [Create a Database User](798e3fd6707940c3bd2219b2d1ebaac2.html).  

### 2026-05-20 · Enhanced Configuration for Database User Password Policy

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.11 · SAP-ID 590833457*

You can now specify more properties for the database user password policy, including complexity requirements, expiration periods, and reuse restrictions.  
See [Set a Password Policy for Database Users](14aedf6cecce474b93b2d5187662a090.html).  

### 2026-05-20 · Cancel Transformation Flows During Table Export

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.11 · SAP-ID 590834086*

You can cancel transformation flows with Apache Spark runtime while exporting a local table used as a source. Users can now immediately stop running flows without waiting for export completion, improving control over long-running data operations and reducing unnecessary resource consumption.  

### 2026-05-06 · Variables in Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.10 · SAP-ID 590834117*

When you use an analytic model as a source for another analytic model, you can now customize its variables and  
- renamed them  
- change their default value  
- use them in the new analytic model, including restricted measures and calculations.  

### 2026-05-06 · Search for Assets, Terms, and KPIs Using Natural Language

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.10 · SAP-ID 590833482*

Natural language search capability is available for assets, terms, and key performance indicators (KPIs). You can formulate complex search queries conversationally instead of manually setting filters, enabling more intuitive discovery of data products through AI-powered query translation.  
See [Natural Language Search](ea382a4ecb004cdd8edceebb63c7645b.html).  

### 2026-05-06 · SAP ECC and SAP BW Supported as Source Connections for Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.10 · SAP-ID 590834090*

You can now use SAP ECC and SAP BW systems as sources for replication flows and replicate data from these systems using the Operational Data Provisioning framework.  
See:  
- [SAP ECC and SAP BW Sources for Replication Flows](c7accb3184274e1eb25c582bf3235789.html)  
- [SAP BW Connections](e589041e80264f43b6c209c407336376.html)  
- [SAP ECC Connections](e546ccd61af54bf49a0f531a43fe0961.html)  

### 2026-05-06 · Run Replication Flows with Delta Load Objects at Scheduled Times

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.10 · SAP-ID 590834088*

You can now schedule replication flows with delta-enabled objects or use them in task chains by setting Delta Load Run to At Scheduled Time. With this setting, replication flows process available delta records and complete after each run.  
See [Configure the Run Settings of a Replication Flow](3f5ba0c5ae3944c1b7279bb989a2a5b5.html).  

### 2026-05-06 · OData Consumption API: Improved Metadata

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.10 · SAP-ID 590833522*

The following request now provides additional information about the definition of variables and filters:  
GET https://<tenant_url>/api/v1/datasphere/consumption/relational/<space_id>/<asset_id>/$metadata See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2026-05-06 · Enable Checkup Option to Debug Your Data Flow in a Task Chain

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.10 · SAP-ID 590833523*

When running a data flow within a task chain, you can now enable the "Enable Checkup" option to generate a detailed health analysis log. This log provides in-depth analysis to help your support team quickly identify and resolve run errors.  
See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html).  

### 2026-05-06 · Analyze the Lineage Graph of a View with Runtime Metrics

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.10 · SAP-ID 590834116*

The Runtime Metrics tool now allows you to analyze the performance of a view including the underlying source objects in the lineage graph. This allows you to refine your metrics for more precises analysis.  
See [Analyzing View Performance](6e9ba26e11dc4062801c1dfcbf9a1072.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Validation Message Area Available in Local Tables monitor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833500*

In the Local Tables monitor, a new validation message area has been introduced to help you take proactive actions on your local tables—reducing the risk of out-of-memory errors and addressing partitioning issues more effectively. In addition, for SAP Datasphere apps that consume local tables, the validation message area can now display partitioning recommendations tailored to help you optimize performance and maintain system stability.  
[Monitoring Local Tables](374046156e5b47599fc9b96c8c3a4dce.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Update Action Available for Installed Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833484*

Data products installed in an SAP Datasphere space now have an Update action, available when new minor versions are released. This improvement streamlines the workflow, providing a more efficient and intuitive process for users tasked with keeping data products current.  
See [Requesting Access to Data Products](ea7cb802cbea47b39a441888873c3a49.html) and [Data Product Details](71f4d1599dba4aa59e6682a314650cf7.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Too Many Merge Task Failures Will Pause the Task

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833509*

To avoid a system overload with automated merge tasks, a threshold of 10 consecutive failed merge runs for local tables (file) has been implemented: After reaching that threshold, any further run of a merge task for that Local Table (File) will fail immediately (before creating any Apache Spark workload) with the status FAILED (TOO_MANY_FAILURES).  
See [Merge or Optimize Your Local Tables (File)](e533b154ed3e49ce9a03e4421a5296e7.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Task Chains Support All Deletion Types for Both Local Tables and Local Tables (File)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833527*

Additional local table deletion activities for task chains are supported:  
- Delete All Records for both Local Tables and Local Tables (file)  
- Delete Filtered Records for both Local Tables and Local Tables (file)  
See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html)  

### 2026-04-22 · Show Inherited Elements for Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833524*

For analytic models that use another analytic model as source, you can switch on Show Inherited Elements in the properties panel.  

### 2026-04-22 · Mask Sensitive Data with Column Hashing in Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833498*

You can now mask sensitive column data in replication flows from ABAP sources to SAP Signavio targets using the SHA3-256 algorithm. This helps protect sensitive data by replacing original values with hashed strings before they are written to the target.  
See [SAP Signavio Targets for Replication Flows](b8f5e28d34b44d71a52f6265e4fc245f.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Improved Monitoring Tool Navigation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833458*

The Monitoring tool now features an enhanced navigation menu for easier access to key monitoring pages:  
- System and Spaces – Monitor system performance, storage, tasks, and out-of-memory issues across all spaces. Track storage consumption and Apache Spark usage.  
- Capacities Monitoring – Track monthly and daily capacity unit consumption against your subscription and download detailed hourly data.  
- Task Logs – Review task execution history and performance.  
- Expensive Statement Logs – Monitor SQL statements exceeding configured thresholds.  
- Elastic Compute Nodes - Monitor elastic compute nodes key performance figures.  
- Data Integration – Monitor, schedule, and run data replication tasks for remote tables and views. Manage flows and task chains.  
See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Import <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP HANA Cloud</span> Calculation Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833501*

You can import calculation views from SAP HANA Cloud as remote tables while preserving semantic information. This helps you leverage existing SAP HANA Cloud analytical models without recreating them, maintaining their business context and metadata for consistent data modeling across platforms.  
Only calculation views deployed in SAP HANA Cloud version QRC 1/2026 or higher can be imported in SAP Datasphere database version 2025.50 or higher.  
To ensure that your calculation view can be imported, deploy the calculation view in the source system with SAP Business Data Cloud Integration Mode set to Flat View or Construction Kit.  
See:  
- [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP HANA Cloud](b37282c4c0d142e3bca256a46cd40278.html)  
- [SAP HANA Connections](e6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9.html#loioe6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9)  

### 2026-04-22 · Download Spark Driver Logs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590834089*

Use the  Download Spark Driver Logs button for transformation flows on file and local tables (file) to get the Spark driver logs as a .txt file. This is useful to better understand your runs and for debugging purposes. To download this file, you must have the DWC_RUNTIME privilege added to your DW Administrator role or custom role.  
See :  
- [Monitoring Flows](b661ea0766a24c7d839df950330a89fd.html)  
- [Monitoring Local Tables (File)](6b2d0073a8684ee6a59d6f47d00ec895.html)  

### 2026-04-22 · Display Data Protection and Privacy Tags in the Catalog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833483*

The Data Protection and Privacy tag hierarchy is a system-defined tag hierarchy that helps identify data products containing personal data or sensitive personal data. During metadata extraction, the system automatically applies a data protection and privacy tag when it identifies a data product with personal data or sensitive personal data. Note  
The catalog doesn't collect or store personal data or sensitive personal data.  
See:  
- [Using Hierarchical Tags](e5d46e7bcb1e4fb5bbcef852fd35ba87.html)  
- [Data Product Details](71f4d1599dba4aa59e6682a314650cf7.html)  

### 2026-04-22 · Default Retention Period Updated for Both Local Tables and Local Tables (File)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833525*

- Local tables: Default is 0 and values must comprise between 0 and 999 days  
- Local tables (file): Default is 7 and values must comprise between 7 and 999 days  
The default retention period has been updated as follows:See :  
- [Deleting Local Table (File) Records](6ec9b8a89dc64b5cac069cee81399c92.html)  
- [Delete Data From Your Local Tables (File)](872ad509995a451890bf8b80b73ec0e6.html)  
- [Load or Delete Local Table Data](870401f211f94132909bd9f2fafd91b2.html)  
- [Controlling Deletion of Local Table Records](2a59b7142e1b4d478b0bf4063084261e.html)  

### 2026-04-22 · Create Partitions on SAP-Managed Local Tables That Contain Data

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833526*

In the table editor, you can create partitions on existing SAP-Managed local tables stored in a standard space (with SAP HANA Database (Disk and In-Memory) storage) that already contain data.  
See :  
- [Partitioning Local Tables](03191f36e9144b2aaa47b8c9eea039c1.html)  
- [Partitioning Local Tables for Intelligent Content](07d9768ac03343498ae1d1da41f03241.html)  

### 2026-04-22 · Cloud Connector Support for Google BigQuery Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833499*

You can now configure Cloud Connector for Google BigQuery. Cloud Connector acts as a reverse invoke proxy and routes traffic through a TLS tunnel, allowing you to use replication flows to write data to Google BigQuery target systems that don't necessarily have public endpoints.  
See [Google BigQuery Connections](30ed77de13864368bdc596099b37ed70.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Change Default Configurations for Tasks in File Spaces and Create your Own Configurations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590834044*
