Limits to the amount of compute resources that Apache Spark applications can consume when running tasks are set with configurations. In addition to using the provided configurations, which are applied by default to new file spaces, you can now change the default configuration for a task activity in each file space, and create your own configurations.  
Note  
As newly provided default configurations for task activities may allocate fewer resources than those used in previous versions (see [3726059](https://launchpad.support.sap.com/#/notes/3726059)), tasks might run more slowly even though they consume less compute. If these defaults don’t meet your needs, you can change the default configuration for a task activity in each file space.  
See [Set Limits to Compute Resources for File Spaces](0cf1015768e44324b4088da4c5ebd8ca.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Auto-Merge Disabled by Default for Local Table (File) Targets

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590833502*

When creating a replication flow with a Local Table (File) target, the Merge Data Automatically option is now disabled by default. This helps reduce unnecessary merge operations and associated compute workloads. You can still enable the option if automatic merging of new data is required.  
See [Creating a Replication Flow](25e2bd7a70d44ac5b05e844f9e913471.html).  

### 2026-04-22 · Analyze Views with Extended Runtime Metrics

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.9 · SAP-ID 590834118*

The Runtime Metrics tool now allows you the flexibility to set the metrics for the entire main view or review the metrics of just the remote tables in the view. This allows you to refine your metrics for more precises analysis.  
See [Analyzing View Performance](6e9ba26e11dc4062801c1dfcbf9a1072.html).  

### 2026-03-24 · Update Listed Data Product Artifacts

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.7 · SAP-ID 590833516*

Add new tables to the artifacts of a listed SAP Business Data Cloud data product.  
See [Updating Artifacts for Listed Data Products for SAP Business Data Cloud](689aa9d06a494b828ec90b8dc0537c8e.html).  

### 2026-03-24 · Simplified Sharing of Views Containing Currency Conversion

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.7 · SAP-ID 590833530*

You can now simply share SQL views containing a currency conversion column to another space, without needing also to share all the supporting currency conversion entities too.  
See :  
- [Create a Currency Conversion Column in a Graphical View](6e3d8bed7ece4c27ba10e2cc523915fe.html)  
- [Enabling Currency Conversion with TCUR* Tables and Views](b462239ffb644d9baab4442a10a72edf.html)  

### 2026-03-24 · Shift for Currency Conversion

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.7 · SAP-ID 590834119*

In the analytic model, you can now control whether currency conversion applies a shift of the decimal points.  

### 2026-03-24 · Manage Replication Flow Runs via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.7 · SAP-ID 590833503*

You can now run, stop, and obtain the status of replication flows via the command line.  
See [Running and Managing Tasks via the Command Line](2b26a31f197444dea314495bc0008eae.html).  

### 2026-03-24 · Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram Support for BW Data Transfer Process

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.7 · SAP-ID 590833485*

A new link type (dashed line) has been added to the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram for Data Transfer Processes (DTPA) and their associated Transformations (TRFN) across all BW systems. You can gain complete data traceability control by visualizing how one or more transformations are connected to and used within Data Transfer Processes (DTPs), enabling better understanding of data flow and dependencies in your BW environment.  
See [Catalog Asset Details](afccc581146542c485a52563167e23cc.html).  

### 2026-03-24 · Create Large Parquet Files for Object Store Targets

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.7 · SAP-ID 590833504*

You can now enable Create Large Files when replicating data to supported cloud object store targets using the Parquet file format. This creates fewer, larger files during the initial load.  
See [Cloud Storage Provider Targets for Replication Flows](43d93a27150a4a218e3df14e3abdf456.html).  

### 2026-03-24 · Changed Default Task Assignment for File Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.7 · SAP-ID 590834043*

The configurations of Apache Spark applications that are used by default to run certain tasks in file spaces have changed. For more information, see SAP Note  
[3726059](https://launchpad.support.sap.com/#/notes/3726059).See [Create a File Space to Load Data in the Object Store](947444683e524cfd9169d7671b72ba0c.html).  

### 2026-03-10 · Monitor Your Tenant Disk Storage Usage

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.6 · SAP-ID 590834042*

You can now view your SAP Datasphere tenant disk storage capacity and its current utilization in the SAP HANA database via the new Disk Storage Usage card of the System Monitor. This will allow you to protect your tenant by identifying in advance risks of storage-related outages.  
See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2026-03-10 · Monitor Child Tasks of Task Chains in SAP Cloud ALM

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.6 · SAP-ID 590834045*

If your SAP Datasphere tenant is enabled for job & automation monitoring in SAP Cloud ALM, you can now monitor the child tasks of the task chains that are run in SAP Datasphere, from the Job & Automation Monitoring app in SAP Cloud ALM.  
See [Job & Automation Monitoring](https://help.sap.com/docs/cloud-alm/applicationhelp/job-automation-monitoring) in the SAP Cloud ALM - Application Help.  

### 2026-03-10 · Email Notification for Expiring Consent

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.6 · SAP-ID 590833528*

You will automatically receive an email notification before your authorization consent expires. This will help ensure your scheduled tasks will continue without interruption.  
See[Changing SAP Datasphere Settings](1084796d09464e78870f32cab8584dfc.html).  

### 2026-03-10 · Define Z-ORDER COLUMNS While Running an Optimize Task

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.6 · SAP-ID 590833507*

While running an optimize task for your local table (file), you can define Z-order columns to co-locate the data by columns, and thus reduce the amount of data that needs to be read.  
See [Merge or Optimize Your Local Tables (File)](e533b154ed3e49ce9a03e4421a5296e7.html).  

### 2026-03-10 · Application and Visibility of Data Access Controls Applied to Associated Dimensions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.6 · SAP-ID 590833547*

When creating an association from a fact to a dimension, you can now choose to apply the dimension's data access controls directly to the fact data.  
Applying dimension data access controls directly to facts will resolve an issue where users may see null aggregate values for records that they do not have permission to view.  
You can see any data access controls applied in this way in a subsection Applied via Dimensions under the list of Data Access Controls in the side panel.  
See [Apply a Data Access Control to a Graphical or SQL View](8f79fc80d6134a89a03837a205d340cd.html)  

### 2026-02-24 · Visibility of Data Access Controls Applied to Sources

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590834125*

You can now see data access controls applied to the sources of your view in a subsection Applied via Sources under the list of Data Access Controls in the side panel.  
See [Apply a Data Access Control to a Graphical or SQL View](8f79fc80d6134a89a03837a205d340cd.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Using Output Parameters in Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833531*

You can use output parameters in task chain objects. You can also map output parameters to the task chain from the task object. This will allow for more flexible usage of task chains and nested task chains.  
See [.](c6e2e01a9df7454fb23707887470ccf1.html)  

### 2026-02-24 · Share Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833529*

You can now share an analytic model to another space, and even multiple spaces. You can then create a new analytic model on top of that in order to consume this model from other spaces.  
See [Sharing Analytic Models to Other Spaces](8b84bd8dbbbf498f9dff8add4bff6489.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Review and Restore Transformation Flow Versions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833534*

You can now review a list of past versions of transformation flows, and:  
- Open a past version in read-only mode.  
- Download a past version to a CSN/JSON file.  
- Restore a past version to replace the current version.  
See [Reviewing and Restoring Object Versions](4f717cc0d90e4453a072b37f6b043593.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Partitioning Local Tables for Intelligent Applications

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833533*

If your SAP Datasphere is part of an SAP Business Data Cloud formation, you can now create partitions for local tables installed via SAP Business Data Cloud as part of an intelligent application. This enables you to break your data down into chunks, and better manage read-only tables with large volume of data.  
See [Partitioning Local Tables for Intelligent Content](07d9768ac03343498ae1d1da41f03241.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Optimized Data Product Uninstallation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833506*

Uninstalling data products is now faster and all related artifacts including replication flows are removed.  
See [Requesting Access to Data Products](ea7cb802cbea47b39a441888873c3a49.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Installing an Intelligent Application for Two or More Source Systems

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833542*

If your SAP Datasphere is part of an SAP Business Data Cloud formation, you can now install a single intelligent application multiple times for different source systems. Each source system creates (or re-uses) its own ingestion space to contain its data products. Source-specific preparation and application spaces identified by an alias are created for each instance of the intelligent application.  
See [Reviewing Installed Intelligent Content](644648756d334daaaf35d4fc9a0feeda.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Improved Tenant Stability: Critical Storage Threshold

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833557*

To protect your tenant from storage-related outages, SAP Datasphere will now lock all spaces once a critical threshold of disk usage is attained. A message informs you in the Space Management app and in each space page. To continue working with your tenant, you must either increase your tenant disk storage or delete unneeded data.  
See [Unlock a Locked Space](c05b6a6d06db427dbdd3041d61fd5840.html) and [Manage Tenant Disk Storage](27d4c686dc9f49c5bd70d826232ee814.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Improved Primary Key Order Handling in Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833505*

During table replication, the primary key order from the source is preserved in the target to prevent replication failures caused by key order mismatches.  
See [Creating a Replication Flow](25e2bd7a70d44ac5b05e844f9e913471.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Filter on Aggregated Measure Values in OData Consumption API

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590833532*

You can now filter on aggregated measure values when creating your OData requests.  
For example, to filter for where dimension Partner_IDis equal to 100000005 and measure Value is greater than one million, use the syntax:?$filter=Partner_ID eq 100000005 and Value gt 1OOOOOO  
See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2026-02-24 · Change the Primary Key Index Type in Local Tables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.5 · SAP-ID 590834120*

If you have defined multiple primary keys, you can change the index type in the Local Table editor to optimize the performance in very large volume scenarios.  
See [Columns](8f0f40df2ed34035b8b5837897205ee6.html)  

### 2026-02-10 · Users and Roles Page Redesign

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.4 · SAP-ID 590833464*

The Users and Roles pages have been redesigned, and you can now:  
- Create users and roles within dedicated wizards.  
- Filter the list of users and roles in sophisticated ways.  
See :  
- [Managing SAP Datasphere Users](4fb82cb61ca84d8389e9cb18d94dab26.html)  
- [Managing Roles and Privileges](3740dacbc2794f33bb5d8d42216cc3bc.html)  

### 2026-02-10 · Technical User OAuth Clients Support Data Access Controls When Accessing the OData API

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.4 · SAP-ID 590833460*

OAuth clients with a Technical User purpose can now use the OData API to consume data from any exposed asset, including those that are protected by data access controls.  
See [Consuming Data Exposed by SAP Datasphere](d7d56284bb5148c887ac4054689bfbca.html).  

### 2026-02-10 · System Monitor: New Apache Spark Resource Monitoring Columns for Task Logs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.4 · SAP-ID 590833459*

In the System Monitor task logs, new columns for Apache Spark resource monitoring provide insights into memory usage, disk spill, and compute time, helping you to optimize application performance and debug issues efficiently.  
See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2026-02-10 · New API Endpoint

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.4 · SAP-ID 590833517*

The new API endpoint /dsc/providers/activate is available to activate data provider memberships using activation keys.  
For more information, see the API documentation on the [SAP Business Accelerator Hub](https://api.sap.com/api/DataSharingCockpit/overview).  

### 2026-02-10 · Delete Records Waiting in the Inbound Buffer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.4 · SAP-ID 590833510*

You can delete records waiting in the inbound buffer of a local table (file), even if a merge task has not run.  
See [Delete Data From Your Local Tables (File)](872ad509995a451890bf8b80b73ec0e6.html).  

### 2026-02-10 · Command Line Interface Parameters for Setting Space Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces Now Decommissioned

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.4 · SAP-ID 590833558*

After a one-year deprecation period, the following parameters in the .json file containing the space definition are now decommissioned:  
- priority  
- workloadClass.totalStatementMemoryLimit.value  
- workloadClass.totalStatementMemoryLimit.unit  
- workloadClass.totalStatementThreadLimit.value  
- workloadClass.totalStatementThreadLimit.unit  
See [The Space Definition File Format](3fcbf619f2774b849fa7df58163b3609.html).  
To set priorities and statement limits for spaces or groups, you must use the datasphere workload command (see [Managing Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups via the Command Line](9e3537b72e6c445d9f34201df650735b.html)).  

### 2026-02-10 · Catena-X Data Products with External Delivery

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.4 · SAP-ID 590834095*

Create Catena-X data products with external delivery to bring data from the Catena-X network within SAP Datasphere.  
See [Creating Data Products for Catena-X](806c56d8a32b48d5a834107543351c20.html).  

### 2026-02-10 · <span class="ph uicontrol">Initial Only</span> Load Type Now Supported for Local Table Targets When Delta Capture is ON

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.4 · SAP-ID 590833508*

You can now use the Initial Only and Initial and Delta load types for local table (file) targets when Delta Capture is ON in replication flows.  
See [SAP Datasphere Targets for Replication Flows](12c45eb2659f4069b29ef4d69bdd9070.html)  

### 2026-01-27 · Use the <span class="ph uicontrol">Data Pipeline Analyzer</span> to Quickly Identify and Resolve Issues With Your Data Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590833536*

The Data Pipeline Analyzer enables you to monitor complex data models efficiently, allowing you to view and analyze errors, warnings, and data discrepancies in real-time. It quickly identifies data transfer failures, allowing you to take immediate action to resolve them.  
See [Data Pipeline Analyzer](54fd00ddd0b24ccc9ce0fa55297bc13d.html)  

### 2026-01-27 · Use OAuth Clients with Specific Privileges and Permissions to Run Schedules

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590833512*

You can now assign a technical user to schedule data integration tasks. This allows you to minimize the number of users that manage schedules. Additionally, this avoids failed schedules and tasks related to expired consent and authorization.  
See [Modify the Owner of a Schedule](4b660c0395454bd0923f732eef4ee4b2.html).  

### 2026-01-27 · Use Longer Technical Names When Creating Data Builder Objects and Elements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590833545*

The increased maximum length for technical names in Data Builder objects and elements allows for more descriptive and detailed naming conventions.  
The maximum length has changed for the following objects and elements:  
- Views, tables, E/R models: 100  
- Views with semantic usage Analytical Dataset (Deprecated): 60  
- Analytic Models: 60  
- Hierarchies created inside a dimension view: 20  
- Columns: 100  
- Input parameters: 100  
See [Rules for Technical Names](982f9a30d4ab49c8b019cfaf3dc08391.html).  

### 2026-01-27 · Transformation Flow: Get More Guidance for Out-of-Memory Errors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590833535*

When working with transformation flows, out-of-memory errors can be difficult to understand. You can now get more guidance when running a transformation flow directly in the monitoring screen.  
For more information, see [Transformation Flows and Memory Consumption](b266bf41f3e845b68519fca2b399a2c0.html).  

### 2026-01-27 · Prevent Spaces from Consuming All Memory and Ensure Database Resiliency

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590833462*

For resilience purposes, we are now limiting to 80%, the maximum memory resources for workload generated by spaces, user group users and agent users. The remaining 20% of memory resources are reserved to ensure that the database can respond during high memory consumption periods.  
In addition, a single statement cannot use more than 25% of the total memory once the total tenant memory consumption reaches 60% (see [3372747](https://me.sap.com/notes/3372747)).  
See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  

### 2026-01-27 · Install Your Own Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590833486*

Data products that are created using the Data Sharing Cockpit of an SAP Datasphere system can be installed in modeling spaces in that SAP Datasphere system, as well as being shared to other consumers in your SAP Business Data Cloud formation.  
See [Requesting Access to Data Products](ea7cb802cbea47b39a441888873c3a49.html).  

### 2026-01-27 · Create Partitions on Local Tables That Contains Existing Data

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590833544*

You can create partitions on existing local tables stored in a standard space (with SAP HANA Database (Disk and In-Memory) storage) that already contain data.  
See [Partitioning Local Tables](03191f36e9144b2aaa47b8c9eea039c1.html)  

### 2026-01-27 · BW Query Objects (ELEM) from <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP BW</span> 7.5 Included in the Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram for an <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span> Model or Story

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590833487*

When viewing the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram for an SAP Analytics Cloud model or story that uses data from a live data connect, users will be able to see BW Query (ELEM) objects from SAP BW 7.5 in the analyzed object's lineage.  
See [Catalog Asset Details](afccc581146542c485a52563167e23cc.html).  

### 2026-01-27 · Advanced Properties for Cloud Data Integration Connections Using Data Provisioning Agent

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.3 · SAP-ID 590834091*

You can now use the following properties when your Cloud Data Integration connection is enabled for remote tables:  
- Subscription Mode: For high-volume data sources, using the Stateless mode can avoid errors related to a filled persistence data store in SAP HANA Cloud that can occur with the default Stateful mode.  
- Page Size: To increase stability and performance when reading the data, you can increase the maximum size of each page returned for the CDI remote source (default is 10000).  
When you have selected a Data Provisioning Agent to enable remote tables, the properties are available in the Configure Advanced Properties step (when creating connections) or in the Show Advanced Properties section (when editing connection).  
See [Cloud Data Integration Connections](cd33107246f446628f9baff56faf5a1b.html).  

### 2026-01-13 · Transformation Flows in File Spaces - Duplicate Records Removal Feature

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590833539*

The Remove Duplicates operator allows users to efficiently remove duplicate records from their transformation flow in a file space. By sorting records and selecting specific columns for duplicate removal, users can ensure data integrity and streamline their data processing tasks.  
See [Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html).  

### 2026-01-13 · Sharing Local Tables (File) Between File Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590834122*

Local tables (file) can be shared between file spaces (also known as SAP HANA Data Lake Files).  
See [Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html).  

### 2026-01-13 · Search for Data Products Using Natural Language

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590833488*

When searching for data products in the catalog, select the Data Products collection and enter a search string in natural language. The search phrase you enter will be interpreted, and results will be filtered accordingly.  
See [Natural Language Search](ea382a4ecb004cdd8edceebb63c7645b.html).  

### 2026-01-13 · Protection of Permissions Entities

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590833546*

The restriction preventing any permission entity from being itself protected by a data access control or having protected views among its sources is removed. Permissions entities and their sources can now have data access controls attached, though this may impact performance.  
See [Securing Data with Data Access Controls](a032e51c730147c7a1fcac125b4cfe14.html).  

### 2026-01-13 · Natural Language Search - Access to Interpreted Filter Criteria

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590833540*

The Repository Explorer and other apps where natural language search is available now automatically show the interpretation of your search phrase in the AI-Generated Filter Conditions filter bar.  
See [Natural Language Search](04170c64c1004fc58d7f235aea0e4970.html).  

### 2026-01-13 · More Functions for Calculated and Restricted Measures

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590834121*

For calculated and restricted measures,and for calculated and restricted structures, the functions YTD, QTD, MTD and %GrandTotal are now supported.  

### 2026-01-13 · Manage Tasks with REST APIs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590833538*

You can use REST APIs to run task chains and retrieve task logs and existing task log history. You can also use a new CLI command to get flexible and controlled log information. For more information,  
See :  
- [Managing Tasks via the REST API](274f2736465c4c48a091c675880502a2.html)  
- [Running and Managing Tasks via the Command Line](2b26a31f197444dea314495bc0008eae.html)  

### 2026-01-13 · Enable Auto-Retry for a Task in a Task Chain

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590833537*

Allow up to three automatic retries for a task in a task chain. This feature will automatically retry failed tasks to help complete a task in a task chain and improve resilience.  
See, [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html)  

### 2026-01-13 · Ability to Connect <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP BW</span> 7.5 Systems to the Catalog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.2 · SAP-ID 590833489*

You can now connect SAP BW 7.5 systems to the catalog and set up a synchronization schedule so that its objects will appear in the catalog.  
- ADSO (Advanced DataStore Object)  
- ALVL (Aggregation Level)  
- AREA (InfoArea)  
- DTPA (Data Transfer Process)  
- DEST (Open Hub Destination)  
- DSUB (Data Subscription)  
- ELEM (BW Query)  
- FBPA (Open ODS View)  
- HCPR (Composite Provider)  
- IOBJ (InfoObject)  
- LSYS (Source System)  
- RSDS (DataSource)  
- TRCS (InfoSource)  
- TRFN (Transformation)  
Catalog administrators will be able to enrich these object types with term, KPI, and tag relationships and then publish them for easy discovery by catalog users.  
See [Connecting to BW Systems](b350d7c6e88c4f089d381dfe6feee792.html).  

### 2026-01-06 · Suppress Duplicates in Replication Might Fail During Algorithm Migration

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.1 · SAP-ID 590833514*

The algorithm used to generate a unique filename to allow SAP Datasphere to suppress duplicate records in a replication flow is changing. The migration process might affect the suppression mechanism, and you might see duplicate records in your target objects (Cloud Storage Provider Targets).  
See[Cloud Storage Provider Targets for Replication Flows](43d93a27150a4a218e3df14e3abdf456.html) .  

### 2026-01-06 · Reuse a Target Table of a Replication as a Source Table of Another Replication Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.1 · SAP-ID 590833694*

You can reuse a target local table (with or without delta capture enabled) of a replication flow as a source table in another replication flow. It will create a relationship between the two replication flows.  
See [Reusing Objects as Source or Target in Several Replication Flows](d570eee0045a4b9ab5d47ac70140d60a.html).  

### 2026-01-06 · Parquet File Format Now Supported for Cloud Storage Sources for Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.1 · SAP-ID 590833513*

You can now replicate data stored in parquet files when using the following cloud storage providers as the source of your replication flow:  
- Amazon Simple Storage Service  
- Google Cloud Storage  
- Microsoft Azure Data Lake Gen2  
See [Cloud Storage Provider Sources for Replication Flows](4d481a2c620f4b52ba65b360299d7719.html).  

### 2026-01-06 · Hierarchy with Directory: Improved Hierarchy Node IDs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2026.1 · SAP-ID 590833541*

You can now select the new Display Dimension Keys as Node Keys option to display the dimension key values (which are often more informative than the hierarchy node keys), when drilling down into your hierarchy in analytic models and in SAP Analytics Cloud.  
Note  
After enabling this option, you must redeploy any analytic models that consume it in order to see the dimension keys.  
See [Create a Hierarchy with Directory](36c39eee184c485a80ebce9d0fec49ec.html).  

### 2026-01-06 · Generic OData Connections: Select Form Data as OAuth Token Request Content Type And Use Format Query Option

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.1 · SAP-ID 590833511*

Generic OData connections provide new options applying to remote tables:  
- You can select Form Data as OAuth Token Request Content Type to create connections for ODATA service endpoints that expect the OAuth2 token request to be sent using content type multipart/form-data and client credentials to be sent as form-data fields in the request body. Note  
If you select Form Data, data flows are disabled for the connection.  
- You can use the Support Format Query property to allow specifying the format in which data should be returned (using the $format=<format> query option). The property is enabled by default. If you disable the property, the format query option is ignored, and the connection always returns the default response format of the OData service.This property is available in the Configure Advanced Properties step (when creating connections) or in the Show Advanced Properties section (when editing connection). The advanced properties include the already existing option to add custom HTTP headers.  
See [Generic OData Connections](5d36f1aae68a4e59989c424a66d948c9.html).  

### 2026-01-06 · Finding the Replication/Data Flow NAT IP (egress) and the Replication/Data Flow LB IP (ingress) IP Adresses

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.1 · SAP-ID 590833461*

Administrators can find the Replication/Data Flow NAT IP (egress) and the Replication/Data Flow LB IP (ingress) addresses from the side navigation area by choosing: System  About  More.  
See [Obtain SAP Datasphere IP addresses For Allowlisting in Remote Systems](0934f7ed9a534e638299f53ab60866ae.html).  

### 2026-01-06 · Finding the Replication/Data Flow NAT IP (egress) and the Replication/Data Flow LB IP (ingress) IP Adresses

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2026.1 · SAP-ID 590833463*

Administrators can find the Replication/Data Flow NAT IP (egress) and the Replication/Data Flow LB IP (ingress) addresses from the side navigation area by choosing: System  About  More.  
See [Obtain SAP Datasphere IP addresses For Allowlisting in Remote Systems](0934f7ed9a534e638299f53ab60866ae.html).  

### 2026-01-06 · Data Validation: Misplaced Hierarchy Data Nodes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2026.1 · SAP-ID 590833543*

A new validation rule can help resolve the problem of hierarchies with directories which are showing inaccurate data due to data node columns containing values when the node is not a data node.  
Note  
The hierarchy with directory Set as Leaf option is renamed to Set as Data Node.  
See [Validating View Data](ed4063dc7f53436d806ea48f2fd3949a.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Use OAuth Clients with Specific Privileges and Permissions to Manage Modeling Objects via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833592*

You can now use an OAuth client with a Technical User purpose to manage modeling objects using the command line interface. See [Managing Modeling Objects via the Command Line](e3b2cbef9d1f4b38b51c64fe9e88c0aa.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Use OAuth Clients with Specific Privileges and Permissions to Manage Connectivity via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833591*

You can now use an OAuth client with a Technical User purpose to manage TLS server certificates and connections using the command line interface. See [Manage Connectivity via the Command Line](3918242966c74f11858db39c8e5228e4.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Select Task Ports in Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833836*

You can now use task ports to provide enhanced flexibility and error handling in task chains. Task ports allow you the control the flow of task based on success or failure outcomes. Additionally, the new Ignore Error feature allows you to disregard the status of a specific task from the overall task chain status evaluation. This feature is beneficial for maintaining workflow continuity, managing errors effectively, and customizing task execution paths. See [Select Task Ports in a Task Chain](04dcfa7fcf374e8798c9807cfe612c0c.html)  

### 2025-11-26 · Search for Objects by Their Lineage and Impacts Using Natural Language

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833859*

You can now use natural language to search for objects based on the tables, views and analytic models in their lineage and impacts.For example, entering all views using table Sales will return a list of all views that have a table with a name like Sales in their lineage.  
See [Natural Language Search](04170c64c1004fc58d7f235aea0e4970.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · OData API: Obtain Lists of Values for Analytic Model Dimensions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833837*

You can now use the syntax ?$select=<DimensionName> with the consumption/analytical API to obtain lists of unique dimension values for your analytic model. If you specify two or more dimension names then you obtain a list of unique combinations of dimension values.  
See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · New Incremental Aggregation Types for Transformation Flows in a File Space

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590834247*

The aggregation types MIN, MAX, COUNT*, and AVG have been added to the already existing types LAST, SUM, and COUNT. See Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space. See [Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Migrate Catalog Content from a Standalone <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Setup to an Integrated Setup with <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Business Data Cloud</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833650*

Organizations transition to use SAP Business Data Cloud by rewiring their standalone SAP Datasphere and SAP Analytics Cloud systems to SAP Business Data Cloud. Once the SAP Datasphere system is added to an SAP Business Data Cloud formation, the migration of catalog content automatically starts.  
If you had previously rewired your systems before this feature was available, you can still migrate the catalog content from the previous setup to the newer setup. Note  
During the catalog migration, personal data in the form of user favorites will also be migrated.  
See [Migrating Catalog Content to SAP Business Data Cloud](b26705d20f4f4ebba77175276127b48f.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Load type <span class="ph uicontrol">Initial</span> and <span class="ph uicontrol">Delta</span> now available for ODP Data Sources

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833692*

You can now replicate data from ODP data sources with load type Initial+Delta without primary key. See [SAP S/4HANA and Other ABAP Sources for Replication Flows](3f70579c92434f4f88471bba2bd70893.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Export the Definition of the Data Preview

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590834246*

You can export the definition of the data preview of an analytic model as a local file. This enables technical support to recreate a data preview using this exported file for analysis purposes.  

### 2025-11-26 · Disable Delta Capture for Local Tables (File)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590834248*

When creating a local table (file) from the table editor or as a target of your transformation flow, you can disable the delta capture settings. See [Creating a Local Table (File)](d21881b121bc4703861be6ead4aea2ab.html) and [Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Define Row-Level Authorizations Based on IdP User Attributes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833977*

You can now use values in custom application attributes provided by your identity provider as identifiers in your data access controls and define conditions that apply to users with these values. Using attributes in this way allows you to apply a single condition to all users whose accounts contain the specified attribute value. See [Use Identity Provider Attributes as Identifiers in Data Access Controls](40f493f922f24663aa8191ad1e79cb77.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Custom Choropleth Layers

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833840*

You can now import shapefile data to provide custom choropleth layers for use in visualizations of geographical data.  
Note  
SAP Datasphere tenants provisioned up to and including version v2025.23 provide default choropleth shapefile data. Tenants provisioned since then no longer include this data, but you can manually install it.  
See [Enable Choropleth Layers for Geographical Visualizations](4e45d4cb71404f52bd3cb01676f328e1.html).  

### 2025-11-26 · Cancel the Start or Stop Process of an Elastic Compute Node

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.24 · SAP-ID 590833594*

When an elastic compute node is starting or stopping, you can now cancel the process. The data is rolled back and restored to the state that existed before the start or stop process was initially triggered.See [Run an Elastic Compute Node](34b35852f2ff4888bda6b17e08f73ce3.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · Using Shared Local Tables and Shared Remote Tables on a Delta Share Runtime as Sources in a Transformation Flow in a File Space

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833842*

You can use local tables shared from an SAP HANA space and shared remote tables on a Delta Share runtime as sources for a transformation flow in a file space. See [Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · New API Endpoints

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833518*

Two new API endpoints are available:  
- /consumer/products/{productUUID}/install to install a data product as a consumer.  
- /dsc/providers/{providerUUID}/licenses/{licenseUUID}/activationKeys/assignUsers to assign users to a license key.  
For more information, see the API documentation on the [SAP Business Accelerator Hub](https://api.sap.com/api/DataSharingCockpit/overview).  

### 2025-11-12 · Manage Space Database Users via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590834015*

You can now create, update, and delete space database users via the command line. See [Managing Database Users via the Command Line](cd3b314dd33f407f9ba507dd870ba1f1.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · Language Support

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833995*

SAP Datasphere now supports Montenegrin, Macedonian, Kazakh, and Serbian Cyrillic in its menus, buttons, messages, and other elements of the user interface.See [System Requirements and Technical Prerequisites](70ffed417b054b4692458edbd56c90f5.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · Information about Objects for a Data Product's API is Available

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833652*

As users discover data products in the catalog, they can get more information about objects for a data product's API from the data product details page. This additional information will help users better understand a data product before they install it to a SAP Datasphere space or share it to a target system.  
See [Data Product Details](71f4d1599dba4aa59e6682a314650cf7.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · Increase Maximum Parallel Job

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833695*

You can increase the maximum number of parallel jobs up to 16 to run replication flows.See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html) and [Sizing, Capacity Planning, and Load Balancing for Replication Flows](5a3419f3be6e49f794248f20331b255b.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · Define Execution Targets When Using ABAP RFC Streaming With SAP ABAP and SAP S/4HANA Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833693*

When using ABAP RFC streaming with Data Provisioning Agent version 2.7.0.0 or higher, you can now specify a list of hosts for streaming batch jobs.  
Each time a streaming query on an ABAP table is executed, the system assigns the corresponding streaming batch job to a target server chosen randomly from this list.  
If you don't specify a list of execution targets, the source system assigns the target servers.  
See:  
- [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Connections](a49a1e3cc50f4af89711d8306bdd8f26.html)  

### 2025-11-12 · Database Users Immediately Deployed

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590834132*

When you create, edit, or delete a database user for a space, it is now immediately deployed without needing to deploy the space.  
See [Create a Database User](798e3fd6707940c3bd2219b2d1ebaac2.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · Cross-Data Product Associations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833651*

If entities in a data product are associated with entities in one or more other data products, those associations will only be visible if the related data products are installed.  
See [Requesting Access to Data Products](ea7cb802cbea47b39a441888873c3a49.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · Check and Update your Java VM Version - relevant if you use Cloud Connector

*Action: Required · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833555*

A change in certificates has been implemented for SAP Datasphere. You need to update your Java VM version used for Cloud Connector. If your connections involving Cloud Connector fail, check your Java VM version and update it to the new version. For more information about the background and required actions, see SAP Note [3556127](https://me.sap.com/notes/3556127).  

### 2025-11-12 · Cancel a Running Merge Task

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590834179*

From the Local Tables (File) monitor, you can now cancel a merge task that is running with a new Cancel button.See [Merge or Optimize Your Local Tables (File)](e533b154ed3e49ce9a03e4421a5296e7.html).  

### 2025-11-12 · Apache Spark Task Logs Included in Task Log Deletion Node

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.23 · SAP-ID 590833593*

When deleting task logs, you will also delete Apache Spark task logs. Deleting task logs can reduce storage consumption, but the storage level will not go down to zero because some files remain. Running procedures may take longer and incur costs after task log deletion. See [Delete Task Logs to Reduce Storage Consumption](c6902024ecd74956b4ba2d1c67ccb073.html).  

### 2025-10-28 · Simplified Consumption Privileges

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.22 · SAP-ID 590833839*

The Data Warehouse Data Builder (-R------) is no longer required for users who connect to SAP Datasphere to consume data exposed from a space. Now such consumers need only be granted the following scoped privileges for the spaces to which they need access:  
- Data Warehouse Consumption (-R------) - To access data exposed by SAP Datasphere spaces.  
- Space Files (-R------) - To access objects in spaces.  
The DW Consumer role template, for example, grants these privileges, and is intended for users who will only consume data and do not need to connect to SAP Datasphere itself.  
See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2025-10-28 · OAuth Clients with Technical User Purpose to Manage Tasks via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.22 · SAP-ID 590833595*

You can now use OAuth clients with the Technical User purpose to manage tasks via the command line interface based on the roles and spaces you assign to the client. See [Running and Managing Tasks via the Command Line](2b26a31f197444dea314495bc0008eae.html).  

### 2025-10-28 · Impact of Changes in the Source of an Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.22 · SAP-ID 590833838*

When a Data Builder object has been changed, saved, and deployed again, the status of any deployed analytic model that uses that object changes to either Changes to Deploy or Design Time Error, depending on which change has been made.  

### 2025-10-28 · Custom Delta Share Data Products Deletion

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.22 · SAP-ID 590833816*

You can now delete delta share data products.  
See [Creating Data Products for SAP Business Data Cloud](b07e95d07a1e4569b87d9bb57b732bcf.html).  

### 2025-10-28 · Convert an Existing Data Flow Into a Replication Flow and/or a Transformation Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.22 · SAP-ID 590833843*

When your data flow is eligible to be converted into a transformation flow or a replication flow coupled with a transformation flow, a banner will be displayed in the data flow editor. Using a replication flow and/or a transformation flow will allow you to benefit from improved connectivity, access to delta load processing, and enhanced resiliency and scalability.  
See [Use Replication Flows and Transformation Flows to Load and Write Data](34ae0a2ea6e94483b19f632a2843d56d.html)  

### 2025-09-30 · Structures for Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590834249*

In order to create restrictions and calculations across all measures of an analytic model, you can create structures. Structures help you to save time and ensure consistency in your calculations, as you don’t need to create a formula/calculation for each measure. See [Create a Structure for an Analytic Model](de1ed4742dec44088236131e239df8b9.html).  

### 2025-09-30 · Space Selection during Data Product Creation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590833817*

When creating a new data product, the Artifact Space selection is now located at the top of the page.  
See [Creating Marketplace Data Products](bbcbf42b0cb541529e63628d95f394c8.html).  

### 2025-09-30 · Performance Improvements for "Single Values" and "Hierarchy" Data Access Controls

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590833978*

If your "Single Values" or "Hierarchy" data access control was created before this version then you should redeploy it and any objects that consume it to obtain performance improvements.Note  
"Operator and Values" and "Hierarchy with Directory" data access controls are not affected by this change and do not require any action.  
See:[Securing Data with Data Access Controls](a032e51c730147c7a1fcac125b4cfe14.html)  

### 2025-09-30 · Managing User Identity and Authentication with Bundled SAP Cloud Identity Services Tenants

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590833597*

Before this version, you could choose between these methods to authenticate to your SAP Datasphere tenant: the default identity provider or your custom identity provider.You can now choose between the following methods:  
- For existing tenants: the default identity provider, your custom identity provider (legacy) or the new bundled SAP Cloud Identity Services tenants.  
- For new tenants: the default identity provider or the new bundled SAP Cloud Identity Services tenants.  
Using bundled SAP Cloud Identity Services tenants allows users to sign in to your SAP Datasphere tenant and to other SAP products via single sign-on (SSO). If you currently use a custom identity provider, we recommend that you migrate to an SAP Cloud Identity Services tenant and configure the tenant to work with your custom identity provider.  
See [Managing User Identity and Authentication](48b5c8b637a54fa491594272941855b9.html).  

### 2025-09-30 · Improved RFC Performance for Replication Flows (SAP ABAP Connections)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590833696*

You can now enable RFC fast serialization for SAP ABAP connections when replicating data using replication flows. This can help improve replication flow performance, particularly in cases of large data volumes.The Fast Serialization property is available in a new Replication Flow section. For new SAP ABAP connections, the default is On. For existing connections, the default is Off.  
See:  
- [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html)  
- [Prepare Connectivity to SAP ABAP Systems](76c9ac1a318c4de2bea29e72c64be8a0.html)  

### 2025-09-30 · Improved Metadata Extraction Monitoring for Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590833653*

Enhanced monitoring of metadata extraction for data products helps users with the Catalog Administrator role ensure that the content in the catalog is reliable.  
- Metadata extraction errors are detected early and are more visible in the extraction summary logs and on the data product details page.  
- Source systems that have data products or APIs with extraction issues are automatically resynchronized with the catalog to update the data products.  
- Manually synchronizing source systems will also update data products that have errors.  
Catalog users won't be able to install data products that have outdated APIs.  
See [Requesting Access to Data Products](ea7cb802cbea47b39a441888873c3a49.html).  

### 2025-09-30 · Delete Data From Your Local Tables (File)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590833697*

Delete records or versions of a local table (File), creating a direct task or using a schedule, and free up storage by allocating the required amount of compute resources that the file space can consume when processing these tasks.See [Delete Data From Your Local Tables (File)](872ad509995a451890bf8b80b73ec0e6.html).  

### 2025-09-30 · Data Validation: Unique Association Target Values

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590833850*

A new validation rule, can help resolve the problem of dimensions, text entities, or hierarchies that do not provide unique values for identifying records when navigated to via an association.See [Validating View Data](ed4063dc7f53436d806ea48f2fd3949a.html).  

### 2025-09-30 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Integrated into SAP Cloud ALM for Job & Automation Monitoring

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.20 · SAP-ID 590833596*

SAP Datasphere is now integrated into SAP Cloud ALM for job and automation monitoring, which enables you to monitor the tasks run or scheduled in one or more SAP Datasphere tenants from the Job & Automation Monitoring app in SAP Cloud ALM. See [Job & Automation Monitoring](https://help.sap.com/docs/cloud-alm/applicationhelp/job-automation-monitoring) in the SAP Cloud ALM - Application Help.To monitor the tasks in the Job & Automation Monitoring app, you need to enable the feature for your SAP Datasphere tenant as it is disabled by default. See [Configuration](https://help.sap.com/docs/cloud-alm/applicationhelp/configuring-job-monitoring) in the SAP Cloud ALM - Application Help.  
Note  
As the technical names of SAP Datasphere tasks can be displayed in SAP Cloud ALM, we recommend that you do not include any sensitive business or personal data in them. See [Rules for Technical Names](982f9a30d4ab49c8b019cfaf3dc08391.html).  

### 2025-09-16 · Update the Run Settings for Your Replication Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833698*

From the Flows monitor or the replication flow editor, you can change the run settings once you have deployed the replication flow in your tenant. Update the Source and target thread limit, and change the delta load frequency if needed for your use case. See [Configure the Run Settings of a Replication Flow](3f5ba0c5ae3944c1b7279bb989a2a5b5.html) and [Working With Existing Replication Flow Runs](da62e1ee746448e8bc043e1be4377cbe.html)..  

### 2025-09-16 · Transformation Flow on File: User-Defined Data Batching in Python Operator

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833845*

Logically group data into batches based on specific column values in a transformation flow on file's Python Operator. Batches provide greater control over data processing for transformation flow on file and helps users manage data skew and optimize batch selection by offering new metrics like total, maximum, minimum, and average batch sizes in the Run Details. See [Metrics for Transformation Flows](b42fa5b6f4e04a9491efa9bf7dab0929.html).  

### 2025-09-16 · OAuth <span class="ph uicontrol">Technical User</span> Clients for OData Consumption APIs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833846*

You can now define OAuth clients with a Technical User purpose to provide access to the OData consumption APIs based on the roles and scopes you assign directly to the client. See:  
- [Create an OAuth2.0 Client with a Technical User Purpose](88b13468fc3c4ebd972bcb8faa6cafbf.html)  
- [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html)  

### 2025-09-16 · New OData Consumption API URLs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590834250*

The OData consumption API URLs are modified to:GET https://<tenant_url>/api/v1/datasphere/consumption/<request>  
The existing URLs are deprecated and will be removed in a future version.See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2025-09-16 · Mapping Types for Variables in the Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833847*

When you add an analytic model as a source to your new analytic model, you can now change the mapping type to Set Value for variables to a fixed value or interval/ range.  

### 2025-09-16 · Fiscal Time Dimensions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833848*

You can now use the new Fiscal Time dimension type to model your fiscal time periods and the columns that define their starts and ends.See:  
- [Create a Dimension to Categorize Data](5aae0e95361a4a4c964e69c52eada87d.html)  
- [Add a Fiscal Time Dimension](6d1ff7426b6342d7892697407bded26f.html)  

### 2025-09-16 · Display User-Friendly System Name in <span class="ph emphasis emphasis">Impact & Lineage Analysis Diagram</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833654*

When viewing the Impact & Lineage Analysis Diagram for an object, the container that represents the source system will show a user-friendly business or technical name that is extracted from the source system. Containers for systems not connected to the catalog show the system type and the word "unmonitored".  

### 2025-09-16 · Data Subscription (DSUB) Objects Available in the <span class="ph uicontrol">Assets</span> Collection

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833655*

Data subscriptions (DSUB) created from the Data Product Generator for SAP Business Data Cloud are available in the catalog. These objects are created in SAP BW∕4HANA systems. With data subscriptions, SAP BW data is pushed to SAP Datasphere local tables (see [Publishing Data to SAP Datasphere](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW4HANA/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/694a1f45e3174756abc6f06ee7d901d3.html) in the SAP BW∕4HANA help documentation).  
On the data subscriptions details page, you’ll be able to see its subscription properties and open a link to the target SAP Datasphere local table, where you can further analyze the object by viewing the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram. Note  
To be able to see the DSUB objects in the catalog, implement SAP Note [3601550](https://me.sap.com/notes/3601550) into your systems and make sure you're on SAP BW∕4HANA 2023 SPS04 or newer.  
See [Connecting to BW Systems](b350d7c6e88c4f089d381dfe6feee792.html) and [Data Subscription (DSUB)](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW4HANA/ed919380760a44388ab90e6bb3e7480a/5ad3c27c5f8640b39ba26a838cd93c1b.html).  

### 2025-09-16 · Creating Input Parameters in a Transformation Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833844*

Create an input parameter to prompt the user to enter a value for use in filtering or other calculations when the transformation flow is run. This feature is available for transformation flows in SAP HANA runtime and Apache Spark runtime. For more information, see [Creating a Transformation Flow](f7161e6c20204672ac4a6d90c81762e4.html).  

### 2025-09-16 · Creating Input Parameters in Task Chains and Nested Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833841*

You can define input parameters on the task level of task chains. You can also create new input parameters in task chains. This will allow for more flexible usage of task chains and nested task chains.  
See: [Create Input Parameters in Task Chains](c9906ec515f04fbd9589c59339b03c6c.html).  

### 2025-09-16 · Configure Email Notification for Replication Flow Failure at Object Level

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.19 · SAP-ID 590833699*

From the Replication Flow editor or the Flows monitor, you can setup an email notification to stay informed when the replication of an individual object fails in a replication flow.See [Configure Email Notification for Replication Flow Failure at Object Level](5dc4db23d3894b10aca6ade3c666554d.html) and [Working With Existing Replication Flow Runs](da62e1ee746448e8bc043e1be4377cbe.html) .  

### 2025-08-19 · X.509 Client Certificate Support for Microsoft Azure SQL Database Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.17 · SAP-ID 590833701*

You can now use X.509 client certificates for authentication when creating connections to Microsoft Azure SQL Databases in a more secure way. The new authentication type is supported for replication flows. If you want to use it for replication flows and you want to access the same Microsoft Azure SQL Database instance with remote tables or data flows using basic authentication (user name and password), you must create separate connections.  
For new connections, X.509 Client Certificate becomes the default authentication type. For existing connections, you can't change the authentication type from User Name and Password to X.509 Client Certificate.  
Note that we've also removed the Version selection field from the Create Connection creation wizard and the Edit Connection dialog because Microsoft Azure SQL Database has only one version, making the field unnecessary.  
See [Microsoft Azure SQL Database Connections](46343fc1c4544fa9a075d97f84d39826.html).  

### 2025-08-19 · X.509 Client Certificate Authentication Supported for Data Federation with ABAP SQL service

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.17 · SAP-ID 590833705*

For the connection types SAP S/4HANA Cloud and SAP ABAP (Web Socket RFC protocol), you can now use authentication with X.509 Client Certificate for data federation with remote tables via the ABAP SQL service.See:  
- [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA Cloud Connections](a98e5ffdf47c44d9a845dca01a18bd82.html)  

### 2025-08-19 · Use SAP Signavio as Target for Your Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.17 · SAP-ID 590833729*

You can now use connection type SAP Signavio and replication flows to load data from SAP source systems, such as SAP S/4HANA, into SAP Signavio as target system. This allows for efficient and scalable data replication to SAP Signavio.See:  
- [SAP Signavio Targets for Replication Flows](b8f5e28d34b44d71a52f6265e4fc245f.html)  
- [SAP Signavio Connections](4c367de075a44ad7b7a6db576a4a9c82.html)  

### 2025-08-19 · Use Microsoft OneLake as a Source for Your Replication Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.17 · SAP-ID 590833700*

You can now use Microsoft OneLake and the corresponding connection type to add sources to replication flows and replicate data in CSV format from the source. See:  
- [Cloud Storage Provider Sources for Replication Flows](4d481a2c620f4b52ba65b360299d7719.html)  
- [Microsoft OneLake Connections](057fa4b51c734e679dd70eec9514839d.html)  

### 2025-08-19 · Identify and Generate Semantic Information for Facts, Dimensions, and Text Entities

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.17 · SAP-ID 590833855*

You can now request SAP Datasphere to suggest a Semantic Usage for your entity, identify measures and attributes, and apply appropriate semantic types to them.See [Generate Semantic Information](2fc1d26ebff748e4905d724247d33531.html).  

### 2025-08-19 · Delta Share Data Products Available in the <span class="ph uicontrol">Data Products</span> Collection

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.17 · SAP-ID 590833657*

Data products created in the data sharing cockpit with Delta Sharing output port are available in the Data Products collection. This enhancement unifies how catalog users search for and access data products in the catalog.  
Delta Share data products are created by data providers that have a profile with the Marketplace Visibility option set to Formations (see [Creating Data Products for SAP Business Data Cloud](b07e95d07a1e4569b87d9bb57b732bcf.html)).  
SAP Datasphere systems can now be a source system for both assets and data products. To help users find systems on the Metadata Extractions, the source systems are organized into groups based on the objects extracted: assets only, APIs only, and both assets and APIs (see [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html)).  

### 2025-08-19 · Automatic Synchronization after Application Upgrade

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.17 · SAP-ID 590833656*

After an application upgrade, source systems with detected changes are automatically synchronized with the catalog. Changes may include correcting metadata inaccuracies or resolving errors and bugs within data products. These updates to data products and assets do not update the object’s version number.  

### 2025-08-06 · Transport Master Data in Local Table Dimensions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.16 · SAP-ID 590833851*

If your SAP Datasphere tenant is part of an SAP Business Data Cloud you can now use the Allow Data Transport switch to authorize the transport of data along with your local table dimensions.See:  
- [Creating a Local Table](2509fe4d86aa472b9858164b55b38077.html)  
- [Create a Dimension to Categorize Data](5aae0e95361a4a4c964e69c52eada87d.html)  
- [Creating Packages to Export](24aba84ceeb3416881736f70f02e3a0a.html)  

### 2025-08-06 · OAuth Clients with Specific Privileges and Permissions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.16 · SAP-ID 590833603*

You can now create an OAuth client with the new Technical User purpose and assign a global or scoped role to limit its privileges and permissions.Initially, the use of an OAuth client with a Technical User purpose is limited to the following workflows:  
- To use the SCIM 2.0 API (see [Managing Users via the SCIM 2.0 API](1ca8c4a9467f43df9ae6d4ed3734f05a.html)).  
- To transport content (see [Transporting Content Through SAP Cloud Transport Management](05383980f0704c71ab9872360ce45622.html) and [api/v1/content](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_ANALYTICS_CLOUD/14cac91febef464dbb1efce20e3f1613/eeace13cc37b4eed96064a7c5d8ee493.html) in the SAP Analytics Cloud REST API Guide).  
- To export a log of user activities to a CSV file (see [api/v1/audit/activities/exportActivities](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_ANALYTICS_CLOUD/14cac91febef464dbb1efce20e3f1613/ca45363ac1de4d669ad4a18115401d5a.html) in the SAP Analytics Cloud REST API Guide).  
- To use the datasphere command line interface (marketplace commands only) (see [Accessing SAP Datasphere via the Command Line](3f9a42ccde6b4b6aba121e2aab79c36d.html)).  
- To allow SAP Signavio to connect securely to SAP systems such as SAP S/4HANA (see [SAP Signavio Connections](4c367de075a44ad7b7a6db576a4a9c82.html).)  
See [Create an OAuth2.0 Client with a Technical User Purpose](88b13468fc3c4ebd972bcb8faa6cafbf.html).  

### 2025-08-06 · Improved Monitor Features for Persisted Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.16 · SAP-ID 590833702*

The View Monitor in the Data Integration Monitor now provides quicker access to views that have been recently run and persisted views. There are also additional task log details to help make more informed decisions and improvements.See [Persisting and Monitoring Views](9af04c990f294fd28c00f46763dd8b0d.html).  

### 2025-08-06 · Define Batches on Transformation Flows when Using SAP HANA Runtime

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.16 · SAP-ID 590833703*

Break large transformation flow datasets into batches to avoid Out-Of-Memory (OOM) errors when using SAP HANA runtime. This feature enhances performance and reliability, ensuring efficient data integration and processing.  
See [Monitoring Flows](b661ea0766a24c7d839df950330a89fd.html).  

### 2025-08-06 · Column Lineage for Transformation Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.16 · SAP-ID 590833849*

The transformation flow's Impact and Lineage graph allows you to visualize column lineage for target and source nodes. See [Creating a Transformation Flow](f7161e6c20204672ac4a6d90c81762e4.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · X.509 Client Certificate Support for SAP HANA Cloud, Data Lake Relational Engine Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833704*

You can now use X.509 client certificates or certificate chains for authentication when creating connections to SAP HANA Cloud, Data Lake Relational Engine in a more secure way. For new connections, X.509 Client Certificate becomes the default authentication type.See [SAP HANA Cloud, Data Lake Relational Engine Connections](40763e2e3e33440db0c37f6bcbe650f0.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Usability Improvements to Enhance Your Catalog Experience

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833660*

The following usability improvements will help users with the appropriate permissions manage assets, terms, and key performance indicators (KPIs) and monitor source systems.  
- Updated pages for creating and managing glossary terms and KPIs  
- Addition of visual cues for areas where AI-generated content is used for enriching content  
- Updated avatar and colors for all objects in the grid view on the catalog search page  
- Updated source property groupings for metadata on the asset details page  
- Renamed the main monitoring page to Metadata Extractions to match the side navigation  
- Renamed the systems table on the Metadata Extractions page to Remote Systems, plus these other updates:  
- Reorganized the information so that it's easier to find and understand  
- Moved system-specific actions from the toolbar to the system's row (for example, Configure is available only for SAP BW systems)  
- Updated the system's details page by adding a toolbar to the header section, which means users can perform actions on the system without having to go back to the main monitoring page (for example, manually synchronizing a system) and reorganizing information in the header section so that it's easier to find and understand  
See the following topics:  
- [Using Key Performance Indicators](3550f14d4cd74dfaa63ebcaa25b288c2.html)  
- [Create and Manage Key Performance Indicators](ca4f04da3afa4f82b85a0835fceb444b.html)  
- [Define the Key Performance Indicator Template](b3ead0d4944c40ddbdb1ebae239cc69f.html)
