- [Create and Manage a Glossary](e40310c9781848bfad031cce7792f394.html)  
- [Create and Manage Glossary Terms](193336a4eba94c978c08fd2cee625a3e.html)  
- [Catalog Asset Details](afccc581146542c485a52563167e23cc.html)  
- [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html)  

### 2025-07-22 · Transport Objects Between File Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833708*

You can export objects from and import objects to file spaces.See [Transporting Content Between Tenants](df12666cf98e41248ef2251c564b0166.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Transformation Flow in File Space - Incremental Aggregations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833854*

Handle incremental data loads and maintain aggregated results efficiently with incremental aggregations. See [Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Running API Tasks in a Task Chain

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590834182*

SAP Datasphere now provides the ability to run tasks in a task chain that use a REST-based API to access external systems.See [Run API Tasks in a Task Chain](9a8489ed7443436197fbd8b8ffba61ab.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Obtain OData Service Route for an Asset

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833857*

You can now obtain the correctly formed OData service route for any asset directly from the Generate OData Request dialog in the asset editor.See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · New Notification Task Object in Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590834181*

SAP Datasphere now allows users to set up additional email notification within a task chain, in addition to the notification specified for completion of the entire task chain. Users drag the new Notification Task object to any point in the sequence of tasks run in a task chain and then specify the subject and message for this new email notification. (These new notifications use the same recipient list as the email notification maintained at the task chain level.)See [Configure Email Notification for Task Chains and Notification Tasks](7ff6a4e584a345a88d002c18c1fc321e.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · New Layout Option in Task Chain Toolbar

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833706*

Options in the task chain editor toolbar have been rearranged and a new Layout option was added. The new Layout option allows you to rearrange the visual display of a task chain on the canvas, from top-to-bottom (the default), or left-to-right.See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Monitor the Size and File Count for Data Waiting in the Inbound Buffer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833707*

In the Local Tables (File) Monitor, two new columns help you monitor the size and file count information for data waiting in the inbound buffer. Thanks to these columns, you can track whether your automated merge has failed or if the merge needs to be run manually.See [Monitoring Local Tables (File)](6b2d0073a8684ee6a59d6f47d00ec895.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Improved Share Status Monitoring for Data Products Shared to Target Systems

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833661*

The share status for data products will be monitored for changes on the target system every 24 hours and updated in the catalog as needed:  
- If the data product share is deleted from the target system, the data product's share status is updated to Shareable.  
- If the data product's current share status is In progress for longer than one hour, but the data product was not shared to the target system, the share status is updated to Shareable.  
- If the data product's current share status is In progress for longer than one hour, and the data product has been shared to the target system, the share status is updated to Shared.  

### 2025-07-22 · Import Terms and Key Performance Indicators

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833658*

If you have terms or key performance indicators in other applications, you can import them into the catalog via a Microsoft Excel spreadsheet. See these topics:  
- [Import Glossary Terms](694ecb26f72e4d0ca8b8f64434da881f.html)  
- [View Details About Glossary Term Import](9395b3a445e44ceabf3e0e7ed3bba373.html)  
- [Import Key Performance Indicators](adc41e274a344172a7ebecf8350e6d3a.html)  
- [View Details About Key Performance Indicator Import](07a5c28b138741f0a86d12f99579932f.html)  

### 2025-07-22 · Exception Aggregation Enhancements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833852*

You can now define measures with exception aggregation based on time showing proper values even if there are no booked values with exception aggregation types FIRST OF DIMENSION, AVERAGE OF DIMENSION, and LAST OF DIMENSION.  

### 2025-07-22 · Enable Multi-Availability Zones for SAP HANA Cloud

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833598*

Use the Enable SAP HANA Multi-Availability Zones option on the Tenant Configuration page to create a synchronous replica of the main system on a system in a different availability zone in the same region. The synchronous replica system has a high-availability setup that can take over processing when necessary. For more information, see [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html) and [Creating and Configuring Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](2f80b57359254b5cbb4faac794ade90c.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Derived Values for Variables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833853*

Derived values are now also available for filter variables and restricted measure variables. See [Derived Variables](82f40f73bbbf42a283648fa312a5aec2.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Data Validation: Missing Hierarchy Nodes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833858*

A new validation rule, run from a fact view, can help resolve the problem of records in the fact that are not assigned to any node in an associated hierarchy with directory (and which consequently cause inaccuracies in your aggregations and visualizations). It can:  
- Identifies hierarchy nodes that are missing for records in the fact.  
- Generate entities that allocate these records to a new Unassigned node and make this node available for display in your aggregations and visualizations.  
See [Validating View Data](ed4063dc7f53436d806ea48f2fd3949a.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Connection Type Generic HTTP

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590834180*

With the new connection type, you can connect to external systems and add tasks that are exposed by REST-based APIs to task chains. For this purpose, we have introduced the new API Tasks connection feature, which is supported only for Generic HTTP connections.See [Generic HTTP Connections](b79b865e64794c7abcd12a54f2f73c8c.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · Additional Filter Options to Delete Records for Data Type Date or Datetime

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590834183*

If you are filtering on a column that has values of data type Date or Datetime, you can refine your filter with additional options.See [Controlling Deletion of Local Table Records](2a59b7142e1b4d478b0bf4063084261e.html).  

### 2025-07-22 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Models Included in the Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram for an <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span> Insight

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.15 · SAP-ID 590833659*

When viewing the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram for an SAP Analytics Cloud insight, users will be able to see models from the local SAP Datasphere system in the insight's lineage.Note  
For insights saved prior to this update, follow these steps to ensure that the lineage appears as expected.  
- In SAP Analytics Cloud, open the insight and save it.  
- Wait a few minutes as the catalog extracts the object's metadata and updates the associated asset.  
- On the catalog search page, search for the insight and view its details page.  
- Select Open Lineage and Impact to see the diagram.  
See [Catalog Asset Details](afccc581146542c485a52563167e23cc.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · Terminology Updates in Data Integration Monitor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833715*

We have harmonized the terminology in the Data Integration Monitor for a consistent user experience among the different monitors. See [Managing and Monitoring Data Integration](4cbf7c7fc64645bfa364332827557267.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · Self-Service Enabling of SAP HANA for SQL data warehousing on Your <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Tenant

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833600*

Administrators can now create the mapping between their SAP Datasphere tenant and their SAP Business Technology Platform account to enable SAP HANA for SQL data warehousing themselves, rather than needing to raise a ticket.  
For more information, see [Enable SAP HANA for SQL Data Warehousing on Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](e9a287849ccf41bb8a132d12dd3fdc8f.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · Secure File Transfer Protocol (SFTP) as Source for Your Replication Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833712*

You can now replicate data from a file storage using a secure file transfer protocol (SFTP) connection for secure and reliable file transfers to a supported target. See:  
- [Secure File Transfer Protocol (SFTP) Sources for Replication Flows](a832ef4cee9d4d3aa1210869743b6173.html)  
- [Generic SFTP Connections](b645de78a8374c24871ab6169be40d35.html)  

### 2025-07-08 · SQL View Editor Improvements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833856*

Syntax errors in SQL sources are marked with line numbers in the message pane and/or highlighting on the erroneous line in the editor.  

### 2025-07-08 · Override Default Apache Spark Application Settings in Table Editor and Task Chain for Local Tables (File)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833861*

When you perform data deletion for your local table (file) either from the Table Editor or the Task Chain editor, you can override the default Apache Spark Application Settings that were defined at space creation by your administrator.See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html) and [Deleting Local Table (File) Records](6ec9b8a89dc64b5cac069cee81399c92.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · Monitor the Storage Consumption for SAP HANA Data Lake Files (Apache Spark)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833599*

New monitoring abilities are available for storage consumption in file spaces and usage of Apache Spark application with the Object Store tab. This allows you to understand where storage consumption is the highest in file spaces (or SAP HANA Data Lake Files) and usage of Apache Spark application when running tasks. For more information, see [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html)  

### 2025-07-08 · Manage Workload by Group

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590834016*

You can now set space priorities and statement limits by group, such as data integration, data modeling, or data consumption, which will ensure a consistent workload distribution for these processes across all spaces and reduce administrative effort. See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · Delta Only Load Type in Replication Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833709*

A new load type, Delta Only is available to run your replication flows: If your business case does not require an initial load of data, you can skip it with this new load type to replicate only delta changes.See [Creating a Replication Flow](25e2bd7a70d44ac5b05e844f9e913471.html), [Configure the Run Settings of a Replication Flow](3f5ba0c5ae3944c1b7279bb989a2a5b5.html) and [Modify Replication Flow Object Schemas and Settings](a24c71f3ba7548909534d4cb52cefbfc.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · Connection Type <span class="ph uicontrol">Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen1</span> Removed from <span class="ph uicontrol">Connections</span> App

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590834184*

Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen1 was retired on February 29, 2024. The corresponding connection type was deprecated with SAP Datasphere version 2023.24. We have now removed the option to create or edit connections for connection type Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen1 from the Connections app:  
- The connection type tile has been removed from the connection creation wizard.  
- Existing connections are still visible in the connections list but cannot be edited anymore.  
Action: Recreate your existing Gen1 connections with the Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen2 connection type, adapt your models and processes to the new connection, and delete your Gen1 connections.  

### 2025-07-08 · Catena-X Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590834238*

You can create a private offering of data products for the Catena-X network.  
See [Creating Data Products for Catena-X](806c56d8a32b48d5a834107543351c20.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · BW Query Objects (ELEM) from <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP BW∕4HANA</span> Included in the Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram for an <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span> Story

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833662*

When viewing the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram for an SAP Analytics Cloud story that is based on a live data model, users will be able to see BW Query (ELEM) objects from SAP BW∕4HANA in the story's lineage.  
See [Catalog Asset Details](afccc581146542c485a52563167e23cc.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · Additional SAP BW Bridge Storage Sizes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590834148*

You can choose from more SAP BW bridge storage sizes. See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · Ability to Connect <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP BW∕4HANA</span> Systems to the Catalog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833664*

You can now connect SAP BW∕4HANA systems to the catalog and the following objects will be included in the catalog:  
- ADSO (Advanced DataStore Object)  
- ALVL (Aggregation Level)  
- AREA (InfoArea)  
- Data Transfer Process (DTPA)  
- DEST (Open Hub Destination)  
- ELEM (BW Query)  
- FBPA (Open ODS View)  
- HCPR (Composite Provider)  
- IOBJ (InfoObject)  
- LSYS (Source System)  
- RSDS (DataSource)  
- TRCS (InfoSource)  
- TRFN (Transformation)  
Catalog administrators will be able to enrich these object types with term, KPI, and tag relationships and then publish them for easy discovery by catalog users.  
See [Connecting to BW Systems](b350d7c6e88c4f089d381dfe6feee792.html).  

### 2025-07-08 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Tables Included in the Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram for a Data Product

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833663*

When viewing the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram for a data product that is from an SAP Datasphere source system, users will be able to see tables from the local SAP Datasphere system in the data product's lineage.  

### 2025-07-08 · "Hierarchy with Directory" Data Access Controls

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.14 · SAP-ID 590833979*

You can now create data access controls in which criteria are defined as hierarchy values in a hierarchy with directory. Each user can only see the records that match the hierarchy values she is authorized for in the permissions entity, along with any of their descendants. See [Create a "Hierarchy with Directory" Data Access Control](44ae6289f96949f48781959f9d284578.html).  

### 2025-06-12 · Search for Objects by Their Contents Using Natural Language

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590833865*

You can now use natural language to search for tables, views, analytic models and Business Builder For example, entering Tables with a measure called sales id will return a list of all tables containing such a measure.  
See [Natural Language Search](04170c64c1004fc58d7f235aea0e4970.html).  

### 2025-06-12 · SQL and Script Views as Source Objects for Your Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590833713*

You can create a replication flow with an SQL view or a script view as a source. However, you must fulfill some conditionsSee [SQL and Script View Source Objects for Replication Flows](a215f8f8257d4cf88a9ef927b8b101c5.html).  

### 2025-06-12 · Python Version 3.11 Available for Your Data Flow Script Operators

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590834251*

When creating a data flow or modifying an existing one, you can now choose the new Python Version 3.11. Note that Python 3.9 will be deprecated soon.See [Create a Script in a Data Flow](f3e2570966ac4036b552ebd998274af1.html).  

### 2025-06-12 · Object Store Requests License

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590833601*

You can now specify the number of API requests separately when configuring object storage.Previously, API calls were bundled under the general Storage option. With the new Requests option, you can define the expected number of API calls per month, giving you more control and accuracy in estimating your object storage needs. See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2025-06-12 · OData <samp class="ph codeph">$batch</samp> Syntax

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590833862*

You can now make calls to the OData consumption APIs with the $batch syntax, which is used by SAP Build and SAP Fiori Elements Apps as well as other data consumers.Note  
SAP Datasphere restricts the contents of the body of a $batch request to a single GET request. Additional requests are not supported.  
See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html)  

### 2025-06-12 · New Data Types Supported for Local Tables (File)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590833863*

You can now select additional data types for your local table (file): DateTime, hana.BINARY and hana.TINYINT.See [Data Types Supported By Local Tables (File)](2f39104e5bd847919b8daee1580c4f68.html)  

### 2025-06-12 · Improved RFC Performance for Replication Flows (SAP S/4HANA Cloud Connections)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590833710*

You can now enable RFC fast serialization for SAP S/4HANA Cloud connections when replicating data using replication flows. This can help improve replication flow performance, particularly in cases of large data volumes.For new SAP S/4HANA Cloud connections, the default of the Fast Serialization property is On. For existing connections, the default is Off.  
See:  
- [Prepare Connectivity to SAP S/4HANA Cloud](abb159e027184c98a54fc1b2a88dd3f5.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA Cloud Connections](a98e5ffdf47c44d9a845dca01a18bd82.html)  

### 2025-06-12 · Enhanced Persistence for Views with Data Access Control

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590833711*

Persisted views with Data Access Control now re-attach persistence upon deployment in newer versions of SAP Datasphere and HANA. For views with Data Access Controls with structure "Single Values" that were set to persisted before SAP Datasphere version 2025.10 and SAP HANA version 2025.2.8, the persistence is removed when the view is deployed. You will need to persist the view again.  
This feature ensures data continuity, eliminating the need to manually re-persist views after deployment.  
See [Persisted Views and Data Access Control](7a4a983611cc4efb9415e6f3db310eaa.html).  

### 2025-06-12 · Drag and Drop Feature for Transformation Flow on File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.12 · SAP-ID 590833860*

[Creating a Transformation Flow in a File Space](b917baf0431343bea8381fa37e12eeb8.html).  

### 2025-05-29 · Use a Single Source Object in Several Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590833718*

One source object can be reused in several replication flows.See [Add the Source for a Replication Flow](749638060e8746e48b0f9bf0e50c6d0c.html).  

### 2025-05-29 · Select Appropriate Data Access Method for Installed Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590833666*

When installing a data product to your space, you can choose the appropriate data access method. See [Requesting Access to Data Products](ea7cb802cbea47b39a441888873c3a49.html).  

### 2025-05-29 · Secure File Transfer Protocol (SFTP) as Targets for Your Replication Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590833717*

You can now create replication flows to replicate data from supported source systems into Secure File Transfer Protocol (SFTP). See:  
- [Secure File Transfer Protocol (SFTP) for Replication Flows](5a14eb1709a24a35821d1618285d021d.html)  
- [Generic SFTP Connections](b645de78a8374c24871ab6169be40d35.html)  

### 2025-05-29 · Options for Filter Variables in the Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590833864*

For variables with filter type Range in analytic models, you can now define several value ranges.  
For variables with filter type Multiple Single Values, you can now define default values with multiple rows.  
See: [Create a Filter Variable](8683b49e60c643f4947da11b5aa31585.html)  

### 2025-05-29 · Monitor Consumption Unit Capacity

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590834146*

You can view the amount of capacity units consumed in a month using the new Capacities Dashboard. Access the dashboard from System Monitor   Capacities. See [Monitoring Capacity Unit Consumption](ba3d05baac854171914c09d64bed7202.html).  

### 2025-05-29 · Manage Additional Connection Types via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590833716*

You can now use the datasphere command line interface to create connections or update connection details (including credentials) for the following additional connection types: Amazon Athena, Amazon Redshift, Hadoop Distributed File System, Microsoft Azure Blob Storage, Microsoft Azure SQL Database, SAP BW∕4HANA Model Transfer, SAP HANA Cloud, data lake Files, and SAP HANA Cloud, data lake Relational Engine.  
See [Manage Connectivity via the Command Line](3918242966c74f11858db39c8e5228e4.html).  

### 2025-05-29 · Joule Available for Conversational Interactions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590834147*

Joule can provide information and automate certain business processes through a chat. This functionality is available for customers who sign up for a restricted release. To learn more about signing up, see [Become an Early Adopter for Joule in SAP Datasphere](https://community.sap.com/t5/technology-blog-posts-by-sap/become-an-early-adopter-for-joule-in-sap-datasphere/ba-p/14096474).  

### 2025-05-29 · HDI Container and Open SQL Schema Tables Replicated in Elastic Compute Nodes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590833602*

You can now enable tables from HDI containers and Open SQL Schemas to be replicated to elastic compute nodes.See [Create an Elastic Compute Node](99ad61e1b63a44de8572a49c6f374e8a.html), [Allow the Space to Access the Open SQL Schema](7eaa370fe4624dea9f182ee9c9ab645f.html) and [Prepare Your HDI Project for Exchanging Data with Your Space](a94e1637db484a5c8ec2da83cfa75156.html).  

### 2025-05-29 · Enhanced Task Scheduling with 5-minute Increments

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590834186*

The new scheduling feature allows tasks to be set at 5-minute intervals, offering greater flexibility and precision in task management.See [Schedule a Data Integration Task (Simple Schedule)](7c11059ed3314e1fb753736b7867512c.html) .  

### 2025-05-29 · Enhanced Apache Spark Area for File Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590834310*

In file space pages (for spaces with a storage type SAP HANA Data Lake Files), the Workload Management area has been renamed to Apache Spark and has been reorganized. The area now displays additional tasks and the applications that are used to run them.See [Create a File Space to Load Data in the Object Store](947444683e524cfd9169d7671b72ba0c.html).  

### 2025-05-29 · Ability to Share Data Products Only in <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Business Data Cloud</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.11 · SAP-ID 590833665*

The ability to share data products to target systems is no longer available when users access the catalog from SAP Datasphere. Sharing data products to target systems will only be available when users access the catalog from SAP Business Data Cloud.  
See [Sharing Data Products to Target Systems](https://help.sap.com/docs/business-data-cloud/governing-and-publishing-data-in-catalog/sharing-data-products-to-sap-databricks).  

### 2025-05-13 · View Persistence Link to Out of Memory Documentation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590834185*

In the event of an out-of-memory error, a link to Help Portal documentation will be provided in the task log messages. The enhanced documentation provides additional information about out-of-memory events.See [Persisted Views and Memory Consumption](e3d04951a4a344c28b25b2b1b13bf3d8.html) .  

### 2025-05-13 · Partition Your Local Table Data With Hash Partitioning

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590833866*

From the Data Builder, when creating your local table, you can define partitions based on one or several columns of your table to break your data down into smaller and more manageable parts. You can now choose between range partitioning or hash partitioning.See [Partitioning Local Tables](03191f36e9144b2aaa47b8c9eea039c1.html)  

### 2025-05-13 · Manage Data Products via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590833818*

You can use the command line interface to install data products for consumers.  
See [Managing Data Marketplace Data Products via the Command Line](34e88f7889734b1691fab7d8715c17fa.html).  

### 2025-05-13 · Improvements for <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span><span class="ph pname">, SAP BW bridge</span> Systems

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590833668*

To ensure the extracted metadata for all SAP Datasphere, SAP BW bridge objects is up-to-date, you can create a synchronization schedule for the source system. See [Connecting to BW Systems](b350d7c6e88c4f089d381dfe6feee792.html).  
In the catalog, users will be able to discover Data Transfer Process (DTPA) objects.  

### 2025-05-13 · Impact of Installed Data Products on <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Objects Included in the Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590833667*

When reviewing the details page for a data product, users can open the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram to see how the installed data product impacts SAP Datasphere objects.  
See [Data Product Details](71f4d1599dba4aa59e6682a314650cf7.html).  

### 2025-05-13 · Enhanced Cross-Space Analysis when Defining Partition

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590833714*

Analysis in View Analyzer now considers all entities from other spaces in partition adviser.See [Creating Partitions for Your Persisted Views](9b1b595054c34da5b67ac07595093f82.html) .  

### 2025-05-13 · Data Preview and Data Management for Local Tables (File) Updates

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590834253*

Working with large data files can require many resources and can lead to performance issues. To save resources and prevent performance issues, some changes have been made on table editor for local tables (file) data management:  
- Previewing data: Data preview is possible, and is now read using Apache Spark resources.  
- Find and replace: You can't add a filter on your Find and Replace activity : Replacement will happen in the whole dataset.  
- Default sorting and filtering is no longer available and is disabled.  

### 2025-05-13 · Create Data Products for <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Databricks</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590833819*

As a data provider using a SAP Business Data Cloud tenant, you can create dedicated data products to bring data coming from the Data Product Generator in the BW Systems into SAP Databricks.  
See [Creating Data Products for SAP Business Data Cloud](b07e95d07a1e4569b87d9bb57b732bcf.html).  

### 2025-05-13 · Confluent Kafka as Source: Expand Array or Map, Configure Opcode and Change Primary Key

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590834188*

From the replication flow editor, you can change the schema properties of an individual object that has Confluent Kafka as source.See [Confluent Kafka Sources for Replication Flows](4f2d0a8f23384efdab6891b29093786a.html).  

### 2025-05-13 · Additional Monitoring Information for Local Tables (file)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.10 · SAP-ID 590834187*

In the Local Tables (File) monitor, you can now display additional information to monitor your local tables (file): Number of Active Records, Active Records File Storage (MiB), Total Table File Storage (MiB) and Previous Versions File Storage (MiB) See [Monitoring Local Tables (File)](6b2d0073a8684ee6a59d6f47d00ec895.html).  

### 2025-04-30 · Share Data Products to <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Databricks</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.9 · SAP-ID 590834174*

Users with the appropriate privilege can share data products to SAP Databricks, where users can query and enrich the data products using machine learning and generative AI tools. Also, the share status for data products are updated so users can easily see which data products have been shared.  
See [Requesting Access to Data Products](ea7cb802cbea47b39a441888873c3a49.html) and [Sharing Data Products to Target Systems](https://help.sap.com/docs/business-data-cloud/governing-and-publishing-data-in-catalog/sharing-data-products-to-sap-databricks).  

### 2025-04-30 · Replace The Fact Source of an Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.9 · SAP-ID 590834257*

You can replace the fact source of an existing analytic model by dragging the new fact source onto the fact source of the analytic model on the canvas. See: [Replace the Fact Source of an Analytic Model](ccf070353186428eb940ee6715c87a6a.html)  

### 2025-04-30 · Remove Data Product from an <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Space

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.9 · SAP-ID 590834175*

When a data product is no longer needed in an SAP Datasphere space, users can uninstall it from that space.  
See [Requesting Access to Data Products](ea7cb802cbea47b39a441888873c3a49.html) and [Data Product Details](71f4d1599dba4aa59e6682a314650cf7.html).  

### 2025-04-30 · Improved Experience when Working with Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.9 · SAP-ID 590833669*

When searching for data products in the catalog, the following collections have been renamed:  
- The SAP Business Data Cloud Data Products collection is now Data Products  
- The Marketplace Data Products collection is now Data Products (Marketplace)  

### 2025-04-30 · Impact and Lineage Analysis for Measures

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.9 · SAP-ID 590833868*

You can display an impact and lineage analysis for measures in the analytic model.  

### 2025-04-30 · Format of Measures

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.9 · SAP-ID 590833867*

You can now change the format of a measure (decimal places and scale) in an analytic model in the Formatting section in the editor.  

### 2025-04-30 · Analyze the Performance of a View with the New <span class="ph uicontrol">Runtime Metrics</span> Tool

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.9 · SAP-ID 590834252*

Execute two database queries on a view directly in the Data Builder to get important runtime indicators to analyze performance. Additionally, you can now generate and download an Explain Plan for a select statement on the view.  

### 2025-04-15 · Use Connection Type <span class="ph uicontrol">SAP ABAP</span> for Data Federation With Remote Tables Using the ABAP SQL Service in <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP S/4HANA</span> or <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP S/4HANA Cloud</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.8 · SAP-ID 590833719*

You can now use the ABAP SQL service for SQL-level remote access to data from published, ABAP-managed CDS view entities in SAP S/4HANA (on-premise) and SAP S/4HANA with connection type SAP ABAP. To facilitate this way of accessing data, we've added a new section called Remote Tables in the connection properties of the SAP ABAP connection type. In this section, you can choose between different data provisioning options. For the RFC protocol, the existing Data Provisioning Agent selection moved from the Features section to the Remote Tables section. To use the ABAP SQL Service for federated access to CDS view entities, you select data provisioning option Cloud Connector (for RFC protocol) or Direct (for Websocket RFC protocol).  
Using the ABAP SQL service is supported for basic authentication with user name and password. For on-premise systems, the SAP Logon connection type must be application server and Cloud Connector must be used.  
See:  
- [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html)  
- [Using ABAP SQL Services for Accessing Data from SAP S/4HANA](4d7474595a5b41bb986616262ff44a3a.html)  
- [Using ABAP SQL Services for Accessing Data from SAP S/4HANA Cloud or SAP BTP ABAP Environment](ef2b2238154f4cd78a08df360447c1d5.html)  

### 2025-04-15 · Integrate SAP BW data into <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.8 · SAP-ID 590833720*

As an administrator in SAP BW or SAP BW∕4HANA, you can now push data into SAP Datasphere as local tables (file). These tables are stored in the object store and can be used for your business case.See [Integrating Data from SAP BW and SAP BW∕4HANA with the Data Product Generator for SAP Business Data Cloud](cca4744c85b14788babe7cb6b77c9973.html).  

### 2025-04-15 · Data Validation: Referential Integrity

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.8 · SAP-ID 590833869*

A new validation rule helps you ensure that all values in foreign key columns in fact views are present in the key columns of the associated dimensions. See [Validating View Data](ed4063dc7f53436d806ea48f2fd3949a.html).  

### 2025-04-15 · Data Access Control Deployment Status and Date

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.8 · SAP-ID 590833980*

The Deployment Status and Deployed On fields are now available in the data access control editor.See [Securing Data with Data Access Controls](a032e51c730147c7a1fcac125b4cfe14.html).  

### 2025-04-15 · Analytic Model as Fact Source

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.8 · SAP-ID 590834254*

You can create a new analytic model using another analytic model as fact source and re-use the modelled definitions for measures, exposed attributes, dimensions, and variables.See [Add a Fact to an Analytic Model](27075eeeba634f55be0b52e92cf88159.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Transformation Flow on File: Support for Python Standard Libraries

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590833871*

A selection of Python standard libraries can be used in a transformation flow on file python operator. See [Python Operator Reference](950d55834dee450c9f71786d8b14a634.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · System Monitoring: Tracing Expensive Statements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590833604*

Expensive statements are now automatically traced for new tenants or tenants that had never enabled this option. You can analyze individual SQL queries whose execution exceeds one or more thresholds with the default settings or set your own. See [Configure Monitoring](9cd0691c44a74f2aa47b52f615f74433.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Retry of Nested Task Chains within a Parent Task Chain

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590834189*

For task chains that include nested task chains, the behavior for retrying failed tasks within a nested chain has been improved. When a task chain reports a failure, and the failure occurred running one of the tasks inside a nested task chain, retry of the parent task chain no longer requires rerunning all the tasks in the nested task chain, but only needs to rerun tasks from the point where an individual task reported a failure.See [Monitoring Task Chains](4142201ec1aa49faad89a688a2f1852c.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Optimize and Merge Your Local Tables (Files) Data

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590833726*

From the Local Tables (File) monitor you can now optimize and merge your data using a direct task run or via a schedule. You can also update the settings to run these tasks using the necessary workload that applies to your case.See [Monitoring Local Tables (File)](6b2d0073a8684ee6a59d6f47d00ec895.html) and [Merge or Optimize Your Local Tables (File)](e533b154ed3e49ce9a03e4421a5296e7.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Microsoft SQL Server as Source for Your Replication Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590834191*

You can now use connection type Microsoft SQL Server to replicate data from Microsoft SQL Server databases using replication flows.See:  
- [Creating a Replication Flow](25e2bd7a70d44ac5b05e844f9e913471.html)  
- [Microsoft SQL Server Connections](a13c8abb328f45be891599c9cc76fb91.html)  

### 2025-04-04 · Manage Additional Connection Types via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590833721*

You can now use the datasphere command line interface to create connections or update connection details (including credentials) for the following additional connection types: Amazon Simple Storage Service, Apache Kafka, Cloud Data Integration, Confluent, Generic JDBC, Generic OData, Generic SFTP, Google BigQuery, Google Cloud Storage, Microsoft Azure Data Lake Store Gen2, Microsoft SQL Server, Oracle, SAP ABAP, SAP BW, SAP ECC, SAP HANA, SAP SuccessFactors, SAP S/4HANA Cloud, and SAP S/4HANA On-Premise  
See [Manage Connectivity via the Command Line](3918242966c74f11858db39c8e5228e4.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Generate an Explain Plan for a Transformation Flow Run

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590833724*

As part of a simulated run, you can now use the Explain Plan to understand how the selected transformation flow run will be executed and get details about its performance. It is a quick way to obtain an overview of your run without saving any changes to your run.  
See [Explore Transformation Flows](7588192bf4cd4e3db43704239ba4d366.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Data Validation: Support for Remote Sources

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590833870*

You can now run data validations on views with remote sources (where this is supported by the connection adapter and the rule). You are warned before starting the validation, and have the option to cancel validation at any time.See [Validating View Data](ed4063dc7f53436d806ea48f2fd3949a.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Change the Settings used to Run a Transformation Flow in a File Space

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590834192*

You can update the required amount of compute resources that the file space can consume when running a specific translation flow.  
See [Override the Default Settings to Run Your Transformation Flow (in a File Space)](e5c4ac8ab3bf4573b86cd4f4f3118c16.html) .  

### 2025-04-04 · Change the Setting Used to Run a Task Chain in a File Space

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590834255*

While creating a task chain, you can update the required amount of compute resources that the file space can consume when running a specific object.See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html)  

### 2025-04-04 · Cancelling Options for Transformation Flow Runs on File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590833723*

You can cancel a transformation flow run on file with or without rollback:  
- Cancel with rollback allows the data to be restored to the version it had when the run started.  
- Cancelling without rollback stops the run where it is and what has already been processed will be kept.  
See [Cancel a Transformation Flow Run](ab885f05210f4a52aebe8306c8cad083.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Automate a Merge Activity While Creating a Replication Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590833872*

When creating a replication flow, you can select the new option Merge Data Automatically: It will start an automatic merge activity to update your target local table (file) with new data. See [Creating a Replication Flow](25e2bd7a70d44ac5b05e844f9e913471.html).  

### 2025-04-04 · Acquire and Prepare Data in the Object Store

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.7 · SAP-ID 590834190*

You can now load large quantities of data via replication flows and store them inexpensively in file spaces in the object store. You can prepare the data using transformation flows and then share data to a standard space to be used as a source of flows, views, and analytic models.See [Acquiring and Preparing Data in the Object Store](2a6bc3f6d79b4c39a01b6d58d043fbaf.html) and [Create a File Space to Load Data in the Object Store](947444683e524cfd9169d7671b72ba0c.html).  

### 2025-03-18 · Use OAuth 2.0 Authentication for Secure Access to <span class="ph uicontrol">SAP ABAP</span> and <span class="ph uicontrol">SAP S/4HANA</span> connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.6 · SAP-ID 590833722*

You can now use OAuth 2.0 authentication with client credentials for SAP ABAP (on-premise) and SAP S/4HANA connections. The OAuth 2.0 authentication type is available when the following conditions are met:  
- You use Cloud Connector to enable the SAP ABAP or SAP S/4HANA connection for replication flows and data flows.  
- For SAP ABAP connections: You use the RFC protocol to connect to an on-premise system.  
Remote tables (and model import for SAP S/4HANA connections) are not supported with OAuth 2.0 authentication. When changing an existing connection from basic authentication to OAuth 2.0 authentication, remote tables will be disabled.  
See :  
- [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Connections](a49a1e3cc50f4af89711d8306bdd8f26.html)  

### 2025-03-18 · Schedule Tasks to Run in Preferred Region/Location Time Zone with Daylight Saving Adjustments

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.6 · SAP-ID 590833725*

You can now schedule tasks in your preferred region/location time zone. Schedules will automatically adjust to reflect daylight saving time. This ensures accurate timing for task execution to syncronize with all shared global operations.See [Schedule a Data Integration Task (Simple Schedule)](7c11059ed3314e1fb753736b7867512c.html) and [Schedule a Data Integration Task (with Cron Expression)](169ba34e19744362be25a4325de8d10c.html).  

### 2025-03-18 · Restore Past Versions of Data Access Controls

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.6 · SAP-ID 590834256*

Support for restoring past versions of objects is now extended to include data access controls.See [Reviewing and Restoring Object Versions](4f717cc0d90e4453a072b37f6b043593.html).  

### 2025-03-18 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Integrated into SAP Cloud ALM for Security Settings

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.6 · SAP-ID 590833981*

Certain SAP Datasphere security settings are now integrated into SAP Cloud ALM.See [SAP Datasphere Security Recommendations](4b14c62a62eb4c83bb74e33f5a646aa5.html), [Configuration & Security Analysis](https://help.sap.com/docs/cloud-alm/applicationhelp/configuration-security-analysis) in the SAP Cloud ALM - Application Help, and [Configuration & Security Analysis – Content](https://support.sap.com/en/alm/sap-cloud-alm/operations/expert-portal/configuration-security-analysis/csa-content.html/) on the SAP Cloud ALM for Operations Expert Portal.  

### 2025-03-04 · Unify the Side Navigation for Catalog and Data Marketplace

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.5 · SAP-ID 590833820*

Access to features and functionality for the catalog and data marketplace have been unified under a single access point in the side navigation. Users can select  (Catalog & Marketplace) to access the catalog:  
- Search for and evaluate data and assets in the catalog (see [Discovering Data Products and Assets in the Catalog](6df42e3675324d73bd35c2e70d46a7ee.html))  
- Manage their marketplace data products installations, activate licenses, and choose contexts they want to be a member of (see [Managing Marketplace Data Products](5d725beb848e432a91eb8de29875d7e2.html))  
- Connect and manage source systems and monitor metadata extraction (see [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html))  

### 2025-03-04 · SAP BW Bridge Process Chains in a Task Chain

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.5 · SAP-ID 590834194*

You can now include and run SAP BW bridge process chains in a task chain. To create and run SAP BW bridge process chains in a task chain, you must first have created the SAP BW bridge space and configured the SAP BW Bridge Cockpit. You use the cockpit to create and update process chains.See [Run SAP BW Bridge Process Chains in a Task Chain](7d7d02a81bfe401ab85ce4d6c527a536.html).  

### 2025-03-04 · Review and Restore Object Versions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.5 · SAP-ID 590833886*

You can now review a list of past versions of objects, and:  
- Open a past version in read-only mode.  
- Download a past version to a CSN/JSON file.  
- Restore a past version to replace the current version (views, analytic models, and local tables only).  
See [Reviewing and Restoring Object Versions](4f717cc0d90e4453a072b37f6b043593.html).  

### 2025-03-04 · New Look and Feel : Horizon Theme

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.5 · SAP-ID 590833996*

SAP Datasphere has adopted the SAP standard Horizon theme, which offers a modern and fresh look with a new vibrant color palette.  
Icons and user interface elements may have changed to adopt this modern design but functionalities remain the same.  

### 2025-03-04 · GrandTotal for Calculated Measures

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.5 · SAP-ID 590833873*

In the analytic model, the function GrandTotal is now available for expressions for calculated measures.  

### 2025-03-04 · Delete Local Table (File) Data From Previous Versions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.5 · SAP-ID 590833875*

Increase the performance and storage efficiency of Local Tables (File) by deleting obsolete versions of data that have already been processed by flows. See [Deleting Local Table (File) Records](6ec9b8a89dc64b5cac069cee81399c92.html).  

### 2025-03-04 · Combining Data Access Controls

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.5 · SAP-ID 590833874*

In the analytic model, you can now combine static (hierarchies and single values) and dynamic filter based (operator and values) Data Access Controls.  

### 2025-02-19 · Unify the Side Navigation for Catalog and Data Marketplace

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.4 · SAP-ID 590833670*

Access to features and functionality for the catalog and data marketplace have been unified under a single access point in the side navigation. Users can select  (Catalog & Marketplace) to access the catalog:  
- Search for and evaluate data and assets in the catalog (see [Discovering Data Products and Assets in the Catalog](6df42e3675324d73bd35c2e70d46a7ee.html))  
- Manage their marketplace data products installations, activate licenses, and choose contexts they want to be a member of (see [Managing Marketplace Data Products](5d725beb848e432a91eb8de29875d7e2.html))  
- Connect and manage source systems and monitor metadata extraction (see [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html))  

### 2025-02-19 · Semantic Type Annotations in OData

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.4 · SAP-ID 590833876*

Annotations indicating the semantic types of columns are now provided when you retrieve the metadata for an asset.See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2025-02-19 · New Content Type to Load Data From an ABAP-Based Source System

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.4 · SAP-ID 590833728*

When creating a new replication flow to load data from an ABAP-based source system, you can choose between Template Type or Native Type. The content type can be modify later on if needed but with some restrictions to consider.See [Creating a Replication Flow](25e2bd7a70d44ac5b05e844f9e913471.html) and [Modify Replication Flow Object Schemas and Settings](a24c71f3ba7548909534d4cb52cefbfc.html).  

### 2025-02-19 · Data Persistence - View Monitoring Improvements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.4 · SAP-ID 590834193*

Refinements have been made in the view partitioning and View Analyzer run details and task logs for improved usability. See [Analyze Results](9ad9b8b42c82414c9cd166c55752c9a8.html) .  

### 2025-02-19 · <span class="ph uicontrol">View Analyzer</span> Interprets Local Tables (File).

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.4 · SAP-ID 590833727*

The View Analyzer identifies local tables (file) consumed directly or inderectly by views. Local tables (file) are evaluated when calculating data persistence candidate scores for views. See [Data Persistence Guidance](85f7be45761f4ae5b7e49819ce4b4d2e.html).  

### 2025-02-04 · View data in a SQL View Transform

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.3 · SAP-ID 590834258*

View the data in objects used in deployed SQL view transforms with the Data Preview panel. See [Create an SQL View in a Transformation Flow](775e0ab758e54c58af5a240294d234f2.html).  

### 2025-02-04 · Natural Language Search

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.3 · SAP-ID 590833882*

You can now enter your Repository Explorer or Data Builder search string in natural language and SAP Datasphere will interpret your phrase and filter your results appropriately. See [Natural Language Search](04170c64c1004fc58d7f235aea0e4970.html).  

### 2025-02-04 · Import or Export IP Allowlist

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.3 · SAP-ID 590833605*

Manage your IP allowlist by importing or exporting your allowlist to the same or a different SAP Datasphere tenant. See [Manage IP Allowlist](a3c214514ef94e899459f68f4c1e2a23.html).  

### 2025-02-04 · Impact of Changes in the Source of an Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.3 · SAP-ID 590833877*

After a dimension has been saved or deployed after performing changes to a hierarchy, the status of any deployed analytic model that use that dimension changed to either Changes to Deploy or Design Time Error, depending on which change has been made.  

### 2025-02-04 · Delete Records From Your Delta Capture Local Table

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.3 · SAP-ID 590833881*

From the table editor, some enhancements have been made to help you with housekeeping on already processed data changes. For local tables where delta capture is enabled, you can now delete records as follows:  
- Delete all records: All records containted in your table will be physically deleted.  
- Delete all records (Mark as "Deleted)= All records contained in your table will take the change type "D" but can still be processed by other apps until they are permanently deleted.  
- Delete all records marked for deletion which are older than the specified number of days: Based on the defined number of days, all fully-processed records with change type "D" will be permanently deleted.  
See [Load or Delete Local Table Data](870401f211f94132909bd9f2fafd91b2.html)  

### 2025-02-04 · Delete Audit Logs Generated for Database Analysis Users to Free Up Disk Storage

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.3 · SAP-ID 590833606*

Audit logs can consume a large quantity of your tenant's disk storage. Until now, audit log entries for database analysis users were kept for 180 days, after which they were automatically deleted. Starting with this version, you can manually delete these audit logs to free up disk storage at any time.Also, database analysis users are now automatically deactivated when their audit logs consume more than a certain threshold of disk storage. Deleting their audit logs will automatically reactivate them.  

### 2025-02-04 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Integrated into SAP Cloud ALM for Health Monitoring

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.3 · SAP-ID 590833612*

SAP Datasphere is now integrated into SAP Cloud ALM for health monitoring, which enables you to check the health of one or more SAP Datasphere tenants from the Health Monitoring app in SAP Cloud ALM.See [Health Monitoring](https://help.sap.com/docs/cloud-alm/applicationhelp/health-monitoring) in the SAP Cloud ALM - Application Help.  

### 2025-02-04 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Business Data Cloud</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.3 · SAP-ID 590833997*

SAP Business Data Cloud is a fully managed SaaS solution that unifies and governs all SAP data and seamlessly connects with third-party data—giving line-of-business leaders context to make even more impactful decisions.  
If your SAP Datasphere tenant is included in an SAP Business Data Cloud formation:  
- Other formation tenants are automatically connected to the SAP Datasphere catalog.  
- Users with an SAP Business Data Cloud administrator role working in the SAP Business Data Cloud Cockpit can:  
- Install pre-packaged intelligent applications to the SAP Datasphere and SAP Analytics Cloud tenants in the formation.  
- Activate data packages that make SAP Business Data Cloud data products available to install from the SAP Datasphere catalog.  
- Users with an SAP Datasphere catalog administrator role can share SAP Business Data Cloud data products to connected systems.  
- Users with an SAP Datasphere modeler role can install SAP Business Data Cloud data products to their spaces.  
See [Working with SAP Datasphere in SAP Business Data Cloud](8f9c3725cfe84e08b3e951e7af06ce57.html).  

### 2025-01-21 · Use SAP Business AI features to Enrich Catalog Assets

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.2 · SAP-ID 590833671*

When enriching catalog assets on the asset details page, use the SAP Business AI features for adding custom summary and custom long descriptions and assigning tag relationships.  
See  
- [Governing Catalog Assets](1218c12e72c34cfd96293e566badb60c.html)  
- [Enriching and Managing Catalog Assets](7ed60a094f2a464da6a8d75e5bfed9d5.html)  
- [Manage Hierarchies and Tags](0c167fa12afc47eeac9f018fe975b73f.html)  
- [Manage Tag Relationships for Assets](bb608dd2a4dc402889351c4303a173a4.html)  

### 2025-01-21 · System Monitor: Cancelling Task Runs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.2 · SAP-ID 590833607*

You can now cancel task runs with the Cancel Task button. Cancelling a task run may be required when it takes too long or if the run impacts negatively other runs by taking too many resources away. See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2025-01-21 · Skip target columns that don't exist in the source

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.2 · SAP-ID 590833732*

If you are using an existing table as the target for your replication flow, this table may contain columns that don't exist in the corresponding source object. Previously, you'd have to remove each of these columns manually in the table editor before you could start you replication flow. Now you can choose to ignore these columns during the replication.See [Add the Target for a Replication Flow](ab490fb4d083442197e2ba3b92079734.html).  

### 2025-01-21 · Seamless Planning

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.2 · SAP-ID 590833880*

SAP Analytics Cloud users can save their planning and analytic model data directly in SAP Datasphere spaces. The SAP Analytics Cloud users can choose for each object, whether to expose it to SAP Datasphere modelers for use as sources for views, flows, and analytic models.See [Store and Consume SAP Analytics Cloud Data in SAP Datasphere](fe958749e31e4130874713cb722cd255.html).  

### 2025-01-21 · New Flow Analysis Possibilities for Transformation Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.2 · SAP-ID 590834195*

Two new options are available in the transformation flow monitor via the Data Integration Monitor:  
- Simulate a run that doesn't save changes in the target table by clicking Simulate Run. Simulating allows you to test a transformation flow and see if you get the desired outcome. Based on the result, you can decide to deploy the flow, resolve errors, or to optimize the flow to improve performances.  
- Download a PLV file of a visual map of the operators and their relationships and hierarchies by clicking Generate a SQL Analyzer Plan File. The plan file contains detailed information about your data model that you can download for further analysis. Analyzing this file allows you to resolve errors and enhance the transformation flow performances.  
See [Explore Transformation Flows](7588192bf4cd4e3db43704239ba4d366.html)  

### 2025-01-21 · Dynamic Default Values for Variables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.2 · SAP-ID 590833879*

You can define dynamic default values for variables in analytic models. The value can then be proposed dynamically in the analytic preview and in the SAP Analytics Cloud variable prompt.See [Create a Standard Variable](8a2978ed34de4d0d975ebdd1bbf96ac4.html) and [Create a Reference Date Variable](a2d060ee364440dab656557ba42c20b4.html).  

### 2025-01-21 · Data Access Controls for Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.2 · SAP-ID 590833878*

You can now apply Data Access Controls to the analytic model to protect it directly without dependency on its fact source authorizations.See [Apply a Data Access Control to an Analytic Model](8d8e2f9b36a74de3b6f5f1384ad8f70d.html).  

### 2025-01-21 · Ability to Open the Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram from Marketplace Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.2 · SAP-ID 590833672*

When reviewing the details page for a marketplace data product, users can open the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram. This enhancement allows data providers to easily access the diagram, where they can gain a better understanding of a data product.  
See [Marketplace Data Product Details](f59e912bf1ee45cf90375fd86109b952.html).  

### 2025-01-07 · Use the ABAP SQL Service in SAP S/4HANA for Data Federation With Remote Tables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.1 · SAP-ID 590833730*

You can now use the ABAP SQL Service for SQL-level remote access to data from published, ABAP-managed CDS view entities in SAP S/4HANA. Instead of accessing ABAP-managed database objects like tables or CDS views via the SQL layer of the SAP S/4HANA database, you can use the SQL service exposure to access SAP S/4HANA directly, taking advantage of ABAP-level security concepts and full ABAP SQL semantics. Using the SQL service, you can expose CDS view entities to SAP Datasphere by creating a service definition and binding and a corresponding role for privileged access. To facilitate this new way of accessing SAP S/4HANA data, we've added a new section called Remote Tables in the connection properties of the SAP S/4HANA connection type. In this section, you can choose between different data provisioning options, including the existing Data Provisioning Agent selection which moved from the Features section to the Remote Tables section. To use the ABAP SQL Service for federated access to CDS view entities, you select data provisioning option Cloud Connector.  
See:  
- [Using ABAP SQL Services for Accessing Data from SAP S/4HANA](4d7474595a5b41bb986616262ff44a3a.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Connections](a49a1e3cc50f4af89711d8306bdd8f26.html)  

### 2025-01-07 · Technical Names for Dimensions and Attributes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2025.1 · SAP-ID 590833883*

You can now can enter technical names for dimensions and duplicated attributes in the editor of the analytic model. The label Dimension Alias has been renamed to Business Name. For more information, see [Add a Dimension to an Analytic Model](4caf0987e7c7460e878fb574f04bd6a4.html).  

### 2025-01-07 · Task Chain Cancelation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.1 · SAP-ID 590833731*

Task chains may now be canceled after a run has started, by selecting the Cancel Run link from the Run Details display for a specific task chain. If you click this link, SAP Datasphere will initiate the cancel operation and immediately remove all child tasks not already run. For any child task that has already started, the effect of the cancel operation depends on the individual object. For example, replication flows and Open SQL procedures cannot effectively be canceled, so successful completion of the task chain cancelation will wait until those child tasks are completed.See [Monitoring Task Chains](4142201ec1aa49faad89a688a2f1852c.html).  

### 2025-01-07 · Python script for Transformation Flow in a File Space

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.1 · SAP-ID 590834259*

You can insert a Python operator to transform incoming data with a Python script and output structured data to the next operator with the new Python operator. See [.](a747acf9d96b450da4b802b80e05a896.html)  

### 2025-01-07 · New DW AI Consumer Role

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.1 · SAP-ID 590833614*

The new global role DW AI Consumer enables the users assigned to it to use SAP Business AI features, when activated in your SAP Datasphere tenant on the new Configuration  AI Services tab.See [Standard Roles Delivered with SAP Datasphere](a50a51d80d5746c9b805a2aacbb7e4ee.html) and [Enable SAP Business AI for SAP Datasphere](1b3fe45f38df4db1a9cda97a5a7bcdaf.html).  

### 2025-01-07 · Improved RFC Performance for Replication Flows (SAP S/4HANA Connections)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.1 · SAP-ID 590833734*

You can now enable RFC fast serialization for SAP S/4HANA on-premise connections when replicating data using replication flows. This can help improve replication flow performance, particularly in cases of large data volumes.For new SAP S/4HANA connections, the default of the Fast Serialization property is On. For existing connections, the default is Off.  
See:  
- [Prepare Connectivity to SAP S/4HANA On-Premise](8de01dd25c1e443e8e2de7d2fbe1364d.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Connections](a49a1e3cc50f4af89711d8306bdd8f26.html)  

### 2025-01-07 · Hierarchy as Directory with Compound Keys

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.1 · SAP-ID 590833884*

Previously, hierarchies could only be identified in the hierarchy directory by a single key column, which was specified in the Hierarchy Name Column property. This property is now renamed to Hierarchy Directory Association. It must contain the association pointing to the hierarchy directory and supports associations with compound keys. Existing hierarchies will be migrated automatically to the new format.  
See [Create a Hierarchy with Directory](36c39eee184c485a80ebce9d0fec49ec.html).  

### 2025-01-07 · Changing License Scope

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2025.1 · SAP-ID 590834239*

You can define the scope of a license by enabling scope changes, and specifying which data product content users can access.  
See [Changing License Scope](7890c5b332504a4b954c981b5f871e49.html).  

### 2024-12-03 · No New Features

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.25 · SAP-ID 590834303*

This version contains no new generally available features.  

### 2024-11-19 · Upcoming change of certificates – relevant if you use SAP Cloud Connector

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.24 · SAP-ID 590833998*

A change of certificates is going to be rolled out with an upcoming release of SAP Datasphere. Once this change has been implemented, if you’re using an older version of Java VM with SAP Cloud Connector, connections involving SAP Cloud Connector will fail because the new certificates are not trusted. We recommend that you update your Java VM to the latest version as soon as possible to avoid issues with the new certificates.  
For more information, see SAP Notes [3369433](https://me.sap.com/notes/3369433) and [3508452](https://me.sap.com/notes/3508452).  

### 2024-11-19 · Run <span class="ph uicontrol">View Analyzer</span> for Views with Input Parameters

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.24 · SAP-ID 590834196*

For views with one input parameter with default value, you can execute View Analyzer with memory consumption. For views with one or more parameters with default value, you can generate Explain Plan or SQL analyzer plan file.See [Getting Started with View Analyzer](e0aeddba00b14be29b5e49b47001d43b.html) .  

### 2024-11-19 · OData Language Parameter

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.24 · SAP-ID 590833885*

You can now specify the language in which to return data from analytic models via the OData API. See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2024-11-19 · New Navigation and Filtering Options in the System Monitor
