*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.24 · SAP-ID 590834149*

Access to task and statement logs is made easier with the creation of Task Logs and Statement Logs tabs.Find task and statement logs faster with the new quick filtering options added to the existing advanced filtering option.  
See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2024-11-19 · More Capacity Units Details

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.24 · SAP-ID 590834150*

The Capacity Units section in the Tenant Configuration tab has additional information including purchased unites, estimate, and available capacity units as well as the amount of capacity units used this month. See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2024-11-19 · Manage Connections via the Command Line or via REST API

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.24 · SAP-ID 590833735*

You can now list, validate, and delete connections and read connection details, and you can create and edit SAP SuccssFactors connections via the command line or via the REST API. See:  
- [Managing Connections via the Command Line](8eb811898d1049fbb426339e44a2eb70.html)

### 2024-11-19 · Change delta interval without impacting running replication flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.24 · SAP-ID 590834197*

You can now change the delta load interval for a replication flow in status Active without stopping the replication flow first.  
See [Modify Replication Flow Object Schemas and Settings](a24c71f3ba7548909534d4cb52cefbfc.html) and [Working With Existing Replication Flow Runs](da62e1ee746448e8bc043e1be4377cbe.html).  

### 2024-11-05 · Sharing and Exporting Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.23 · SAP-ID 590834199*

You can now share task chains to other spaces where they can be nested in other task chains. This allows you to run tasks in multiple spaces from a single chain. In addition, you can export a task chain from its editor to a CSN/JSON file, which can then be imported into another space or tenant.See:  
- [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html)  
- [Sharing Entities and Task Chains to Other Spaces](64b318f8afd74bb78467cf56eb44294f.html)

### 2024-11-05 · Marketplace Data Product Lineage Available in the <span class="ph uicontrol">Impact and Lineage Analysis</span> Diagram

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.23 · SAP-ID 590833673*

The lineage for marketplace data products are visible in the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram.  
If you know the assets that are impacted by or in the lineage of a certain data product, you can access the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram from the assets details page. See [Catalog Asset Details](afccc581146542c485a52563167e23cc.html).  

### 2024-11-05 · Generate Explain Plan in View Analyzer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.23 · SAP-ID 590833733*

As a data modeler or data integrator, you have the option to generate an Explain Plan in the view analyzer to analyze the performance of a view and get a quick and efficient overview of your model.  
See [Getting Started with View Analyzer](e0aeddba00b14be29b5e49b47001d43b.html) .  

### 2024-11-05 · Data Type Validation Rule

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.23 · SAP-ID 590834261*

new validation rule lets you verify that your view columns have appropriate data types.See [Validating View Data](ed4063dc7f53436d806ea48f2fd3949a.html).  

### 2024-11-05 · Data Persistence Guidance in View Analyzer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.23 · SAP-ID 590834198*

The View Analyzer provides data persistence guidance. This feature provides concrete recommendations for views persist to improve performance, run-time, and memory consumption. The goal is to provide readily available recommendations to optimize performance and minimize evaluation time for the business user.  
See [Data Persistence Guidance](85f7be45761f4ae5b7e49819ce4b4d2e.html).

See

- [Integrating Data via Connections](eb85e157ab654152bd68a8714036e463.html)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.21 · SAP-ID 590833737*

You can now validate the status for connections to Apache Kafka and Confluent in the Connections app.See [Validate a Connection](99bd2294c3724d478c41555e186ce0ef.html).

Remote tables can now consume SAP HANA calculation view with input parameters.See [Review and Edit Imported Table Properties](75cea7b1cbeb4c9dba0ac13605c527b3.html).  

### 2024-10-08 · Manually Synchronize Source System After Application Upgrade

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.21 · SAP-ID 590833674*

If after an application upgrade the status for a source system becomes Outdated, the catalog administrator must run a manual synchronization.  
See [Manually Synchronizing a System](48c11915d74b41fcb8c07dd4bf1c4c86.html).  

### 2024-10-08 · Improved monitoring information for replication flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.21 · SAP-ID 590833736*

Monitoring information for replication flows is now available at partition level (not just for the replication flow run as a whole). Information at partition level is not available for replication flow runs that were started before version 2024.21 and are in status Completed or Failed. If you run the respective replication flow again, partitioning information is displayed for this latest run.  
For more information about monitoring, see [Monitoring Flows](b661ea0766a24c7d839df950330a89fd.html).  

### 2024-10-08 · Deleting a Space Moves it in the Recycle Bin

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.21 · SAP-ID 590834017*

To prevent spaces from being deleted unintentionally, deleting a space now moves the space in the new Recycle Bin area, from which you can either restore the space or permanently delete the space from the database.Note  
Be aware that deleting spaces via the command line interface continues to work as before, which means that the spaces are permanently deleted right away from the database.  
See [Delete Your Space](3eb19b96e6ba41dfbffd759c5c8370bb.html) and [Manage Deleted Spaces in the Recycle Bin](c4e26c09325a45d3ab7011a600c8fc6c.html).  

### 2024-10-08 · Cross-Space Analysis in View Analyzer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.21 · SAP-ID 590833738*

Models consuming entities shared from other spaces can now be analyzed fully in the View Analyzer.See [Exploring Views with View Analyzer](8921e5acf2ad4c8a98073edae4c214c7.html).  

### 2024-10-08 · Apply a Patch Release to SAP HANA Database

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.21 · SAP-ID 590833610*

You can upgrade your SAP HANA database patch version via the System   About dialog.See [Apply a Patch Upgrade to Your SAP HANA Database](489dc3b4ba4040b79bd97a1ca236c004.html).  

### 2024-09-24 · Order of Variables in Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.20 · SAP-ID 590833887*

You can now change the order of variables in the editor of an analytic model by drag&drop. This order will be taken into account in the data preview and in the prompt in SAP Analytics Cloud.  

### 2024-09-24 · Improvements to the Data Marketplace Data Product Details and Data Providers Profile Pages

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.20 · SAP-ID 590833675*

The layout of the pages for data marketplace data product details and data provider profiles has been updated so that the look and feel is harmonized to the catalog asset details pages. This improved layout provides visual consistency among all objects available in the catalog. Also, information about the data products and data providers is easier to find in the updated layout.  
See [Installing Marketplace Data Products](92c35efd6a4945a1a78250539aee9a51.html) and [Reviewing Data Provider Profiles](9ce49dcb70df4065880342beec03e25e.html).  

### 2024-09-24 · Delete Local Tables Records On-Demand, Using Filter Conditions or Using a Schedule.

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.20 · SAP-ID 590833739*

From the Local Tables monitor, you can clean up your table records:  
- Delete all records contained in the table.  
- Delete records using filter conditions.  
- Delete records marked as deleted in your local table where delta capture is enabled.  
See [Controlling Deletion of Local Table Records](2a59b7142e1b4d478b0bf4063084261e.html).  

### 2024-09-24 · Consume Analytic Models with Multi-Value Variables via OData API

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.20 · SAP-ID 590834260*

You can now consume analytic models containing multi-value variables via the OData consumption API.See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2024-09-24 · Activate or Deactivate a User via the SCIM 2.0 API

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.20 · SAP-ID 590834151*

You can now activate or deactivate a user via the SCIM 2.0 API by using the active attribute. See [Managing Users via the SCIM 2.0 API](1ca8c4a9467f43df9ae6d4ed3734f05a.html).  

### 2024-09-10 · Removing replicated and persisted data from tables and views in task chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.19 · SAP-ID 590834202*

When creating a task chain, the task chain editor now lets you choose to remove the replicated or persisted data for remote tables and views.  
See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html).  

### 2024-09-10 · Premium outbound volume for replication flows displayed in <span class="ph uicontrol">Flows</span> monitor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.19 · SAP-ID 590833740*

The total used premium outbound volume (for all replication flows in the tenant) for the last 90 days is now displayed directly in the Flows monitor. See [Working With Existing Replication Flow Runs](da62e1ee746448e8bc043e1be4377cbe.html).  

### 2024-09-10 · Navigation within and to the Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.19 · SAP-ID 590833888*

You can now navigate to entities used in the analytic model from the properties panel and from error messages via links.  
Your navigation steps are now included in the URL, which means you can easily share a link to a specific object to other users in your company.  

### 2024-09-10 · Data Viewer Support File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.19 · SAP-ID 590833889*

The new Export Support Information in the Data Viewer toolbar generates a file containing technical information to send to SAP support.See [Viewing Object Data](b338e4aa7e7e494eb68c383720ebfd3a.html).  

### 2024-09-10 · Cloud storage providers as sources for replication flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.19 · SAP-ID 590833741*

You can now use the following cloud storage providers and the corresponding connection types to add sources to replication flows:  
- Data lake Files from SAP HANA Cloud, data lake  
- Amazon Simple Storage Service  
- Google Cloud Storage  
- Microsoft Azure Data Lake Gen2  
See  
- [Cloud Storage Provider Sources for Replication Flows](4d481a2c620f4b52ba65b360299d7719.html)  
- [Integrating Data via Connections](eb85e157ab654152bd68a8714036e463.html)  

### 2024-09-10 · Changing a space source language for metadata translation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.19 · SAP-ID 590834201*

You can now change the source language of a Space enabled for Translation. For more information, see Translating Metadata for SAP Analytics Cloud. See [Translating Metadata for SAP Analytics Cloud](fe829debe389450394cf7a15860e2caa.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · Variable Import

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590834135*

You can import variables in the global filter of the query definition as well as in restricted key figures to SAP Datasphere and use them there in the analytic model. The default values set in SAP BW bridge are displayed in the preview. You can also define your own values.  

### 2024-08-27 · Set Space Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces in New Workload Management Area

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590834019*

You can now set space priorities and statement limits for all the spaces in the new Workload Management page (in System  Configuration).  
In the space details pages, you can view the values set for the space priority and statement limits but you can no longer edit them.  
See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · Select Space to Import Package

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590833894*

Previously, when working with content packages, you were restricted to importing into spaces with the same technical name as the spaces listed in the package. Now you can choose which spaces to import into by selecting a Space Mapping for each space listed in your package, so long as any shared objects can be resolved in the same package.See:  
- [Importing Content from Another Tenant](b607a12931d74c4a93506ea64c55ab4e.html)  
- [Importing SAP and Partner Business Content from the Content Network](400078d689bf4454b3fc977a4e201c2f.html)  

### 2024-08-27 · Release Stable Views for Consumption

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590833897*

To encourage confidence in the stability of your views and to guarantee backward compatibility when they are updated, you can set their Release State to Released. Once a view is released, it must continue to provide the same output columns until it is replaced by a successor, at which point it can be deprecated and, eventually, decommissioned.  
See [Releasing Stable Views for Consumption](5b99e9bcb5964ab69b094215d285feb1.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · New target objects for replication flow created at saving

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590834204*

For replication flows having the local repository (SAP Datasphere) as the target, new target objects would previously only be created when you deployed the flow. Now they are already created when you save the flow. This helps to ensure that dependencies are displayed correctly in Impact and Lineage Analysis as well as within packages, and it makes the deployment process faster. However it also means that certain changes to the replication flows are no longer possible (or have to be done in a different way) after you save the flow for the first time. See [Modify Replication Flow Object Schemas and Settings](a24c71f3ba7548909534d4cb52cefbfc.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · More Information Available on MDS Queries

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590834152*

When you enable SAP HANA multi-dimensional services (MDS) information tracing to analyze individual MDS queries in the System Monitor, the view M_MULTIDIMENSIONAL_STATEMENTS is now also made accessible in addition to the view M_MULTIDIMENSIONAL_STATEMENT_STATISTICS and provides extensive information about MDS queries.Also, you can now specify the maximum number of records that are stored for MDS requests in the monitoring tables.  
See [Configure Monitoring](9cd0691c44a74f2aa47b52f615f74433.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · Metadata API Layer for ABAP Customer Code

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590834134*

You now have the option of obtaining information about the metadata of SAP BW bridge objects using specific interfaces, and thus accessing these objects in your custom applications.The following interfaces provide you with access to the metadata:  
- IF_RS_BW_INFOPROVIDER (metadata for InfoProviders), i.e. DataStore objects  
- IF_RS_BW_DATASTORE (metadata for DataStore objects)  
- IF_RS_BW_INFOOBJECT (metadata for InfoObjects)  

### 2024-08-27 · Manage Transport Objects View

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590833559*

A new view is available in the BW modeling tools. In the Manage Transport Objects view, you can collect a large number of local objects in a list and assign them to a transport request.  

### 2024-08-27 · Improved User Experience for Monitoring Elastic Compute Nodes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590833613*

Before this version, one tab was automatically created in the System Monitor for each elastic compute node. You can now go in the new tab “Elastic Compute Nodes” and select the elastic compute node that you want to monitor. See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · Command Line Interface Changes for Setting Space Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590834018*

In consistency with the interface changes, we’ve made changes to the command line interface.The following parameters in the .json file containing the space definition are deprecated and will be removed in future releases:  
- priority  
- workloadClass.totalStatementMemoryLimit.value  
- workloadClass.totalStatementMemoryLimit.unit  
- workloadClass.totalStatementThreadLimit.value  
- workloadClass.totalStatementThreadLimit.unit  
Starting with this version, we recommend that you use these new commands:  
- The new datasphere workload list command allows you to list space priorities and statement limits for all spaces and to change them.  
- The new datasphere workload update command allows you to change the space priorities and statement limits for one or more spaces.  
See [Managing Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups via the Command Line](9e3537b72e6c445d9f34201df650735b.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · Automating the Creation and Processing of Data Protection Worklists

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590833560*

You can now check whether changes have been made to the data flow and then update the data protection template by choosing Sync Template.  

### 2024-08-27 · Automatically Publish Assets to the Catalog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590833676*

Organizations don't need separate enrichment and publishing steps. They can choose to automatically publish objects from the source system to the catalog. When the catalog synchronizes with the source system, assets will be automatically published to the catalog where catalog users can discover them.  
See [Publishing Content to the Catalog](f31d2d97afb0482a874acb0ec61465fe.html), [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html), and [Manually Synchronizing a System](48c11915d74b41fcb8c07dd4bf1c4c86.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · Add customer-specific projection logic to replication flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590834203*

You can now use a BAdI implementation to add your own projection logic for replication of CDS view data. Note that you can only use this feature if the corresponding BAdI is available for your source system release. See [Define Custom Projection Logic in a Replication Flow](970636e38ce44a10ab0a8734a284c7c9.html).  

### 2024-08-27 · Access to Data of DataStore Objects Via CDS Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590834133*

You can now customize your system to generate CDS views when generate DataStore objects. These CDS views provide a data model for the DataStore object and can be used for custom development.  

### 2024-08-27 · <span class="ph uicontrol">Start Date</span> Column for Filtering Tasks and Statements Logs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.18 · SAP-ID 590833611*

To improve performance when viewing the data on the Tasks and Statements tabs of the System Monitor, you can now filter by the start date of the tasks and statements. You can use  (Columns Settings) show or hide the Start Date column.See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2024-07-30 · View Data in Shared Views Containing Input Parameters

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590833892*

Previously, it was not possible to view data in a view that is shared from another space if the view contained input parameters. This restriction is now removed.See [Viewing Object Data](b338e4aa7e7e494eb68c383720ebfd3a.html).  

### 2024-07-30 · Use the ABAP SQL Service in SAP S/4HANA Cloud for Data Federation With Remote Tables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590833743*

You can now use the ABAP SQL Service for SQL-level remote access to data from published, ABAP-managed CDS view entities in SAP S/4HANA Cloud. Instead of accessing ABAP-managed database objects like tables or CDS views via the SQL layer of the SAP S/4HANA Cloud database, you can use the SQL service exposure to access SAP S/4HANA Cloud directly, taking advantage of ABAP-level security concepts and full ABAP SQL semantics.  
Using the SQL service, you can expose CDS view entities to SAP HANA by creating a service definition and binding and a corresponding communication arrangement. To facilitate this new way of accessing SAP S/4HANA Cloud data, we've added a new section called Remote Tables in the connection properties of the SAP S/4HANA Cloud connection type. In this section, you can choose between different data provisioning options, including the existing Data Provisioning Agent selection which moved from the Features section to the Remote Tables section. To use the ABAP SQL Service for federated access to CDS view entities, you select data provisioning option Direct.  
See:  
- [Why SQL Services?](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_S4HANA_CLOUD/6aa39f1ac05441e5a23f484f31e477e7/4082fe1b66164eeb8aa41192166526af.html) in the SAP S/4HANA Cloud documentation  
- [Using ABAP SQL Services for Accessing Data from SAP S/4HANA Cloud or SAP BTP ABAP Environment](ef2b2238154f4cd78a08df360447c1d5.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA Cloud Connections](a98e5ffdf47c44d9a845dca01a18bd82.html)  

### 2024-07-30 · Support User with <span class="ph uicontrol">DW Support User</span> and <span class="ph uicontrol">DW Scoped Support User</span> Role

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590833615*

If you have allowed SAP Product support to generate support users based on incidents, the support engineer can now request a support user with an extended global viewer role along with a scoped viewer role which has the spaces required to analyze the incident assigned as scopes. See [Request Help from SAP Technical Support](831a97714dfe4301918afece5a3b380b.html).  

### 2024-07-30 · Objects without primary key as sources for replication flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590833742*

You can now use CDS views and ODP artifacts that do not have a primary key as the source for a replication flow (load type Initial Only). See [SAP S/4HANA and Other ABAP Sources for Replication Flows](3f70579c92434f4f88471bba2bd70893.html).  

### 2024-07-30 · Extended User Experience Survey Available

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590834304*

To further improve your product experience, we're now offering an extended, 5-minute user experience survey. We assign each user to one of four survey phases per year, during which we provide a 21-day period to take the survey. You will receive a notification when the survey becomes available to you. During its availability, you can access the survey by clicking Give Feedback in the shell bar. Besides, the shorter feedback survey remains available at all times.See [Navigating in SAP Datasphere](bd79b74face14b17a4cfa844a2fd36c7.html).  

### 2024-07-30 · Attributes in Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590833891*

You can specify a custom prefix or suffix for the technical name of attributes. See [Add a Prefix or a Suffix to Dimension Attributes](0373c60d7e614dd5b5189de767bb672b.html).  

### 2024-07-30 · Access third-party data from Datarade

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590833677*

You can access additional third-party data products from various data providers on the Datarade website.  
See [Searching for Data Products and Assets in the Catalog](09bc571a876f448bb526a33d1ad00c61.html)  

### 2024-07-30 · Access third-party data from Datarade

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590833822*

You can use the Datarade data finder website to search and request additional third-party data products from various data providers.  
See [Searching for Data Products and Assets in the Catalog](10478251045b43e782fa15e0f3e113b0.html).  

### 2024-07-30 · Access Oracle On-Premise Databases With Oracle Connection Type

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.16 · SAP-ID 590833744*

Previously, data flows were able to consume data from Oracle databases available on the public internet. This restriction is removed. Using Cloud Connector with your Oracle connection, data flows now can also consume data from Oracle on-premise databases.See [Oracle Connections](c73ae0601d364f47830d339b6e86b7e8.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · View Data as Another User

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833983*

You can now use the View as User tool in the Data Viewer panel to review the effects of the data access controls you apply by checking the records that another user will be allowed to see.See [Viewing Object Data](b338e4aa7e7e494eb68c383720ebfd3a.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · UX Improvements for Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590834262*

We have improved the user experience of the editor of the analytic model:  
- You can now change the orientation of the layout of the diagram of the analytic model.  
- When you edit an analytic model, you can now undo or redo your changes.  

### 2024-07-16 · Space Storage Values Removed From <span class="ph uicontrol">Space Management</span> Tool

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590834021*

The total storage available and the amount assigned to and used by spaces are no longer displayed in the bars at the top of the Space Management page but instead in the System Monitor Dashboard tab. See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Persisted Views and Input Parameters

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833746*

Data persistence can be run for a view that contains one single input parameter, and if this input parameter has a default value maintained. The technical name of the parameter used to persist the view is displayed in the metrics tab in the Views monitor detail screen. See[Persisting and Monitoring Views](9af04c990f294fd28c00f46763dd8b0d.html) and [Understanding the Data Persistence Metrics](d65994c3611946c19c2634e1a6c0a7ad.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Now Including Elastic Compute Nodes Area

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590834020*

The Space Management now includes the area where administrators can create and manage elastic compute nodes. See [Add Scalable Processing Capacity via Elastic Compute Nodes](1f7c1811d264462fb0cd03e37da07ad7.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Local tables with load type <span class="ph uicontrol">Initial and Delta</span> as sources for replication flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833745*

Local tables that are enabled for delta capturing and have load type Initial and Delta can now be used as source objects in a replication flow. See [Add the Source for a Replication Flow](749638060e8746e48b0f9bf0e50c6d0c.html) and [Capturing Delta Changes in Your Local Table](154bdffb35814d5481d1f6de143a6b9e.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Improvements to the Asset Details Page

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833679*

The layout of the asset details page has been improved so that information about the asset (including details, relationships, and enrichments) is easier to find and manage.  
See [Accessing Catalog Assets](dc061a23484241b1b791f5540b1f38e3.html) and [Enriching and Managing Catalog Assets](7ed60a094f2a464da6a8d75e5bfed9d5.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · For Microsoft Azure Deployments: Virtual Network Subnet ID in <span class="ph uicontrol">About</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833616*

If you're using SAP Datasphere on Microsoft Azure and want to connect to a firewall-protected Microsoft Azure storage account, such as Microsoft Azure Data Lake Store Gen2, within the same Azure region, an Azure administrator must allow the SAP Datasphere's Virtual Network Subnet ID in the Microsoft Azure storage account. You can now find this ID in the SAP Datasphere Dialog About dialog. See [Obtain SAP Datasphere IP addresses For Allowlisting in Remote Systems](0934f7ed9a534e638299f53ab60866ae.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Folder Hierarchy for <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span> Objects Appears in the Impact and Lineage Diagram

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833678*

In SAP Analytics Cloud, objects can be saved to subfolder locations. When these objects are part of the impact and lineage diagram, their folder hierarchies appear as a series of nested containers.  
See [Accessing Catalog Assets](dc061a23484241b1b791f5540b1f38e3.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Export Data from Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833893*

In the data preview of the analytic model, you can now export the data as an Excel or CSV file. This enables you to share the intermediate results with colleagues, and to validate the calculations and measures.  

### 2024-07-16 · Elastic Compute Node Name Metric

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833748*

The details screen in the Views monitor displays the name of the elastic compute node used to persist the view.See [Understanding the Data Persistence Metrics](d65994c3611946c19c2634e1a6c0a7ad.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Downsize Tenant Memory

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833621*

In Tenant Configuration, you can now reduce the amount of memory allocated to your tenant. See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Data Products Visible in the Impact and Lineage Diagram

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590834176*

When catalog assets have lineage to a data product, that data product and its exposed entities appears in the Impact and Lineage Analysis diagram.  
See [Accessing Catalog Assets](dc061a23484241b1b791f5540b1f38e3.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Create Users and Assign Them to Roles via the SCIM 2.0 API

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590833619*

You can now create, read, update, and delete users and add them to roles via the SCIM 2.0 API.See [Managing Users via the SCIM 2.0 API](1ca8c4a9467f43df9ae6d4ed3734f05a.html).  

### 2024-07-16 · Change of Default Value for Total Statement Thread Limit

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590834311*

The value of the default configuration for the total amount of threads that each space can consume has been changed to 70%.See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html)  

### 2024-07-16 · Add Scalable Processing Capacity via Elastic Compute Nodes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.15 · SAP-ID 590834153*

If certain views and SAP HANA multi-dimensional services (MDS) requests regularly require more resources than are available on your SAP Datasphere tenant, you can now purchase additional on-demand compute and processing memory. You can then create elastic compute nodes, allocate the additional resources to them and schedule them to spin up to handle read peak loads.You can identify peak loads and monitor elastic compute nodes in dedicated dashboards in the System Monitor. Note  
Users of SAP Analytics Cloud and Microsoft Excel (with the SAP add-in) will then automatically benefit from the improved performance of the elastic compute nodes when consuming data exposed by SAP Datasphere.  
- To purchase compute and processing memory for elastic compute nodes in the Tenant Configuration page, see [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  
- To create and run elastic compute nodes in the Space Management, see [Add Scalable Processing Capacity via Elastic Compute Nodes](1f7c1811d264462fb0cd03e37da07ad7.html).  
- To monitor elastic compute nodes in the System Monitor, see [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  
In Tenant Configuration, you can also modify your elastic compute node consumption settings. See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2024-07-03 · Variables in the Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.14 · SAP-ID 590834263*

There are several changes for variables in analytic models:  
- You can now use standard variables in calculated measures.  
- We have renamed source variables to standard variables.  
- The data type of variables is now checked. You may need to select a data type in your variables now.  

### 2024-07-03 · Transport Your Content Through SAP Cloud Transport Management

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.14 · SAP-ID 590833747*

You can now integrate SAP Datasphere with SAP Cloud Transport Management service to transport content packages across different landscapes. See [Transporting Content Through SAP Cloud Transport Management](05383980f0704c71ab9872360ce45622.html).  

### 2024-07-03 · Tenant Linking Now in Administration App

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.14 · SAP-ID 590833617*

The Tenant Links tab for linking your SAP Datasphere tenant to an SAP Analytics Cloud Dialog tenant is now available in the Administration app.See [Review and Manage Links to SAP Analytics Cloud and SAP Business Data Cloud Tenants](40db56764bff4f9ab7eace16ac8e7e67.html).  

### 2024-07-03 · Manage Welcome Email Notification

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.14 · SAP-ID 590833622*

You can now disable the welcome email from being sent to new users added to your tenant. See [Configure Notifications](4388411066ef476aa99e4f35ce64bab6.html).  

### 2024-07-03 · Improved User Experience for Managing Tunnel Connections and Location IDs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.14 · SAP-ID 590833618*

When creating live data tunnel connections on the Data Integration tab of the Configuration tool, the user interface is reduced to the required functions and source system selection. On the Data Source Configuration tab of the Administration tool, when adding or editing a location, you can only see the required options.  

### 2024-07-03 · Configure OData Requests from Asset Editors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.14 · SAP-ID 590833895*

You can now configure your OData requests directly from the analytic model and graphical and SQL view editors, including providing values for variables, input parameters, and OData parameters. Your request is automatically generated and can be copied directly or downloaded in OpenAPI request format.See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2024-07-03 · Command Line Enhancements for Global and Scoped Roles

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.14 · SAP-ID 590833620*

The following enhancements are now available:  
- New datasphere global-roles commands allow you to list global roles and to add and remove users.  
- Role names can now be provided in all relevant commands without a technical prefix.  
- The following commands have been harmonized with the interface:  
- scoped-roles scopes add replaces scoped-roles scopes assign  
- scoped-roles scopes removereplaces scoped-roles scopes unassign  
- scoped-roles users add replaces scoped-roles users assign  
- scoped-roles users remove replaces scoped-roles users unassign  
- The JSON formats for adding and removing users to and from roles and adding roles to users are simplified.  
- The JSON format for creating and updating scoped roles now accepts inheritance of the role template as a string.  
Note  
The assign and unassign commands and old JSON formats are deprecated and will be removed in a future release.  
See [Manage Users and Roles via the Command Line](3a3d0ef3d4954797acac12afbcf9ab5d.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Views in Transformation Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590834205*

You can now add a view as a source in a transformation flow. See [Creating a Transformation Flow](f7161e6c20204672ac4a6d90c81762e4.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Search for Data Providers

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833680*

Use the new Data Provider collection or the Data Provider filter when searching for data providers. A data provider is a person or company that offers one or multiple data products through the Data Marketplace.  
See [Searching for Data Products and Assets in the Catalog](09bc571a876f448bb526a33d1ad00c61.html)  

### 2024-06-18 · SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Import Enhancements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590834264*

You can now choose to use replication flows when importing entities from SAP S/4HANA on-premise connections.See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP S/4HANA](845fedbd28574aa8b84239df848936f6.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Replication flows: parallel processing during delta load

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590834207*

For CDS views and, SLT tables that have load type Initial and Delta, you can now specify a thread count greater than 1 for delta load and thus enable parallel processing.  
See [Configure the Run Settings of a Replication Flow](3f5ba0c5ae3944c1b7279bb989a2a5b5.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Persist Views Protected with Data Access "Operator and Value" and "Hierarchy"

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833750*

You can persist a view when one or multiple "Operator and Value" or "Hierarchy" DACs are assigned to the view.See[Persisting and Monitoring Views](9af04c990f294fd28c00f46763dd8b0d.html) .  

### 2024-06-18 · Open SQL Schema Objects in Transformation Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833752*

You can now add an open SQL schema object as a source in a transformation flow. See [Creating a Transformation Flow](f7161e6c20204672ac4a6d90c81762e4.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Local Tables Monitor Available in the Data Integration Monitors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833753*

See [Monitoring Local Tables](374046156e5b47599fc9b96c8c3a4dce.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Include Open SQL Schema Procedures in Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833749*

You can now include Open SQL procedures within task chains. See [Run Open SQL Procedures in a Task Chain](59b9c773035a48c5beb54ce9bb29f1d8.html). For procedures to be available to be included in a task chain, an administrator must grant EXECUTE privileges for objects in the Open SQL schema space. See [Allow the Space to Access the Open SQL Schema](7eaa370fe4624dea9f182ee9c9ab645f.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Improved status texts in replication flow monitoring

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833754*

The status descriptions in replication flow monitoring have been rephrased so that they are more self-explanatory. In particular, they now differentiate more clearly between the situation at flow level and at object level. See also [Working With Existing Replication Flow Runs](da62e1ee746448e8bc043e1be4377cbe.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Enter a Description for Your Space

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590834022*

You can now enter descriptive information about your space in a new dedicated field.  
See [Create a Space](bbd41b82ad4d4d9ba91341545f0b37e7.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · E/R Model Data Viewer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833896*

The E/R model editor now allows you to view data for any diagram entity with support for rich sorting and filtering.See [Viewing Object Data](b338e4aa7e7e494eb68c383720ebfd3a.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Data Validation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833898*

We have introduced a new validation framework to help you to validate the data output by your views. In this version, we include initial validation rules to check the consistency of your key columns and hierarchy nodes.  
See [Validating View Data](ed4063dc7f53436d806ea48f2fd3949a.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Data Access Control Metrics

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833751*

The details screen in the Views monitor now indicates if your view data is protected by a data access control.See [Understanding the Data Persistence Metrics](d65994c3611946c19c2634e1a6c0a7ad.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Column Lineage

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590833904*

The Impact and Lineage Analysis viewer now allows you to select one or more columns in your analyzed object and show their sources and the columns that they impact.  
See [Impact and Lineage Analysis](9da4892cb0e4427ab80ad8d89e6676b8.html).  

### 2024-06-18 · Add new replication objects without impacting running replication flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.13 · SAP-ID 590834206*

As a Data Integrator you want to monitor all local tables whichYou can now add objects to a replication flow in status Active without having to stop the run first. See [Working With Existing Replication Flow Runs](da62e1ee746448e8bc043e1be4377cbe.html).  

### 2024-05-22 · Transport layer security for Schema Registry in Confluent Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590833755*

You can now use TLS encryption for the Schema Registry in Confluent connections.See [Confluent Connections](d83c08ad4eaf49dba9602b1d51c07a52.html#loiod83c08ad4eaf49dba9602b1d51c07a52).  

### 2024-05-22 · Restore Accidentally Deleted Service Instance

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590834154*

If you accidentally deleted your SAP Datasphere service instance in SAP BTP, you can now restore it within seven days.  
See [Delete Your Service Instance in SAP BTP](2665ce1e83314dd396cea0b88ed5af40.html).  

### 2024-05-22 · Process Graph Display

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590833561*

In the SAP BW Bridge Cockpit in the Process Graph Display app, you can find a graphical display in the form of a Gantt chart that shows all SAP BW processes that ran in the selected period of time. This allows you to see which processes have consumed how many resources.  
In addition to the status of the process, the following key figures can be visualized:  
- Average CPU Usage (ABAP) A list of process chains, sorted by the average CPU usage caused by processes in ABAP.  
- Average Peak Memory (ABAP) A list of process chains, sorted by the average peak memory usage caused by processes in ABAP.  
- Average CPU Usage (Database) A list of process chains, sorted by the average CPU usage caused by processes on the SAP HANA database.  
- Average Peak Memory Usage (Database) A list of process chains, sorted by the average peak memory usage caused by processes on the SAP HANA database.  
See [Process Graph Display](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/d8e576798cd842559667598b1d00112e.html)  

### 2024-05-22 · Package Field in Additional Editors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590833900*

When one or more packages is available in your space, modelers can now a state that is not changing every day. See package directly from the relevant editor:  
- Business entity  
- Fact model  
- Consumption model  
- Authorization Scenario  
See [Packages](a806c67ed11749c788142775d2cc2494.html).  

### 2024-05-22 · External Access to Process Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590834136*

As an SAP BW bridge administrator, you want to control external process chains, that is process chains from a third-party tool or from SAP BW / SAP BW∕4HANA. You can do this by using the following communication scenarios in SAP BW bridge:[Integration of Process Chain API (Communication Scenario SAP_COM_0941)](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/f01aefe0cc5442d0808d847c365b5630.html) and [Integration of Process Type Remote Process Chain (Communication Scenario SAP_COM_0A16)](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/895a1b2b9cc54662becc13c65c6adde3.html)  

### 2024-05-22 · Enhancement of Monitoring While Uploading data in Table Editor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590833756*

Upload of data in a table can now be monitored in a details log that is accessible by clicking on the task in the notification area or navigating to the logs tab of the System Monitor.See [Load or Delete Local Table Data](870401f211f94132909bd9f2fafd91b2.html).  

### 2024-05-22 · Enhancement of Dependent Objects for Tables with Delta Capture Enabled

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590833906*

If a local table with delta capture enabled is used in a replication flow or a transformation flow, information is now displayed in the Dependent Objects of the local table.See [Review the Objects That Depend on Your Table or View](ecac5fdf2a81435fa6dbd51975e8416d.html)  

### 2024-05-22 · Enable Input Parameters for Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590833823*

You can now use artifacts containing input parameters in data products.See [Creating Marketplace Data Products](bbcbf42b0cb541529e63628d95f394c8.html).  

### 2024-05-22 · Data Persistence Metrics

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590834208*

Once a data persistence task is completed, metrics information relating to the persisted view are displayed  
See [Understanding the Data Persistence Metrics](d65994c3611946c19c2634e1a6c0a7ad.html).  

### 2024-05-22 · Data Access Controls Move to Data Builder App

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.11 · SAP-ID 590833984*

Data access controls are now listed in the Data Builder app.Note  
In addtion to the Data Warehouse Data Access Control (CRUD----) privileges, users needing to create and edit data access controls must also have Data Warehouse Data Builder (CRUD----). All necessary permissions are included in the standard DW Space Administrator role.  
In addition, you can now import and export data access controls in CSN/JSON files.  
If you have previously bookmarked any data access controls, you should update your bookmarks from:https://<tenant_url>/index.html#/authorizations&/auth/<SpaceID>/<TechnicalName>To:https://<tenant_url>/index.html#/databuilder&/db/<SpaceID>/<TechnicalName>  
See:  
- [Securing Data with Data Access Controls](a032e51c730147c7a1fcac125b4cfe14.html)  
- [Importing and Exporting Objects in CSN/JSON Files](f8ff0628c9fc49229740ffcd4d20e9aa.html)  
- [Privileges by App, Tool, Object, and Task](2d8b7d04dcae402f911d119437ce0a74.html)  
.  

### 2024-05-07 · Task Chain Email Notification

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.10 · SAP-ID 590833759*

If email notification is set up for a task chain, you'll now receive email notifications if an error occurs during initialization or preparation to run the task chain, before the task chain run actually starts.See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html).  

### 2024-05-07 · Improved User Experience for Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.10 · SAP-ID 590833899*

For the analytic model, we have improved the user experience:  
You can now copy existing measures to create a new measure.  
For measures, attributes and associated dimensions there is now a Select All/Unselect All checkbox.  

### 2024-05-07 · Improved Resiliency

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.10 · SAP-ID 590833623*

For resilience purposes, we are now limiting the maximum CPU resources for workload generated by spaces, user group users and agent users to 80%. The remaining 20% of CPU resources are reserved to ensure that the system can respond under heavy load.  
You can now configure the total amount of threads that each space can consume up to a maximum of 100% of this 80% of the threads available in your tenant.  
Also, statements are now queued when the CPU usage reaches 90% (instead of 80%) of the database capacity. This value, which you cannot change, applies to all spaces, including spaces created before this version.  
See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  

### 2024-05-07 · Duplicating Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.10 · SAP-ID 590833824*

You can now duplicate existing data products.  
See [Duplicating Data Products](14232081d7444195b1f66c16e56f6d09.html).  

### 2024-04-23 · Replication-Free Consumption of <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span> Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590834266*

You can now persist your SAP Analytics Cloud models directly in the SAP Datasphere repository and choose to expose them for use as sources of SAP Datasphere views and flows.See [Consume SAP Datasphere Data in SAP Analytics Cloud via an OData Service](9de2c660fd3b4db2b89ad25e584e8857.html)  

### 2024-04-23 · RFC Serialization for ABAP-Based Connection Types

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590834209*

For the connection types SAP ABAP, SAP BW, SAP ECC, and SAP S/4HANA On-Premise, we've added RFC Serialization to the advanced connection properties. The property is available with Data Provisioning Agent version 2.3.7.2 or higher. You can use it to specify whether to use fast column-based serialization or row-based serialization when transferring data. The default is column-based serialization. See:  
- [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html)  
- [SAP BW Connections](e589041e80264f43b6c209c407336376.html)  
- [SAP ECC Connections](e546ccd61af54bf49a0f531a43fe0961.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Connections](a49a1e3cc50f4af89711d8306bdd8f26.html)  

### 2024-04-23 · Processing Mode to Persist View Data is Asynchronous Only

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590833758*

To ensure better resilience, the SQL procedure to persist view data is always executed asynchronously.  

### 2024-04-23 · Measure Type <span class="ph uicontrol">Non-Cumulative Measure</span> for Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590833901*

When one or more packages is available in your space, modelers can now a state[Create a Non-Cumulative Measure](58fcee02df8044119777cf060000aca8.html) .  

### 2024-04-23 · Import Entities from SAP S/4HANA Cloud via Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590833905*

If your SAP S/4HANA Cloud connection supports using replication flows to load data to local tables, the Import Entities dialog will now default to this data access setting.See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP S/4HANA](845fedbd28574aa8b84239df848936f6.html).  

### 2024-04-23 · Disable Real-Time Data Replication When the Source System is not Available

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590834211*

When the source system is not available, you can disable real-time data replication from the Remote Tables monitor or from the table editor of the Data Builder. The data access of your remote table is switched from Replicated (Real-time) to Replicated (Snapshot), and the replicated data are kept to allow further consumption or actions on the object such as deleting or changing the properties of the connection. See [Disabling Real-Time Data Replication](82380e6f01c84fac898a285ac40a1e50.html).  

### 2024-04-23 · Delta-Enabled Local Tables As Source for Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590833757*

For local tables as the source of a replication flow, you can now also use local tables that are enabled for delta capturing. However this is still only possible for load type Initial Only ( not Initial and Delta).  
See [Add the Source for a Replication Flow](749638060e8746e48b0f9bf0e50c6d0c.html).  

### 2024-04-23 · Define Filter Conditions While Creating a Data Flow When Your Data Source Come From Open Connectors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590833902*

When you create a data flow and the data source come from Open Connectors, you can define filter conditions to apply on the data.See [Add a Source to a Data Flow](7b50e8e304244ced9ff1e62e2f2fe919.html)  

### 2024-04-23 · Create and Update Modeling Objects via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590834265*

You can now create and update modeling objects of any type from the command line using the following commands:  
- datasphere objects <object-type> create --space <id> --file-path <file>.json  
- datasphere objects <object-type> update --space <id> --technical-name <name> --file-path <file>.json  
See [Manage Modeling Objects and Tasks via the Command Line](6f5c65f209004751aa48f9682ee2ec45.html).  

### 2024-04-23 · Changes to Associations in the Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590833903*

When an association in the underlying views or tables of an analytic model is changed or deleted, you can still save the analytic model, but you get an error message and the status of the analytic model changes to design time error.  

### 2024-04-23 · Assets from <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span><span class="ph pname">, SAP BW bridge</span> Available in the Impact and Lineage Diagram

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590833681*

Assets from SAP Datasphere, SAP BW bridge source systems appear in the impact and lineage diagram.  
See [Accessing Catalog Assets](dc061a23484241b1b791f5540b1f38e3.html).  

### 2024-04-23 · Add a Visual Tenant Type Indicator

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590834155*

You can now add a visual tenant type indicator to distinguish which system you are using. For example, between a test or production system. When enabled, a colored information bar is visible to all users of the tenant, and the browser favicon is updated with the matching color.See [Display Your System Information](6bdd79878afa4ec5bcd9d3502158a06e.html).  

### 2024-04-23 · Ability to Add an <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span><span class="ph pname">, SAP BW bridge</span> as a Source System

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.9 · SAP-ID 590833682*

You can add an SAP Datasphere, SAP BW bridge system to the catalog. With this addition, metadata for the following objects from SAP BW Cloud systems are monitored and extracted to the catalog.  
- ADSO (Advanced DataStore Object)  
- AREA - (InfoArea)  
- HCPR (Composite Provider)  
- IOBJ (InfoObject)  
- LSYS (Source System)  
- RSDS (DataSource)  
- TRCS (InfoSource)  
- TRFN (Transformation)  
Once extracted, you can publish these objects as assets to the catalog.  
See [Connecting to BW Systems](b350d7c6e88c4f089d381dfe6feee792.html).  

### 2024-04-11 · Scheduling and Task Chains for Replication Flows (Initial Only)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590833760*

Replication flows can now be scheduled and included in task chains if all their objects have load type Initial Only.See:  
- [Schedule a Data Integration Task (Simple Schedule)](7c11059ed3314e1fb753736b7867512c.html)  
- [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html)  

### 2024-04-11 · New Feedback Button to Comment all Guides

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590834305*

A few months ago, we have enabled 3 of our guides to be commented using the Open Documentation Initiative. See [blog](https://community.sap.com/t5/technology-blogs-by-sap/open-documentation-initiative-for-sap-datasphere-provide-feedback-and-help/ba-p/13562111)  
As from wave 2024.08 all SAP Datasphere guides can be commented directly from the Help Portal: the Feedback button becomes visible in the action bar once you are logged in  

### 2024-04-11 · More Granular Control Over Tenant Configuration

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590834156*

In Tenant Configuration, the size of storage blocks been reduced to offer you more granular control over your tenant configuration. You can now set tenant memory by size instead of by the number of compute blocks. Performance classes have been added to allow you to choose the vCPU allocation to your tenant.See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2024-04-11 · Modify Data Lake Storage in Increments of 1 TB

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590833624*

In Tenant Configuration, you may now increase the size of data lake storage in increments of 1 TB instead of 5 TB.See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html) .  

### 2024-04-11 · Improved Object Selection Experience

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590833907*

The improved Select Object dialog provides new filtering options to help you quickly find your required object when creating associations and doing other tasks that require you to select an object from your space. This new dialog is available when creating an association, a text association, applying a data access control, and adding related entities to an E/R model diagram.  

### 2024-04-11 · Control OAuth Client Token Lifetimes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590833626*

You can now control the duration of the token and refresh token lifetimes for your OAuth clients.See [Create OAuth2.0 Clients to Authenticate Against SAP Datasphere](3f92b46fe0314e8ba60720e409c219fc.html)  

### 2024-04-11 · Confluent Kafka as Target for Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590834210*

You can now use Confluent Kafka and the corresponding connection type to add targets to replication flows.See:  
- [Confluent Kafka Targets for Replication Flows](74b3c95464f246aa8c3fd510661daa6d.html)  
- [Integrating Data via Connections](eb85e157ab654152bd68a8714036e463.html)  
- [Confluent Connections](d83c08ad4eaf49dba9602b1d51c07a52.html#loiod83c08ad4eaf49dba9602b1d51c07a52)  

### 2024-04-11 · Catena-X Access Policy

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590834240*

Use the Catena-X Access Policy context to create a private offering within the Catena-X network.  
See [The Context Management App](2f3dbb9da8ae4b7a992c6358ab4fc2be.html).  

### 2024-04-11 · CLI Commands for Licenses, Releases, and Contexts

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590833825*

You can now manage Data Marketplace licenses, releases, and contexts using the command line.  
See:  
- [Managing Data Marketplace Licenses via the Command Line](39ea782902c444fdb3f350cc4c19b4bc.html)  
- [Managing Data Marketplace Releases via the Command Line](2199031b1ee84f7999e98537a5d44e44.html)  
- [Managing Data Marketplace Contexts via the Command Line](b90ff75d933546ad81891f73b681820f.html)  

### 2024-04-11 · Assign Yourself to a Scoped Role

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590833625*

You can now assign yourself to a scoped role.See [Create a Scoped Role to Assign Privileges to Users in Spaces](b5c4e0b6c462414783ebbfc053815521.html), [Assign Users to a Role](57a78804d8bd48ca9e7e73a0d33e355f.html) and [Control User Access to Your Space](9d59fe511ae644d98384897443054c16.html).  

### 2024-04-11 · <span class="ph uicontrol">Views</span> Monitor Displays all Deployed Views Into 3 Tabs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.8 · SAP-ID 590833761*

In Views monitor, you can now see all views that have been created in the Data Builder without additional actions from your side. Views are displayed into 3 tabs to allow a better navigation:  
- All Views: It displays all the deployed views that have been created in the Data Builder for the current space.  
- Scheduled (default): It displays the views for which a schedule is defined to persist the data.  
- Persisted: It displays the views already persisted.  
See [Persisting and Monitoring Views](9af04c990f294fd28c00f46763dd8b0d.html).  

### 2024-03-12 · X.509 Client Certificate Support for <span class="ph uicontrol">SAP HANA</span> Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590833762*

You can now use X.509 client certificates or certificate chains for authentication when creating connections to SAP HANA in a more secure way. For new connections, X.509 Client Certificate becomes the default authentication type.See [SAP HANA Connections](e6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9.html#loioe6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9).  

### 2024-03-12 · View Partitioning Advisor Provides More Precise Findings

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590834213*

In Views monitor, the findings provided with the option Rate Column Suitability while creating partitions to persist your views, have been refined with more precise information.See [Creating Partitions for Your Persisted Views](9b1b595054c34da5b67ac07595093f82.html).  

### 2024-03-12 · Improved User Experience for Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590833910*

We have improved the user experience for the analytic model. Apart from smaller adjustments, semantic information for fact source measures is displayed. On the entry page of the analytic model editor, the deployment date is now displayed. You can now delete multiple dimensions at once. In the details for analytic models, measures, attributes, and variables, the dependencies are now displayed.  

### 2024-03-12 · Improved Consumption of Associations in Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590833908*

In cases where multiple associations and/or text association are defined on a single attribute in a fact or dimension, you can now specify which association (or local text attribute) should be used for each attribute when analytic models consume your entity.See [Specify Attributes as Keys, Units, and Other Characteristics](cedc59c994cf488bb6b43b105e72c4d3.html).  

### 2024-03-12 · Delta Extraction

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590833563*

You can now import tables for delta extraction from an SAP BW bridge connection into an SAP BW bridge space.  

### 2024-03-12 · Data Center Availability

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590833562*

SAP BW bridge is now available for the EU30 (Europe -Frankfurt), IN30 (India - Mumbai), and US30 (US Central -IA) regions running on the Google Cloud Platform (GCP).See [Creating and Configuring Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](2f80b57359254b5cbb4faac794ade90c.html).  

### 2024-03-12 · Consume Analytic Models with Single-Value Variables via OData API

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590834268*

You can now consume analytic models containing variables that require single values via the OData consumption API.See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2024-03-12 · Additional Open SQL Schema Table Metadata

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590834267*

When you import SAP HANA SQL views via Open SQL schemas, you can now obtain input parameters and Not Null and Default Value metadata for columns.See [Review and Edit Imported Table Properties](75cea7b1cbeb4c9dba0ac13605c527b3.html).  

### 2024-03-12 · Add Source Tables from SAP BW Bridge Spaces to Transformation Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.6 · SAP-ID 590834212*

You can add remote tables located in SAP BW bridge spaces to a transformation flow. See [Creating a Transformation Flow](f7161e6c20204672ac4a6d90c81762e4.html).  

### 2024-02-27 · Settings and Notifications in the SAP BW Bridge Cockpit

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.5 · SAP-ID 590833565*

In the Me Area in the SAP BW bridge cockpit, you can make launchpad settings.  
The SAP Fiori launchpad displays notifications from the Request Management and Process Chains areas that are relevant for you due to your business role.  
See [Notifications](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/6c5d18f5abbf48d995cf173e979b8ec8.html) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2024-02-27 · Process Chains for Streaming

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.5 · SAP-ID 590833564*

A process chain for streaming can be started and executed very frequently, and makes it possible to update data in real-time to DataStore objects. Process chains therefore provide a holistic solution for periodic and very frequent scheduling and for streaming. You set whether or not a process chain should be used for streaming using an attribute in the process chain. See [Process Chains for Streaming](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/ac029de05e164a12ac1ce08d16180f05.html) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2024-02-27 · New <span class="ph uicontrol">Authorization Overview</span> page

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.5 · SAP-ID 590833627*

Users with the DW Administrator global role can use the new Authorization Overview page to see all the users, roles, and spaces in the tenant and how they relate to each other. You can filter by user, role, or space to see, for example, which users are assigned with which roles to a particular space.
