See [View Authorizations by User, Role, or Space](c6538ea2db884e599072e62bdb03e68c.html).  

### 2024-02-27 · Metadata Extraction as Part of Role SAP_BR_DEVELOPER_DWC

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.5 · SAP-ID 590834138*

Using the role SAP_BR_DEVELOPER_DWC, you can now extract metadata from SAP BW bridge using the catalog in SAP Datasphere. See [Business Role Templates and Business Catalogs](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/75d2fa4c36a548ca86d0b8527123beb6.html) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2024-02-27 · Data Tiering Optimization

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.5 · SAP-ID 590834137*

Data Tiering allows you to assign data to various storage areas and storage media. The critera for this decision are data type, operational considerations, performance requirements, frequency of access, and security requirements. Data Tiering Optimization (DTO) helps you to classify the data in your DataStore object as warm or cold, depending on how frequently it is accessed. See [Data Tiering Optimization (DTO)](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/9d76dae79ab047099ee81b50208d5945.html) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2024-02-27 · Creating Objects

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.5 · SAP-ID 590833567*

If you create an InfoObject or a DataStore object and select the package ZLOCAL or a subordinate package, the system issues a warning as objects in the package ZLOCAL cannot be transported.  

### 2024-02-27 · APIs for InfoObjects and DataStore Objects

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.5 · SAP-ID 590833566*

You can use various APIs to load, activate, and delete data. See [APIs for InfoObjects and DataStore Objects](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/b3701cd3826440618ef938d74dc93c51/188562b742aa4f1ebd355294086049f6.html) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2024-02-13 · Operator and Values Data Access Control

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590833986*

You can now create data access controls in which criteria are defined as operator and value pairs. Each user can only see the records that fulfill the operator-value pairs she is authorized for in the permissions entity, including support for complex AND and OR combinations. See [Create an "Operator and Values" Data Access Control](501594bf2afb4e49ab5ce254e35e3504.html).  

### 2024-02-13 · New Monitoring View SPACE_USERS_V_EXT

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590833628*

The new monitoring view SPACE_USERS_V_EXT is deployed in the DWC_GLOBAL schema and tracks information about the user types.  
See [Working with SAP HANA Monitoring Views](4ab45090c5684ebf8765757a1dfc4e5d.html).  

### 2024-02-13 · Nested Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590833911*

In addition to Remote Table Replication, View Persistency, Intelligent Lookup, Data Flow, and Transformation Flow tasks, you can now also nest other task chains within a task chain.  

### 2024-02-13 · Hierarchy Data Access Controls

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590833985*

You can now create data access controls in which criteria are defined as hierarchy values. Each user can only see the records that match the hierarchy values she is authorized for in the permissions entity, along with any of their descendants. See [Create a "Hierarchy" Data Access Control](0afeeed6b56a40f5a02581032ec39420.html).  

### 2024-02-13 · Enhancement of Data Persistence Run Logs with Spaces Information

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590833764*

When you persist view data that is coming from different spaces, you can display the information of the data provenance by spaces clicking in the View Details in the log. From this View Details, you can navigate to the Remote Queries monitor and check the statement details.  

### 2024-02-13 · Default Values for Remote Tables Input Parameters

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590833912*

You can define default values for each remote table parameter. See [Review and Edit Imported Table Properties](75cea7b1cbeb4c9dba0ac13605c527b3.html)  

### 2024-02-13 · Data Preview for ABAP CDS Views and SAP Landscape Transformation Replication Server Objects in Data Flow Editor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590834269*

You can now preview data for ABAP CDS views and SAP Landscape Transformation Replication Server objects if your system meets the requirements:  
- For CDS views, the source connection is SAP S/4HANA Cloud 2302, SAP S/4HANA on-premise 1909 or higher.  
- For SAP Landscape Transformation Replication Server objects, ABAP Add-on DMIS 2018 SP08 / DMIS 2020 SP04 is installed.  
See [Creating a Data Flow](e30fd1417e954577baae3246ea470c3f.html)  

### 2024-02-13 · Change the Run Mode for a Transformation Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590834214*

You can change the run mode for a transformation flow and choose between performance and memory consumption. See [Change Transformation Flow Settings](f7da0290f5084e3db17c65b3d7c3140a.html).  

### 2024-02-13 · Change the Data Persistence Run Mode

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590834218*

From the Views monitor, in the details screen of a data persistence view run, you can change the run mode to choose between performance and memory consumption depending on your needs.See [Change Persisted View Settings](d04f5dd6bbda44448407d54d2a7979be.html).  

### 2024-02-13 · Additional Columns in Remote Queries Monitor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.4 · SAP-ID 590834216*

The Remote Queries monitor have been enhanced with additional columns, which provide additional information regarding used sources and runtimes. See [Monitoring Remote Queries](806d7f0c45a14f1fb07db0a226b2b822.html).  

### 2024-02-01 · When Running a Manual Synchronization, Metadata from Only the Objects that Have Changed is Extracted

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590833683*

When a manual synchronization is run on an existing source system, only the objects that have been added or changed since the last manual synchronization are updated in the catalog. This ensures that only the necessary resources are used for the synchronization.  

### 2024-02-01 · Variables for Currency Conversion Measures

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590834270*

For currency conversion measures in the analytic model, you can now define variables for the target currency, the reference date, and the exchange rate type. For more information, see [Create a Conversion Measure for Currencies](ec00efb338f3421a87dab4006d7ce6c8.html).  

### 2024-02-01 · Validation of Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590833913*

Validations for analytic models using level based hierarchies have been improved. Some analytic models may now raise an error message and require adaptation.  

### 2024-02-01 · Repository Folders

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590834274*

You can now use folders to organize the objects in your space. You can create a folder in the Repository Explorer, Business Builder, or Data Builder and it is immediately available across each of these apps. Once a folder is created, you can move objects to it or create objects in it.  
You can create as many levels of folder as necessary.Note  
The requirement that each object in a space must have a unique technical name, remains unchanged.  
See [Organizing Objects in Folders](bbedad63821443518fcd8fe56f7d26f7.html).  

### 2024-02-01 · Read and Delete Modeling Objects via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590833924*

You can now list modeling objects of any type in your space, and read and delete individual objects from the command line using the following commands:  
- datasphere objects <object-type> list --space <id>  
- datasphere objects <object-type> read --space <id> --technical-name <name>  
- datasphere objects <object-type> delete --space <id> --technical-name <name>  
See [Manage Modeling Objects and Tasks via the Command Line](6f5c65f209004751aa48f9682ee2ec45.html).  

### 2024-02-01 · Improved Display of Warning Messages in <span class="ph uicontrol">Connections</span> App

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590833763*

You now have a better overview of all connections with warning messages. When there is one or more messages for your connections, a button is displayed in the toolbar of the Connections app specifying the number of warning messages for your connections. Clicking the button opens the list of messages providing guidance on how to solve the issues. In the connections list, connections with warning messages are highlighted in yellow. See [Integrating Data via Connections](eb85e157ab654152bd68a8714036e463.html).  

### 2024-02-01 · Improved Data Builder Source Browser

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590833918*

The new Repository tab of the Source Browser shows all the tables, views, and intelligent lookups in your space in a single list and lets you filter by collection (All, Recent, My Objects, Shared, Favorites). You can modify the sorting, perform complex filtering, and drill down into folders, as well as previewing properties and data before adding your source.  
See [Using the Source Browser](7d2b21d974e44bdc9d548cf7532b5a43.html).  

### 2024-02-01 · Derived Variables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590833909*

In the analytic model, you can now use variables which derive their value through a lookup entity.  

### 2024-02-01 · Create an SQL View Transform For a Transformation Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590834217*

For transformation flows, you can now create an SQL view transform to combine and transform data in a powerful SQL editor.See [Create an SQL View in a Transformation Flow](775e0ab758e54c58af5a240294d234f2.html).  

### 2024-02-01 · Business Builder Objects in the Repository Explorer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590833919*

Business Builder objects are now listed alongside Data Builder objects in the Repository Explorer (while remaining accessible via the Business Builder).See [Repository Explorer](f8ce0b4a24fe473a962176c8aa3cad42.html).  

### 2024-02-01 · Add Connections to Packages and Transport Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.3 · SAP-ID 590834215*

You can now add connections to packages either via the  (Transport) app or when creating or editing connections. Furthermore, you can export and import connections via the  (Transport) app. If your imported content included one or more connections that didn't exist in your space before the import, you must:.  
- Edit the connections to complete their configuration and provide the credentials to connect to the remote systems  
- Manually deploy imported objects that depend on the imported connections (and which could not, therefore, be automatically deployed)  
See:  
- [Transporting Content Between Tenants](df12666cf98e41248ef2251c564b0166.html)  
- [Create a Connection](c2165842082c43fc85bad9f0c97572bb.html)  
- [Edit a Connection](ba20892f25744b86a62ae571e3ea6ead.html)  

### 2024-01-17 · Watermarks for Transformation Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.2 · SAP-ID 590833767*

When you run a transformation flow that loads delta changes to a target table, the system uses a watermark (a timestamp) to track the data that has been transferred. You can now view the watermark for a source table. You can also reset the watermark if required.  
If you reset a watermark, the system will transfer all data to the target table the next time the transformation flow runs (using the load type Initial and Delta).  
See [Watermarks](890897f00a4944c7a6f90d3816a8d4c6.html).  

### 2024-01-17 · Suppress Duplicate Records

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.2 · SAP-ID 590833766*

When replicating data to a cloud storage provider, you now have the option to suppress duplicate records in the target.  
See [Using a Cloud Storage Provider As the Target](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_DATASPHERE/c8a54ee704e94e15926551293243fd1d/43d93a27150a4a218e3df14e3abdf456.html).  

### 2024-01-17 · Include Data Products in the Search Results

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.2 · SAP-ID 590833684*

Data products are data purchased from a third-party provider. Listed data products available in data marketplace are searchable in the catalog.  
See [Catalog Tools and Features](bfc939d764c5490b96c5b1684f10a9cf.html).  

### 2024-01-17 · Identify Source Changes for Several Tables Sharing the Same Connection

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.2 · SAP-ID 590833921*

Identify available table structure updates for all tables sharing the same source connection, and resolve conflicting errors resulting from these updates.See [Process Source Changes for Several Remote Tables](4e0be1612c9147069d0cddc9c6201455.html).  

### 2024-01-17 · Enable Apache Spark Compatibility

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.2 · SAP-ID 590833765*

When replicating data to Parquet files in a cloud storage provider, you can now have the time data type columns converted into int64 (microseconds after midnight) so that they can be consumed by Apache Spark.  
See [Using a Cloud Storage Provider As the Target](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_DATASPHERE/c8a54ee704e94e15926551293243fd1d/43d93a27150a4a218e3df14e3abdf456.html).  

### 2024-01-17 · Connection Type <span class="ph uicontrol">Microsoft SQL Server</span> Supports Version 2022

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.2 · SAP-ID 590834219*

You can now create Microsoft SQL Server connections with the version Microsoft SQL Server 2022 to connect to databases with this version.See [Microsoft SQL Server Connections](a13c8abb328f45be891599c9cc76fb91.html).  

### 2024-01-09 · Scheduled Task Run Frequency Settings

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590833771*

When scheduling a new task in the Data Integration Monitor, or elsewhere in SAP Datasphere, users can now specify a frequency for scheduled runs in minutes, in addition to hourly, daily, weekly, or monthly intervals.See [Schedule a Data Integration Task (Simple Schedule)](7c11059ed3314e1fb753736b7867512c.html).  

### 2024-01-09 · Remote Tables Now Consume Entities With Input Parameters

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590833920*

If your remote table consumes parameterized Hana SQL views using SAP HANA smart data access as source, the parameters are displayed with their properties in the table editor.See [Review and Edit Imported Table Properties](75cea7b1cbeb4c9dba0ac13605c527b3.html)  

### 2024-01-09 · Package Field in Additional Editors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590833916*

When one or more packages is available in your space, modelers can now add further types of objects to a package directly from the relevant editor:  
- Data flow  
- Replication flow  
- Transformation flow  
- Task chain  
- Intelligent lookup  
See [Packages](a806c67ed11749c788142775d2cc2494.html).  

### 2024-01-09 · New Privilege <span class="ph uicontrol">Data Warehouse Runtime</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590834157*

The new privilege Data Warehouse Runtime with the Read permission allows users of the View Analyzer to download the generated SQL analyzer plan file.See [Privileges and Permissions](d7350c6823a14733a7a5727bad8371aa.html).  

### 2024-01-09 · Local Table with Delta Capture Enabled: Delete Fully-Processed Records Marked as Deleted

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590833915*

When you delete records in a local table that is delta capture enabled, records take the value "change type = deleted" but they are not physically deleted from the database to allow running flows that can transfer the deletion information to further targets. Once they have been fully processed, you can safely delete these records within a task chain or a using the Delete Data from Table in the Data Builder.See [Load or Delete Local Table Data](870401f211f94132909bd9f2fafd91b2.html) and [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html)  

### 2024-01-09 · Import of <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP BW∕4HANA</span> Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590833914*

The import of SAP BW∕4HANA models is now integrated in the Import Entities wizard, which can be accessed from the Data Builder and the Repository Explorer. With the import via the Import Entities wizard, you can now also import InfoProviders besides analytic queries.See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP BW∕4HANA or SAP BW Bridge](7bcd3218b4064b898ca367b47ff246e2.html).  

### 2024-01-09 · Generate SQL Analyzer Plan File

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590833769*

In addition to the data persistence simulation, the View Analyzer now offers the possibility to generate an SQL analyzer plan file that you can download for further analysis.See[Exploring Views with View Analyzer](8921e5acf2ad4c8a98073edae4c214c7.html).  

### 2024-01-09 · Find and Replace Local Table Data

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590834271*

You can now use the Data Editor to find and replace data in a local table. For more information, see [Maintain Local Table Data](4bd5e641be48409c8c79336df0c4a3c7.html).  

### 2024-01-09 · Delta Interval for Replication Flow Can Be Changed

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590833768*

You can now define the length of the delta interval for replication flows with load type Initial and Delta.  
See[Configure the Run Settings of a Replication Flow](3f5ba0c5ae3944c1b7279bb989a2a5b5.html) .  

### 2024-01-09 · Additional Views Available in the Monitoring Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2024.1 · SAP-ID 590834158*

You can now access the _SYS_BI schema views from the monitoring spaces. See [Configure Monitoring](9cd0691c44a74f2aa47b52f615f74433.html).

It is now possible to overwrite existing objects in SAP BW bridge.  

### 2023-11-28 · Semantic Onboarding

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.25 · SAP-ID 590833774*

### 2023-11-28 · QTM (Queued Task Manager) Runtime for Process Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.25 · SAP-ID 590834139*

Process chains are executed in the Queued Task Manager framework (i.e. in ODQ_TQ_JOB jobs) rather than in a separate background job per process.  

### 2023-11-28 · Privileges for Catalog Access Can Be Added to Custom Roles

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.25 · SAP-ID 590833685*

On the Roles page, you can now see the individual privileges for the standard Catalog User and Catalog Administrator roles. These privileges allow you to:  
- Read, create, and edit glossary and glossary terms, key performance indicators (KPIs), and tag hierarchies.  
- Add enrichments to asset metadata.  
- Manage and monitor source systems.  
Also, instead of assigning the standard application roles to users to access and manage the catalog, you can now include the individual privileges for accessing and managing the catalog to your custom roles.  
See [Standard Roles Delivered with SAP Datasphere](a50a51d80d5746c9b805a2aacbb7e4ee.html), [Privileges by App, Tool, Object, and Task](2d8b7d04dcae402f911d119437ce0a74.html), and [Create a Custom Role](862b88eed50244049d41361ba3290456.html)

- Data access control

When creating a variable for an analytic model, or an expression for a restricted measure, you now get a value help with possible values.  

### 2023-11-15 · System User Needed for Extracting Metadata from Source System

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.24 · SAP-ID 590833687*

When you create a connection to a source system, you must authenticate a user for the source system. The purpose of this user is for customizing the scope of objects that will be extracted from the source system to the catalog.  
Note  
For all connections to source systems created before version 2023.24, a user for the source system must be authenticated. If a user is not authenticated for the source system, automatic metadata extraction for that source system will stop. For more information, see SAP note [3344922](https://launchpad.support.sap.com/#/notes/3344922).  
See [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html).  

### 2023-11-15 · Setting Default Values for Input Parameters and Propagating Them

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.24 · SAP-ID 590834277*

You can now easily set or change a default value for input parameters with the assistance of the Value Help dialog. This dialog follows you along your workflow and allows you to change an input parameter default value while defining the parameter, previewing or consuming an object using the parameter, and also while modeling and previewing an analytic model. See [Create an Input Parameter in a Graphical View](53fa99ad58c04a1ba3bb87288756dabc.html), [Preview Data in an Analytic Model](9f1fa73a33424cbe9bac3064702c0dbd.html), [Creating a Graphical View](27efb479c4814252964d3fbc6ca2dfc3.html), and [Add a Fact to an Analytic Model](27075eeeba634f55be0b52e92cf88159.html).  

### 2023-11-15 · Monitoring Page Improvements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.24 · SAP-ID 590833686*

The Monitoring page improvements aim to provide you with a better experience when creating and monitoring connections to all your source systems. You can now do the following tasks:  
- Connect to more than three SAP Analytics Cloud tenants.  
- See an overview of all system types and their statuses in a single table.  
- See advanced systems details for a selected source systems:  
- The extraction summary of all data and analytic objects.  
- The extraction logs for automatic and manual synchronizations, where you can see which object failed to synchronize.  
See [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html).

### 2023-11-15 · Deprecation of Connection Type <span class="ph uicontrol">Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen1</span>

*Action: Required · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.24 · SAP-ID 590833775*

Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen1 will be retired on February 29, 2024. After this date, it might not be possible to access data with Gen1 connections.The corresponding SAP Datasphere connection type Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen1 is deprecated and may be removed in future releases.  
Action: After your Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen1 has been migrated to Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen2, recreate existing Gen1 connections with the Gen2 connection type and adapt your models and processes to the new connections.

The deployment state is now shown in the object status including changes to dependent objects. After a change in a dimension that requires redeployment of the analytic model, you can now deploy it from the Data Builder landing page. You can even select several analytic models and deploy them in one go.

In Tenant Configuration, you may now allocate blocks for premium outbound integration. See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).

You can now grant access to Application Function Library functions to your database users working in HDI containers on your SAP Datasphere tenant.See [Consume Space Objects in Your HDI Container](656eebc2ced14ec09afa455224fa9a98.html).

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.23 · SAP-ID 590834162*

Additional driver versions are supported for Amazon Redshift, Oracle, and Google BigQuery connections. We recommend to use the newest supported versions. See [Upload Third-Party ODBC Drivers (Required for Data Flows)](b9b5579054df48c39381d5b17286bf21.html).  

### 2023-10-31 · Manage Users and Scoped Roles via the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.23 · SAP-ID 590833631*

You can now manage users and scoped roles from the command line using the following commands:  
- datasphere users - See [Managing Users via the Command Line](72dc33a8f41944f78318138bc1a57307.html).  
- datasphere spaces users - see [Managing Space Users via the Command Line](f58c71c028b149088903193b4e1f06da.html).  
- datasphere scoped-roles - See [Managing Scoped Roles via the Command Line](85085a35a58a4589bc121fb94efc4876.html).  

### 2023-10-04 · No More Membership of Users in Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.21 · SAP-ID 590834312*

Users are now assigned to spaces via scoped roles, they are no more members of spaces. The Members area in a space page has been renamed to Users.See [Create a Space](bbd41b82ad4d4d9ba91341545f0b37e7.html) and [Control User Access to Your Space](9d59fe511ae644d98384897443054c16.html).  

### 2023-10-04 · New Flexible Space and Role Assignment for Users - Scoped Roles

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.21 · SAP-ID 590834163*

Now, each user can have different privileges in each of the spaces she is assigned to. For example, a user can be a viewer in space A, a modeler in space B and a space administrator in space C:  
Note  
Scoped roles and all related features will be rolled out to all tenants over the course of a number of versions. For more details, see SAP Note [3380409](https://launchpad.support.sap.com/#/notes/3380409).  
SAP Datasphere Roles Up to Version 2023.21: Each user was assigned one or more roles and could use all the privileges granted by these roles in all the spaces of which she was a member.  
SAP Datasphere Scoped Roles from Version 2023.21: Each assignment of a user to a space is performed via a scoped role which grants the privileges of a particular role to the user in the context of one or more spaces. By assigning the user to other spaces via other scoped roles, the administrator can provide the user exactly the privileges that she needs in each space. For example, Julia can be a modeler in space Sales Germany and Sales France and a viewer in space Europe Sales.  
- For SAP Datasphere tenants that were created before version 2023.21, the roles and user assignment to spaces have been converted so that users can continue to perform the same actions as before in their spaces. See [Automated Conversion to Scoped Roles](6f7c6dfa3cb249debb749c729025bae0.html).  
- Assigning users to spaces:  
- A DW Administrator can assign a role to multiple users in multiple spaces, in a single scoped role. See [Create a Scoped Role to Assign Privileges to Users in Spaces](b5c4e0b6c462414783ebbfc053815521.html).  
- A DW Space Administrator can assign and unassign users to their spaces and the changes are reflected in the scoped roles. See [Control User Access to Your Space](9d59fe511ae644d98384897443054c16.html).  
- New scoped roles vs global roles:  
- Each standard or custom role is now either a global role or a template for scoped roles.  
- A scoped role inherits a set of scoped privileges from a standard or custom role and grants these privileges to users for use in the assigned spaces.  
- A global role enables users assigned to it to perform actions that are tenant-wide (and not space-related), typically a role that enables to administrate the tenant. A global role includes global privileges. The DW Administrator, Catalog Administrator and Catalog User standard roles delivered with SAP Datasphere are global roles.  
- Privileges and permissions are now either global or scoped. See [Privileges and Permissions](d7350c6823a14733a7a5727bad8371aa.html).Note  
When you create a custom role: if you’re creating it to enable users to perform global actions, you should include only global privileges; if you’re creating it to enable users to perform space-related actions, you should include only scoped privileges. See [Create a Custom Role](862b88eed50244049d41361ba3290456.html).  
See [Managing Roles and Privileges](3740dacbc2794f33bb5d8d42216cc3bc.html) and [Standard Roles Delivered with SAP Datasphere](a50a51d80d5746c9b805a2aacbb7e4ee.html).  
- Standard roles become templates - The following standard roles that are delivered with SAP Datasphere become templates for creating customs roles, because they are space-related and can be used as templates for creating scoped roles: DW Space Administrator, DW Integrator, DW Modeler, DW Viewer, DW Consumer. Predefined scoped roles that are based on the roles mentioned above are also delivered with SAP Datasphere and inherit the privileges and permissions of the standard roles they are derived from. See [Standard Roles Delivered with SAP Datasphere](a50a51d80d5746c9b805a2aacbb7e4ee.html).  
- Main privileges and permissions changes in standard roles:  
- The DW Administrator role no longer has access to modeling apps (such as Data Builder).  
- The DW Space Administrator role can manage the users in their space now by using the new privilege Scoped User Role Assignment.  
- No more membership of users in spaces - Users are now assigned to spaces via scoped roles, they are no more members of spaces. The Members area in a space page has been renamed to Users. See [Create a Space](bbd41b82ad4d4d9ba91341545f0b37e7.html) and [Control User Access to Your Space](9d59fe511ae644d98384897443054c16.html).  
- Assigning users to a role via SAML attributes - You can now assign users to a role via SAML attributes. If a user is assigned to a scoped role, the user is automatically assigned to all the spaces included in the scoped roles. See [Assign Users to a Role Using SAML Attributes](33157115bac34f9b8e96dc0797969387.html).  

### 2023-10-04 · New Customizable Homepage

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.21 · SAP-ID 590833999*

The new SAP Datasphere homepage gives you access to recent objects, quick actions, and blog posts. You can choose to show, hide, and reorder cards to suit your needs.  
See [The SAP Datasphere Homepage](2d85c8b63c454df585099fc93e4f8baf.html).  

### 2023-10-04 · Hierarchy with Directory

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.21 · SAP-ID 590833926*

You can now model hierarchies with directories. These types of hierarchy entities can include nodes from multiple dimensions (for example, country, cost center group, and cost center) and are commonly imported from SAP S/4HANA Cloud and SAP BW systems.  
See [Create a Hierarchy with Directory](36c39eee184c485a80ebce9d0fec49ec.html)  

### 2023-10-04 · DW Administrators Can Create Spaces via Content Network Packages

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.21 · SAP-ID 590834161*

Users with the DW Administrator role (or equivalent privileges) can now create spaces by importing packages via the Content Network or Transport apps.See [Importing Content from Another Tenant](b607a12931d74c4a93506ea64c55ab4e.html) and [Importing SAP and Partner Business Content from the Content Network](400078d689bf4454b3fc977a4e201c2f.html).  

### 2023-09-20 · Transformation Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590833778*

You can now create transformation flows to to load data from one or more source tables, apply transformations (such as a join), and output the result in a target table. You can load a full set of data from one or more source tables to a target table. You can also load delta changes (including deleted records) from one source table to a target table.  
See [Creating a Transformation Flow](f7161e6c20204672ac4a6d90c81762e4.html).  

### 2023-09-20 · Reduce or delete the data lake storage used by your tenant

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590834164*

In Tenant Configuration, you can now delete your data lake storage in order to reduce or remove the storage used by your tenant.  

### 2023-09-20 · OpenSQL Access

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590833826*

Allow users to consume data products directly from the Data Marketplace using OpenSQL access.  
See [Enabling OpenSQL Access](afae5a2de300472794c69b58ce670b2e.html).  

### 2023-09-20 · New Guide <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Security Recommendations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590833987*

The SAP Datasphere Security Recommendations guide is now available. It provides a list with our recommendations for the configuration of SAP Datasphere to help you meet your compliance goals and secure your business.See [SAP Datasphere Security Recommendations](4b14c62a62eb4c83bb74e33f5a646aa5.html).  

### 2023-09-20 · Descriptions for Entries in IP Allowlists

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590833632*

To better understand your IP entries in the Trusted IPs and Trusted Cloud Connector IPs allowlists in  System     Configuration, you can now add a description for each IP entry.See: [Manage IP Allowlist](a3c214514ef94e899459f68f4c1e2a23.html)  

### 2023-09-20 · Data Sharing Cockpit REST APIs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590833821*

You can use the Data Sharing Cockpit REST APIs to manage data provider tasks.  
See [Managing the Data Marketplace via the REST API](59768ad8c8054d2db92fdf545b0a2485.html).  

### 2023-09-20 · Capturing Delta Changes in Your local table

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590834278*

In the Data Builder, when creating a local table, you can enable the new setting Delta Capture to track later changes in your local table. Local tables can be reused as source or target objects in many SAP Datasphere objects. See [Capturing Delta Changes in Your Local Table](154bdffb35814d5481d1f6de143a6b9e.html)  

### 2023-09-20 · <span class="ph uicontrol">MDS Version</span> Removed from <span class="ph uicontrol">About</span> Dialog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590833633*

The information about the SAP HANA multi-dimensional services (MDS) version is removed from the More section of the  System    About dialog. The information about the Database Release now also applies to the MDS version.  

### 2023-09-20 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Command Line Interface: Client Module Improvements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.20 · SAP-ID 590834000*

- The list of available options per command has been cleaned up: only command specific options are listed now.  
- A new config command regroups previously available commands like host, cache etc…  
- You can now log in to multiple tenants in parallel.  
For more information, see [Accessing SAP Datasphere via the Command Line](3f9a42ccde6b4b6aba121e2aab79c36d.html).  

### 2023-09-07 · Use <span class="ph uicontrol">Rate Column Suitability</span> to Identify the Most Suitable Columns to Partion Your Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.19 · SAP-ID 590833779*

While defining partitions when persisting your views, switch on the option Rate Column Suitability to get information on column suitability for partitioning: The columns are ranked so that you can quickly identify the most suitable columns and additional information is provided about important factors that influence the suitability for each column to help you with your choice.See [Creating Partitions for Your Persisted Views](9b1b595054c34da5b67ac07595093f82.html).  

### 2023-09-07 · Package Modeling

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.19 · SAP-ID 590833925*

Users with space administrator privileges can create packages to model groups of related objects for transport between tenants. Modelers can add objects to packages via the Package field, which appears in editors when a package is created in their space. Once a package is complete and validated, the space administrator can export it to the Content Network. The structure of your package is preserved and, as the objects it contains evolve, you can easily export updated versions of it. See [Packages](a806c67ed11749c788142775d2cc2494.html) and [Creating Packages to Export](24aba84ceeb3416881736f70f02e3a0a.html).  

### 2023-09-07 · Intelligent Lookup: Rule-Level Filtering of Input and Lookup Records

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Preparation · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.19 · SAP-ID 590833548*

You can now define filter conditions to restrict the input and/or lookup records that will be considered for matching in any individual rule.See [Create an Exact Match Rule](897d26c3ffe24efaa27cd0d7262a78ad.html) and [Create a Fuzzy Match Rule](b063518d7a684051b66cc6d9681c8753.html).  

### 2023-09-07 · Change the capacity units allocated to Data Integration applications

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.19 · SAP-ID 590834165*

In Tenant Configuration, you may now modify the capacity units allocated to Data Integration applications. See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2023-08-23 · Use OAuth 2.0 Authentication for <span class="ph uicontrol">Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen2</span> Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833780*

You can now use authentication type OAuth 2.0 with Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen2 Connections supporting authentication with client ID and client secret, client ID and X.509 certificate, and username and password.  
See [Microsoft Azure Data Lake Storage Gen2 Connections](cd06b3c5ab5147c0905e3fa8abd13eb1.html).  

### 2023-08-23 · Required Privilege to Maintain Data Provider Profiles

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590834241*

To create a new data provider profile, or edit an existing one, you must have the Space > Update privilege assigned to your role.  

### 2023-08-23 · Perform Actions on Multiple Objects

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833782*

In the Data Integration Monitor, you can select multiple objects to perform the same action, whenever it’s applicable. You can also pause, resume, delete and take the ownership of multiple schedules.  

### 2023-08-23 · Partition Your Local Table

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833928*

You can now create partitions based on one column of your local table to break your data down into smaller tables, and better manage tables with large volume of data.See [Partitioning Local Tables](03191f36e9144b2aaa47b8c9eea039c1.html).  

### 2023-08-23 · Package Explorer View

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833570*

In the BW Modeling tools, a new default view is available that displays the ABAP packages with the BW objects assigned to them.  
You can also create objects for the selected package here. The package is then preselected for these objects.  
This assignment is important in SAP BW bridge because it allows you to recognize the relationship to the software component which is the basis for transport.  

### 2023-08-23 · Loading Data into DataStore Objects and InfoObjects with Write Interface

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833572*

For scenarios with a high frequency of data loading, there is now a new way to load data into the corresonding object, the messaging request.  
To to use this option, you have to include the new process type PUSH_MSGR, Close messaging request, in your process chain  

### 2023-08-23 · Improved Business Builder Object URLs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833465*

The URLs of Business Builder objects now include the technical name of the object. Old bookmarks are deprecated and should be updated, but will continue to work for the time being.  

### 2023-08-23 · Creating Source Systems of Type ODP (SOAP/HTTP)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833569*

As an administrator, you want to create a connection to a source system of type ODP in order to extract data from an ODP SOAP (HTTP) source.  

### 2023-08-23 · Anonymizing Data Using the Data Protection Workbench

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833571*

New features are now available for anonymizing data records.  
- Propagation of updates of anonymized data  
- Anonymization of data in data mart DataStore objects  
- New formula editor for definition of anonymization rules  
- New function for simulating anonymization  
See [Anonymizing Data Using the Data Protection Workbench](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/214922c4dd93476394f961fde8ad97fc.html?locale=en-US) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2023-08-23 · Add <span class="ph uicontrol">Details</span> Tab for the Asset Details Page

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590834177*

When viewing the information for an asset, a new Details tab is available. This tab shows detailed metadata about the asset, which can include columns, attributes, measures, dimensions, and properties specific to each data object.  
See [Accessing Catalog Assets](dc061a23484241b1b791f5540b1f38e3.html).  
Note  
For existing connections to source systems with assets that have already been extracted to the catalog, you must manually resynchronize the systems to be able to see metadata in the Details tab.  
- In the side navigation area, choose  (Catalog & Marketplace)   (Metadata Extractions).  
- For each existing source system in the Systems Connection table, click  (Synchronize).  
After the resynchronization finishes, you can select an asset to see if metadata in the Details tab is available.  
See [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html).  

### 2023-08-23 · Ability to Add KPI and Term Relationships to the Detailed Metadata for the an Asset

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.18 · SAP-ID 590833688*

Catalog administrators can add term and KPI relationships to the detailed metadata (columns, attributes, measures, etc.) for an asset. For example, when reviewing the Details tab of an asset, you can create relationships between a specific measure and a term or KPI. After the detailed metadata of the asset is enriched, the catalog user can review the relationships when they evaluate the asset.  
See [Enriching and Managing Catalog Assets](7ed60a094f2a464da6a8d75e5bfed9d5.html) or [Accessing Catalog Assets](dc061a23484241b1b791f5540b1f38e3.html).  

### 2023-08-09 · Use Input Parameters for Calculated Columns in Data Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.17 · SAP-ID 590833781*

In data flows, you can now use input parameters in the expression for calculating a column.  
See [Create an Input Parameter in a Data Flow](a6fb3e70da0a42b2aa4a7ac7b73f5ae1.html).  

### 2023-08-09 · Support User with DW Administrator Role

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.17 · SAP-ID 590833634*

If you have allowed SAP Product support to generate support users based on incidents, the process of requesting and generating a personalized support user now supports creating the support user with the DW Administrator role.  
See [Request Help from SAP Technical Support](831a97714dfe4301918afece5a3b380b.html).  

### 2023-08-09 · Statistics Information on Remote Queries Involved While Persisting Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.17 · SAP-ID 590834223*

In the View Persistency Monitor – Details screen, execution logs have been enhanced with statistics information related to remote queries. You can use this information and navigate directly to the Remote Query Monitor to analyze what’s happened with the remote statements.See [Persisting and Monitoring Views](9af04c990f294fd28c00f46763dd8b0d.html).  

### 2023-08-09 · Saving SQL Views with Errors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.17 · SAP-ID 590833927*

You can save a SQL View even if it contains errors so as to keep your changes for future corrections. Save your view as usual and then click Save Anyway.  

### 2023-08-09 · Guide has been removed

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.17 · SAP-ID 590834001*

The contents of the guide Using SAP Datasphere, SAP BW Bridge in SAP Datasphere are now included in the guide Getting Started With SAP Datasphere, SAP BW Bridge and the topic Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP BW Bridge.  

### 2023-08-09 · Constant Selection

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.17 · SAP-ID 590834279*

For restricted measures in the analytic model, you can now enable constant selection.  
See [Create a Restricted Measure](bfb43ddc98bc4162bc4196faf3e5d8c6.html).  

### 2023-07-20 · Internal Data Marketplace

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.16 · SAP-ID 590833827*

Use the Internal Data Marketplace to enable all the users of a tenant to acquire data products internally. Indeed, create Internal Data Marketplace contexts to build up an internal data exchange with consumers from the same tenant, or from a list of specified tenants.  
See [Using Contexts to Realize Public, Private, and Internal Offers](c936499e7ef045b09139723fd0ac9e09.html).  

### 2023-07-20 · Ability to Connect Multiple <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Tenants to the Catalog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.16 · SAP-ID 590833689*

You can connect the local SAP Datasphere tenant and multiple external SAP Datasphere tenants to a single catalog.  
See [Connecting to SAP Datasphere Systems](16d4e71b8dd545b0be1b956c26e652cd.html).  

### 2023-07-13 · Support for Window Functions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.15 · SAP-ID 590834282*

You can now use SAP HANA Cloud window functions such as RANK and WEIGHTED_AVG, which allow you to perform analytic operations over a set of input rows, in the SQL and graphical view editors.See [SQL Functions Reference](6d624a1956234d818d0bfdc77cbd0e09.html).  

### 2023-07-13 · Informative Web Page Titles

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.15 · SAP-ID 590834002*

SAP Datasphere page titles now give you clear information about the object and application you are working in. You can easily distinguish between open browser tabs, quickly navigate your browser history, and have more meaningful bookmarks.  

### 2023-07-13 · Data Preview of Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.15 · SAP-ID 590834280*

The data preview that is integrated in the editor of the analytic model has been improved:  
- There are now more filter options  
- The filter options are now available under Settings  
- Values are formatted according to the data type of the columns.  

### 2023-07-13 · Connection Type Google BigQuery Supports Data Flows and Regions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.15 · SAP-ID 590834224*

We've enhanced the Google BigQuery connection type:  
- You can now use the connection type to add source objects to data flows. To use the connection for data flows, it is required to upload an ODBC driver. To enable an existing connection for data flows, you need to edit the connection and re-enter your credentials.  
- You can now enter an additional region to access BigQuery datasets, for example EU, us-west1, or asia-east1.  
See [Google BigQuery Connections](30ed77de13864368bdc596099b37ed70.html).  

### 2023-06-28 · Usability Improvements to Enhance Your Catalog Experience

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.14 · SAP-ID 590833690*

To help catalog administrators better manage the assets, improvements have been made in how you maintain glossary terms, tags, and KPIs, and how you apply them to assets. There are also improvements from the main catalog page to access other areas of the catalog. To help catalog users, improvements have been made to how property information in the asset details is organized. To help both users, performance has improved with caching.  
See:  
- [Manage Tag Relationships for Assets](bb608dd2a4dc402889351c4303a173a4.html)

- [Accessing Catalog Assets](dc061a23484241b1b791f5540b1f38e3.html)  
- [Using the Business Glossary](224f6bf5e5054c0697cbf26ff770358d.html)  
- [Create and Manage a Glossary](e40310c9781848bfad031cce7792f394.html)  
- [Create and Manage a Glossary Category](e9ce9f6e36384517b531f6e9cf88f498.html)  
- [Create and Manage Glossary Terms](193336a4eba94c978c08fd2cee625a3e.html)  
- [Create and Manage Key Performance Indicators](ca4f04da3afa4f82b85a0835fceb444b.html)  
- [Create and Manage Key Performance Indicator Categories](bc3b630394494381b83bf406b262a598.html)  
- [Define the Key Performance Indicator Template](b3ead0d4944c40ddbdb1ebae239cc69f.html)  
- [Publishing Content to the Catalog](f31d2d97afb0482a874acb0ec61465fe.html)  

### 2023-06-28 · New Name for the <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Command Line Interface

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.14 · SAP-ID 590834003*

The SAP Datasphere command line interface module has been renamed from dwc to datasphere. The command dwc will be decommissioned at the end of 2023: please use the new datasphere command instead. For more information, see [Accessing SAP Datasphere via the Command Line](3f9a42ccde6b4b6aba121e2aab79c36d.html).  

### 2023-06-28 · Changed SAML Authentication for SAP SuccessFactors Connection Type

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.14 · SAP-ID 590834225*

The previous method to use the /oauth/idp API to generate and provide SAML assertions has been deprecated as it is considered unsafe. You now directly enter the SAML assertion when creating or editing a connection.  
For new connections, in the OAuth 2.0 section of the connection configuration, the properties related to the previous method (OAuth API Endpoint and OAuth User ID) have been removed. In the Credentials section, the Client Secret property has been replaced by the new property SAML Assertion where you can add a valid SAML assertion.  
For existing connections, you can set the Provide SAML Assertion property in the OAuth 2.0 section to true to switch to the new method and then enter the SAML assertion in the Credentials section.  
See:  
- [Deprecation of OAuth IdP API /oauth/idp](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_SUCCESSFACTORS_RELEASE_INFORMATION/8e0d540f96474717bbf18df51e54e522/db7ccbbbc7a54e929a305ff92d12241c.html) in the SAP SuccessFactors Release Information documentation  
- [SAP SuccessFactors Connections](39df02030d4b411487bacecf9afea4e8.html)  

### 2023-06-15 · Support for Intelligent Lookups in Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590834281*

You can now include intelligent lookups in task chains, in addition to previously allowed remote table replication, view persistency, and data flow objects. Like other objects, intelligent lookups can be included anywhere within the order of tasks defined in a task chain.See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html).  

### 2023-06-15 · Stop Running Statements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590834167*

A database analysis user can now stop a statement by running a database procedure in SAP HANA Database Explorer (for example when a long-running statement causes performance issues).  
See [Stop a Running Statement With a Database Analysis User](0cf11ede362b4f4186c459097464943d.html).  

### 2023-06-15 · New <span class="ph uicontrol">Fact</span> Semantic Usage (and Deprecation of <span class="ph uicontrol">Analytical Dataset</span>)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590833930*

A new Fact semantic usage is available, and the Analytical Dataset semantic usage is deprecated.The preferred way to expose data to SAP Analytics Cloud is now to identify your measures in a table or view with a semantic usage of Fact and then to use this fact in one or more analytic models, each of which can be consumed by one or more stories (see [Creating an Analytic Model](e5fbe9e2cb93484dab8b1963145e565f.html)).  
This new workflow provides the following benefits: Feature Analytical Dataset Fact + Analytic Model Aggregations Standard aggregations only, such as SUM, COUNT. Standard and post-aggregation measures (calculated measures, restricted measures, and count distinct measures), including support for exception aggregations.Move post-aggregation measures from your SAP Analytics Cloud stories into your analytic models to benefit from real-time previewing of results and to promote re-use.  
Measures and attribute selection All measures and attributes and first-level dimension always exposed to SAP Analytics Cloud. More remote dimensions cannot be included. Model as many measures, attributes, and associations to dimensions as appropriate in a fact, and then choose to expose only those that you need in each analytic model. You can select attributes from any dimension that is associated with the fact, whether it is a first-level dimension (directly associated with the fact) or any dimension that can be reached by following further associations. Data viewer Relational data viewer shows lists of records Rich analytic viewer, based on the SAP Analytics Cloud Data Analyzer, supports measure and attribute selection, filtering and pivoting, and hierarchy support​.  
See [Create a Fact to Contain Measurable Data](30089bd2aa754ab996a62cf5842ae60a.html) and [Analytical Datasets (Deprecated)](70dab71147834c20b98ead017d7c5d8a.html).  

### 2023-06-15 · Modify or Duplicate Remote Tables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590833931*

From the table editor of the Data Builder, you can now change the remote table connection, the remote table source object, or create a copy of an existing table with the Save As option. See [Modify or Duplicate Remote Tables](8c3632f28dc94e068dbdab8fe9bbeeb9.html).  

### 2023-06-15 · Locked Partitions and Changes in the Partition Definition

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590833783*

If you change the partition definition, you can lock the unchanged partitions to avoid unnecessary data loads and ensure that only the new defined partitions are updated.See [Creating Partitions for Your Persisted Views](9b1b595054c34da5b67ac07595093f82.html).  

### 2023-06-15 · Get Assistance when Inserting Values in the Graphical View Expression Editor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590833929*

In a graphical view, when using a filter, calculated column, or, in some cases, an aggregation node, you can get help inserting values in a SQL expression in the expression editor.  
See:  
- [Create a Calculated Column in a Graphical View](3897f480b9404e6f82de9bba410f17c0.html)  
- [Filter Data in a Graphical View](6f6fa18c7c9444da8ca62d76656b7b1a.html)  
- [Aggregate Data in a Graphical View](7733250f9d2a46b690a5288634d269f0.html)  

### 2023-06-15 · Display Additional Information on MDS queries

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590834166*

To further analyze SAP HANA multi-dimensional services (MDS) queries in the System Monitor, you can now enable the tracing of MDS information.See [Configure Monitoring](9cd0691c44a74f2aa47b52f615f74433.html) and [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2023-06-15 · Compound Keys

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590833468*

The definition of compound keys is now better supported by the UI.  
See [Define a Key](9748bab194d94b9085eae7a139b55097.html).  

### 2023-06-15 · Amazon Simple Storage Service Connection (S3) Supports Server Side Encryption and Restricted Access by Assuming a Role

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590834226*

To improve security when connecting to your Amazon S3 buckets, the connection configuration has been enhanced:  
- You can now enable server side encryption to use encrypted objects in data flows, optionally using an AWS Key Management Service key for encryption.  
- You can now assume a role to use temporary security credentials and restrict access to your Amazon S3 buckets based on an Identity and Access Management role (IAM role).  
See [Amazon Simple Storage Service Connections](a7b660a0a4ef4a4fbee57b44f5b2147d.html).  

### 2023-06-15 · "Analytical Dataset" Business Entities Renamed to "Fact"

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.13 · SAP-ID 590833467*

"Analytical Dataset" business entities are now renamed to "Facts" to align with the new "Fact" semantic usage available for Data Builder entities.  
See :  
- [Creating a Business Entity](c912cdc1537d4efbb24b08327ea68918.html)  
- [Create a Fact to Contain Measurable Data](30089bd2aa754ab996a62cf5842ae60a.html)  
- [Analytical Datasets (Deprecated)](70dab71147834c20b98ead017d7c5d8a.html)  

### 2023-05-30 · Transport Business Builder Objects

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.12 · SAP-ID 590833470*

You can now export and import business entities, fact models, consumption models (including perspectives), and authorization scenarios via the  (Transport) app.  
See [Transporting Content Between Tenants](df12666cf98e41248ef2251c564b0166.html).  

### 2023-05-30 · Selecting Languages for Importing Texts

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.12 · SAP-ID 590833574*

In the SAP Datasphere, SAP BW Bridge Cockpit, you can now use the Data Load Languages - Maintain app to select which of the languages available in the cloud you want the texts (master data texts) to be imported in.  
See [Apps at a Glance](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/65fc810abf0549ee861b0d2c7b73ada2.html?version=latest) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2023-05-30 · Prevent Overloading the Database - Admission Control Rejection Threshold Enabled

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.12 · SAP-ID 590833636*

To prevent overloading the SAP HANA database, the parameter ADMISSION CONTROL REJECT CPU THRESHOLD is now part of the default configuration (in the Workload Configuration area of a space) with a threshold value set to 99%.  
Note  
This parameter cannot be changed, and its threshold value applies to all spaces – including spaces created before this version.  
See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  

### 2023-05-30 · Overview of Queries Available for Import

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.12 · SAP-ID 590833575*

In the SAP Datasphere, SAP BW Bridge Cockpit, you can now use the Query app to obtain an overview of all analytical queries available for import.  
See [Apps at a Glance](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/65fc810abf0549ee861b0d2c7b73ada2.html?version=latest) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2023-05-30 · Metadata Extraction of Predictive Scenarios and Data Actions from <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Analytics Cloud</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.12 · SAP-ID 590834178*

Predictive scenarios and data actions can now be extracted from SAP Analytics Cloud tenants and added to the catalog. Note  
You can take advantage of this enhancement with the release of SAP Analytics Cloud Q3 2023.  
See [Connecting to SAP Analytics Cloud Systems](27a4f9620761458dbc60cf9cf853989d.html).  

### 2023-05-30 · Import Queries as Analytic Models

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.12 · SAP-ID 590833466*

The SAP BW∕4HANA import wizard now creates an analytic model for each query imported and provides an option to control whether Business Builder objects are created.  
See [Importing SAP BW∕4HANA Models](a3d4a2f91bea4810ba8839ff73577dac.html).  

### 2023-05-30 · Enhancement of Monitoring View TASK_LOGS_V_EXT

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.12 · SAP-ID 590833635*

The monitoring view TASK_LOGS_V_EXT now tracks resource consumption key figures, and collect task chains information.See [Working with SAP HANA Monitoring Views](4ab45090c5684ebf8765757a1dfc4e5d.html)  

### 2023-05-30 · Assigning IDs to Source Systems

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.12 · SAP-ID 590833573*

In the SAP Datasphere, SAP BW Bridge Cockpit, you can now use the Source Systems – Maintain IDs app to define source system IDs for source systems or groups of source systems.  
See [Using Source System IDs](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/ccf50027ead14dedab2c37df83c97369.html?version=latest) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2023-05-16 · New <span class="ph uicontrol">Image URL</span> Semantic Type

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.11 · SAP-ID 590833933*

You can now use the Image URL semantic type to identify a dimension attribute as containing a URL pointing to an image file. The image will be displayed when you show the attribute in an SAP Analytics Cloud story.See [Specify Semantic Types for Measures and Attributes](f7272c0e8be34ce782d04304580c0243.html).  

### 2023-05-16 · Larger Configuration Available for Your Tenant in Certain Regions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.11 · SAP-ID 590833637*

If you’re using a tenant in regions EU10 and US10 for the hyperscaler Amazon Web Services (AWS), you can now choose a larger memory size for your tenant.  
See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2023-05-16 · Generic OData Connection Type Supports Custom HTTP Headers and Selection of OAuth2 Token Request Content Type

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.11 · SAP-ID 590833784*

The configuration of Generic OData connections has been enhanced:  
- You can now specify custom HTTP headers to add them to the OData request.  
- You can now specify the content type of the OAuth2 token request. It can be either URL Encoded or JSON.  
See [Generic OData Connections](5d36f1aae68a4e59989c424a66d948c9.html).  

### 2023-05-16 · Currency Conversion in Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.11 · SAP-ID 590833932*

You can now create currency conversion measures in the analytic model.  

### 2023-05-03 · Multi-Select Objects in the Import Entities Wizard

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.10 · SAP-ID 590833788*

You can now select multiple objects to import simultaneously via the Import Entities wizard.See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP S/4HANA](845fedbd28574aa8b84239df848936f6.html) and[Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP BW∕4HANA or SAP BW Bridge](7bcd3218b4064b898ca367b47ff246e2.html) .  

### 2023-05-03 · Import Semantically-Rich Entities from <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP S/4HANA</span> On-Premise Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.10 · SAP-ID 590833789*

You can now import semantically-rich entities from SAP S/4HANA On-Premise v1610 or higher connections via the Import Entities wizard.See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP S/4HANA](845fedbd28574aa8b84239df848936f6.html).  

### 2023-05-03 · Create Input Parameters for Data Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.10 · SAP-ID 590833785*

You can define input parameters in data flows and use them as a filter condition in projection operators.  
At runtime, the user is prompted to enter a value. You can keep the default value for all parameters or define new values as filter criteria.  
See [Create an Input Parameter in a Data Flow](a6fb3e70da0a42b2aa4a7ac7b73f5ae1.html).  

### 2023-05-03 · Allow Support User Creation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.10 · SAP-ID 590834168*

You can now allow SAP Product Support to generate support users based on SAP support incidents.  
With the new setting, you don't need to create a support user for each incident separately. To improve security, the user is a personalized user which is enabled for multi-factor authentication and will be deleted after the incident has been solved.  
See [Request Help from SAP Technical Support](831a97714dfe4301918afece5a3b380b.html).  

### 2023-04-24 · Show Primary Key Column Field Propagation in Data Flow

*Action: Required · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.9 · SAP-ID 590833786*

You can now see the active primary key column with a new key icon shown in the properties panel and how it is propagated from source to target table.  
A notification informs you if the source table has been modified and shows which columns have been set or removed.  
See [Columns](8f0f40df2ed34035b8b5837897205ee6.html).  
To apply new features to existing data flows, sync or open to update new primary key columns. For remote sources refresh the metadata.  

### 2023-04-24 · Create Parallel Task Branches in Task Chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.9 · SAP-ID 590833787*

In addition to linear task chains in which one task is executed after another, you can also create task chains in which individual tasks are run in parallel and continuation of the entire task chain run depends on whether ANY or ALL parallel tasks are completed successfully.See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html).  

### 2023-04-24 · Analytic Model: Compounded Keys

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.9 · SAP-ID 590834283*

In the editor of the analytic model, associations containing compounded keys are now available. When you add an associated dimension that uses a compounded key, the key attributes are added automatically.  

### 2023-04-06 · Update Perspectives for Transport

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.8 · SAP-ID 590833469*

Older perspectives use internal naming conventions for dimensions that are not stable when transporting between tenants.  
You can now update these perspectives to make them safe for transport.  
See [Define Perspectives](ce26fd3da31b414f9482292d3969340a.html).  

### 2023-04-06 · Provide Feedback on Documentation Using the New Collaboration Process

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.8 · SAP-ID 590834004*

The documentation process becomes more collaborative, by inviting you to provide feedbacks and contributions to our content through GitHub. The collaboration process is available for the following documentation sets:  
- [Acquiring, Preparing, and Modeling Data with SAP Datasphere](b4a5d02cefdf45478e7376860c985202.html)  
- [Administering SAP Datasphere](70ee87c19d3b47459ac7f2c8d4fea48a.html)  
- [Integrating Data and Managing Spaces in SAP Datasphere](8f98d3c917f94452bafe288055b60b35.html)  
For more information, see this [Blog](https://blogs.sap.com/2023/03/28/open-documentation-initiative-for-sap-datasphere-provide-feedback-and-help-us-improve-the-documentation/)  

### 2023-04-06 · Pausing Scheduled Tasks in the Edit Schedule dialog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.8 · SAP-ID 590833791*

Using options available for creating, editing, and removing task scheduling, you can now suspend or pause scheduled data integration tasks such as remote table replication, persisting views, data flow and task chain execution.  
After pausing a scheduled task, future scheduled runs will also not be executed, however, the defined schedule for those runs is still saved. So, when you resume a paused task, execution of future scheduled task runs will also resume.
