See [Pause or Resume a Scheduled Task](5eb55cbcaa8241f5a488d16036e78e15.html).  

### 2023-04-06 · New Define Partitions Wizard Can Suggest Suitable Partitioning Columns

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.8 · SAP-ID 590833790*

The new Define Partitions wizard, available in the View Persistency Monitor, can analyze your view and suggest suitable columns on which to partition your data.See [Creating Partitions for Your Persisted Views](9b1b595054c34da5b67ac07595093f82.html).  

### 2023-03-22 · Execute <span class="ph uicontrol">View Analyzer</span> with Memory Consumption Checks

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.7 · SAP-ID 590833792*

The View Analyzer has been enhanced with some improvements:  
- A smoother navigation within the results thanks to a new user interface, and a graphical dependency graph, that allows you to understand the data provenance,  
- The possibility to start a view persistency simulation for one or several views of your data model, with the ability to check how much memory would be consuming and the number of records to be persisted.  
See [Exploring Views with View Analyzer](8921e5acf2ad4c8a98073edae4c214c7.html).  

### 2023-03-22 · Ensure That Your Data Replicated in Real-time are Accurate and Complete

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.7 · SAP-ID 590833793*

In the Remote Table Monitors, two new columns have been added to help you to quickly identify issues with your data replication in real-time:  
- Latest Change (Source) will help you to identify performance issues with your data replication from the source system to SAP Data Warehouse Cloud to ensure that your data is always up-to-date.  
- Number of Records will help you to identify transport errors with your data replication to ensure that your data is fully copied into SAP Data Warehouse Cloud.  
See: [Monitoring Remote Tables](4dd95d7bff1f48b399c8b55dbdd34b9e.html).  

### 2023-03-08 · Tenant Configuration Page Includes Data Integration and Catalog Blocks

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590833639*

Your tenant configuration now includes blocks designated for Data Integration applications, as well as Catalog assets, activities and metadata. The size of the Data Integration and Catalog blocks cannot be modified.  
See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2023-03-08 · Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590834228*

You can now create a replication flow to load multiple data assets from one source into one target in a fast and easy way.  
Possible data sources are, for example, SAP HANA, SAP HANA Cloud, or SAP S/4HANA. Possible targets are the local repository (SAP Datasphere); SAP HANA Cloud; SAP HANA; or SAP HANA Cloud, data lake.  
For more information about available connection types, see [Integrating Data via Connections](eb85e157ab654152bd68a8714036e463.html).  
You can either copy all relevant data once (initial load) or choose initial and delta, where the system does an initial load, then checks for source data changes once every 60 minutes and replicates the changes to the target.  
You can define filters and mapping to delimit the scope of the replication flow.  
See [Creating a Replication Flow](25e2bd7a70d44ac5b05e844f9e913471.html).  

### 2023-03-08 · Prevent Users From Replicating Remote Tables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590833794*

While creating or updating an SAP HANA connection, you can now choose between two options:  
- Remote Only: It prevents users to replicate remote tables.  
- Remote and Replication: It allows users to replicate remote tables whenever they need to do it.  
See:  
- [Remote Tables in Data Access Remote Only Vs Data Access Remote and Replication](9b9db1490df6452a843f5081581aa5a3.html)  
- [SAP HANA Connections](e6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9.html#loioe6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9)  

### 2023-03-08 · New Catalog Administrator and Catalog User Roles

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590833638*

Two new roles added for the catalog:  
- Catalog Administrator: Allows users set up and implement the catalog in SAP Datasphere.  
- Catalog User: Allows users (for example, data modelers) to look for assets and allows users to view it in full or use as part of a data project to build something new.  
See [Standard Roles Delivered with SAP Datasphere](a50a51d80d5746c9b805a2aacbb7e4ee.html).  

### 2023-03-08 · Easy Navigation from Objects to Editors in the Data Layer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590833934*

In the data layer, you can now see that, in many cases, the names of data objects is turned into a link. Clicking one of these names allows you to open the object in its editor in a new tab and edit it without interrupting your work.  

### 2023-03-08 · Connection Types Supporting Replication Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590834227*

The following connection types now support adding source objects to replication flows:  
- [SAP S/4HANA Cloud Connections](a98e5ffdf47c44d9a845dca01a18bd82.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Connections](a49a1e3cc50f4af89711d8306bdd8f26.html)  
- [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html)  
- [Microsoft Azure SQL Database Connections](46343fc1c4544fa9a075d97f84d39826.html)  
The following connection type now supports adding target objects to replication flows:  
- [SAP HANA Cloud, Data Lake Files Connections](356e41e880e54255891b702d2afefeb3.html)  
The following connection type now supports adding source and target objects to replication flows:  
- [SAP HANA Connections](e6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9.html#loioe6b63f176d3640609adcf06297fb37e9)  
See [Integrating Data via Connections](eb85e157ab654152bd68a8714036e463.html).  

### 2023-03-08 · Analytic Model

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590834284*

In the Data Builder, a new object is available: the analytic model. Analytic models are now the preferred objects for making data ready for consumption in SAP Analytics Cloud. They allow you to create and define multi-dimensional models to provide data for analytical purposes to answer different business questions. Pre-defined measures, hierarchies, filters, variables, and associations provide flexible and simple navigation through the underlying data.  
See [Creating an Analytic Model](e5fbe9e2cb93484dab8b1963145e565f.html).  

### 2023-03-08 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Catalog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Cataloging · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590833691*

In this release, we’ve introduced the new SAP Datasphere catalog. The catalog provides an effective data governance strategy and is the central place to identify, enrich, publish, and promote the use of your organization’s trusted data and analytic assets. To get started, check out: [Governing and Publishing Data in the Catalog](de29b96a9438439682715a93212ae4f4.html).  
- Manage Source Systems  
You can use the Monitoring page to connect to data sources, monitor the data source extractions, and remove or restore data sources.  
See:  
- [Connecting Source Systems](0b5de87f256f466f95b46d4bcaa52640.html)  
- [Monitoring Metadata Extractions](6a03ef378f7b4dffaff5ef0e3aa7f5d5.html)  
- [Deleting Connections to Source Systems](d07b3e27cb1e46dea507c3d0cb7c43eb.html)  
- Glossary Terms, Tags, and KPIs  
You can manage glossary terms, tags, and KPIs and link them to assets.  
See:  
- [Using the Business Glossary](224f6bf5e5054c0697cbf26ff770358d.html)  
- [Enriching and Managing Catalog Assets](7ed60a094f2a464da6a8d75e5bfed9d5.html)  
- [Using Key Performance Indicators](3550f14d4cd74dfaa63ebcaa25b288c2.html)  
- Discover Content  
The catalog is the central place where you find data from your data sources.  
See:  
- [Searching for Data Products and Assets in the Catalog](10478251045b43e782fa15e0f3e113b0.html)  
- [Accessing Catalog Assets](dc061a23484241b1b791f5540b1f38e3.html)  

### 2023-03-08 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Data Warehouse Cloud</span> Is Now <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.6 · SAP-ID 590834005*

SAP Datasphere enables a business data fabric architecture that uniquely harmonizes mission-critical data across the organization, unleashing business experts to make the most impactful decisions. It combines previously discrete capabilities into a unified service for data integration, cataloging, semantic modeling, data warehousing, and virtualizing workloads across SAP and non-SAP data.  
SAP Datasphere includes the following major new features:  
- Replication Flows - Load data with both batch and delta functionality from your source systems with a single flow.  
- Catalog - Enable self-service discovery of data and analytic assets, glossaries and terms, and key performance indicators.  
- Analytic Models - Combine, filter, and enrich your analytical datasets and dimensions to produce business-ready models optimized for consumption in SAP Analytics Cloud.  
For details, see the relevant new feature items in this release.  
Our SAP Help documentation is now at [https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_DATASPHERE](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_DATASPHERE). Your existing bookmarks will be redirected.  

### 2023-02-22 · Utilizing SAP Fiori Launchpad Spaces and Pages in the SAP BW Bridge Cockpit

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590833578*

You can now structure the SAP BW Bridge cockpit user interface into spaces and pages. We thus deliver suitable spaces and pages for the business role templates that are required for the cockpit. Administrators can either use these spaces and pages or create own ones and then assign them to custom roles.  
See [First Steps in the Cockpit](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/4fb1a6d41b8247a0bf374e91c497fb21.html?version=latest) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2023-02-22 · Using the Command Line Interface for Data Marketplace for Data Provider and Data Product (Mass) Operations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590833828*

With this release you can use the command line interface, dwc, to create, read, update, and modify data providers and data products. You can create new data providers and data products in mass operations, or update properties, such as pricing information, publishing status, or context assignments of data products. It is also possible to list all data providers or data products you have access to.  
See:  
- [Managing Data Marketplace Data Products via the Command Line](34e88f7889734b1691fab7d8715c17fa.html)  
- [Managing Data Marketplace Data Providers via the Command Line](ca9f910607da4a9e8a2385f655edab95.html)  

### 2023-02-22 · New Guide for Command Line Interface

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590834306*

The new guide includes all information on accessing SAP Data Warehouse Cloud via the command line.  
You will find information on how to install and update the dwc command line interface and how to log in via an OAuth client. It lists all commands to perform various operations on the following entity types:  
- Spaces  
- Data providers (Data Marketplace entity)  
- Data products (Data Marketplace entity)  
The new guide includes also the definition file formats for the named entity types.  
See [Accessing SAP Datasphere via the Command Line](3f9a42ccde6b4b6aba121e2aab79c36d.html).  

### 2023-02-22 · Monitoring CPU and Memory Usage

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590834140*

As an administrator, you can now use four new key figures and a statistics record to find out how much usage there is in ABAP and the SAP HANA database when you execute a BW process.  
See:  
- [Monitoring CPU and Memory Usage](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/eba48b07e2764047b42916eca979cae1.html?version=latest)  
- [Average CPU Usage (ABAP)](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/822d528e19544c2293157a0fb6b99e8a.html?version=latest)  
- [Average Peak Memory (ABAP)](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/6639e270bb4d444ab3700a3c094e3d6b.html?version=latest)  
- [Average CPU Usage (Database)](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/7bb87d939e8e499e860a8bca09fe4eb2.html?version=latest)  
- [Average Peak Memory Usage (Database)](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/00ea7cabf2b24d2d995c2d6fa141e93a.html?version=latest)  
in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2023-02-22 · InfoObject With Write Interface

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590833576*

You can now use InfoObjects with a write interface. Texts and attributes are supported by the familiar master data types. The data in these InfoObjects can be moved to the inbound table using a tool such as Data Services or via SAP Cloud Platform Integration (CPI) by PUSH.  
See [InfoObject with Write Interface](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/18a38e808ce7438eb72700dd9f02d5d4.html?version=latest) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2023-02-22 · Data Products: New Live Access Delivery Mode

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590834242*

Use the Live Access mode to deliver data within the same tenant without replication. Indeed, the data view is shared live with the customer version of the tenant. In this way, the data is always up-to-date and doesn’t require any update.  
See [Creating Marketplace Data Products](bbcbf42b0cb541529e63628d95f394c8.html).  

### 2023-02-22 · Data Flow: New Features for Table Sources from OData Remote Connections.

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590834285*

- Define OData parameters to control how the data is returned from the service with the OData Parameters section. You can define custom parameters or select suggested parameters in a dropdown list.  
- Browse entities related to the table source up to the second level when navigating properties by setting the depth of OData objects with the OData Properties section.  
See [Add a Source to a Data Flow](7b50e8e304244ced9ff1e62e2f2fe919.html).  

### 2023-02-22 · Data Flow Monitor: Running Metrics

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590834286*

After running a data flow, source and target table metrics are displayed in the Data Flow Monitor for longer and can be used for further analysis.  
See [Creating a Data Flow](e30fd1417e954577baae3246ea470c3f.html).  

### 2023-02-22 · Changed Behavior for Audit Log Deletion

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590834301*

Audit logs can consume a large quantity of GB of disk in your tenant. To avoid over-usage of your disk space, we’ve changed the audit log deletion behavior.  
Behavior before this version: When doing the actions listed below, all related audit log entries were kept, even when the retention period specified for the audit logs had expired. An administrator could decide to delete the audit logs entries using the dedicated area System  Configuration  Audit.  
- Deleting a space  
- Deleting a database user (open SQL schema)  
- Disabling an audit policy for a space  
- Disabling an audit policy for a database user (open SQL schema)  
- Unassigning an HDI container from a space  
New behavior starting with this version:  
- For spaces that have been deleted before this version: All related audit logs have been kept and an administrator can decide to delete them using the dedicated area System  Configuration  Audit.  
- For spaces that are deleted from this version onward: audit log entries are deleted once the number of retention days has been reached (given that the space or database user that generates the logs is not deleted). Also, when doing the actions listed above, all related audit log entries are now deleted, and you’re informed about this directly on the interface.  
See [Logging Read and Change Actions for Audit](266553976e1c4db9aaa28a75e2308b77.html).  
Before performing any of these actions, which will delete the audit log entries, you may want to export them.  

### 2023-02-22 · Anonymizing Data Using the Data Protection Workbench

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.5 · SAP-ID 590833577*

If personal data has been locked or deleted in an application system, it can still be useful for reporting purposes to eliminate the personal data while not deleting the data records completely. You can now use anonymization rules to define how the individual fields are changed if you want a data record to be anonymized.  
Anonymization rules are part of the InfoProvider’s metadata and can be edited in the BW Modeling tools in the standard InfoProvider editor. With SAP BW∕4HANA 2021 SPS04, you can define anonymization rules for DataStore objects. The anonymization rules can be created with a selected SQL syntax as formulas.  
If you have defined anonymization rules, the Anonymization action is available in the Data Protection Workbench. As part of this action, the data records are changed in accordance with the anonymization rules.  
See:  
- [Settings for BW Modeling Tools](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/3e09128a1e56437a9171f15bb8df87e6.html?version=latest)  
- [Define Anonymization Rules](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/cda8e5c422e345d1b4c39d79734f536c.html?version=latest)  
- [SQL Syntax for Anonymization Rules](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/ca8bab950426465a8c136b6729d02566.html?version=latest)  
- [Anonymizing Data Using the Data Protection Workbench](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/214922c4dd93476394f961fde8ad97fc.html?version=latest)  
in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2023-02-07 · Question Mark Highlights Screen Elements with Context-Sensitive Help

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2023.4 · SAP-ID 590834307*

As of the current release, when displaying context-sensitive help in the application, a question mark () replaces the information icon () to highlight screen elements with context-sensitive help on the user interface.  
To open context-sensitive help, select  (Help) in the shell bar of the application.  

### 2023-02-07 · Prevent a Space from Consuming Too Many Threads with Statement Limits

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.4 · SAP-ID 590834023*

To prevent a space from consuming too many tenant threads, we’ve reduced the range of allowed values for custom configuration statement limits in the Workload Configuration area. You can now enter a percentage between 1% and 70% (or the equivalent number) of the total number of threads available in your tenant. Also, the default value for custom configuration is now 70% instead of 80%.Note  
For spaces created before version 2023.04: If you’ve set a thread limit percentage (or number) that is no longer allowed, it is changed to the new default percentage 70% (or the number corresponding to 70% of the total number of threads).  
See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  

### 2023-02-07 · Prevent Statements from Consuming Too Many Threads with Admission Control Parameters

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.4 · SAP-ID 590834024*

To prevent statements from consuming too many threads available in your tenant, the value of the parameter ADMISSION CONTROL QUEUE CPU THRESHOLD has been lowered from 90% to 80% for both default and custom configurations of the Workload Configuration area.  
Note  
This parameter cannot be changed and its threshold value applies to all spaces – including spaces created before this version.  
See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  
You can now monitor the statements that are queued by viewing the cards dedicated to admission control events in the Dashboard tab of the System Monitor.  
See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2023-02-07 · Monitor Admission Control Events (Queued Statements)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.4 · SAP-ID 590834169*

You can now monitor the statements that are queued by viewing the cards dedicated to admission control events in the Dashboard tab of the System Monitor.  
See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2023-02-07 · Mass Deployment of Task Chains in the Transport Application

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.4 · SAP-ID 590833795*

You can now deploy multiple task chains at the same time, in addition to other objects, using the “Deploy after Import” Transport application option.  
See [Importing Content from Another Tenant](b607a12931d74c4a93506ea64c55ab4e.html).  

### 2023-02-07 · Mass Deployment of Task Chains in the Data Builder and the Repository Explorer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.4 · SAP-ID 590833935*

You can now deploy multiple task chains at the same time, in addition to other objects, with the deploy option in the Data Builder or Repository Explorer.  
See:  
- [Repository Explorer](f8ce0b4a24fe473a962176c8aa3cad42.html)  
- [Modeling Data](5c1e3d4a49554fcd8fcf199d664d1109.html)  

### 2023-02-07 · Configuring the Size of Your Test Tenant

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.4 · SAP-ID 590833640*

When you purchase a subscription-based tenant for testing purposes, you can now allocate the capacity units to the storage and compute resources to obtain a configuration that fits your needs, just like for a productive tenant. Note that the minimal configuration provided for SAP Data Warehouse Cloud resources for a test tenant is: 128 GB of storage and 1 compute block.  
See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html)  

### 2023-01-24 · Manual Refresh of Single or Multiple Partitions

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.3 · SAP-ID 590833796*

In the View Persistency Monitor, you can now refresh a single or multiple partitions for a dedicated view, even if the partitions are locked. You can thus avoid running a full data load.  
See [Creating Partitions for Your Persisted Views](9b1b595054c34da5b67ac07595093f82.html).  

### 2023-01-24 · Improved Import Wizard for Entities

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.3 · SAP-ID 590833798*

The import wizards for importing entities from SAP S/4HANA Cloud into SAP Datasphere and for importing entities from SAP BW bridge have been merged. The import process now works the same for both types of import.  
See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP S/4HANA](845fedbd28574aa8b84239df848936f6.html) and [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP BW∕4HANA or SAP BW Bridge](7bcd3218b4064b898ca367b47ff246e2.html).  

### 2023-01-24 · Import of SAP BW Bridge Entities

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.3 · SAP-ID 590833579*

New features are available for the import of SAP BW bridge entities:  
- For SAP BW bridge entities, the semantics is now considered, and dependent objects are also imported.  
- During the import, the entities are now already shared to a chosen target space.  
See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP BW∕4HANA or SAP BW Bridge](7bcd3218b4064b898ca367b47ff246e2.html).  

### 2023-01-24 · Identifying New Columns in a Data Flow Script Node

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.3 · SAP-ID 590833936*

In a Data Flow, the Script node now allows you to identify columns added in the node at a glance with the  (New Column) icon.  
See [Create a Script in a Data Flow](f3e2570966ac4036b552ebd998274af1.html).  

### 2023-01-24 · Fully Enabled SAP SuccessFactors Connection

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.3 · SAP-ID 590833797*

With the connection type, you can now access all SAP SuccessFactors services including OData V4 API endpoints. The restriction to being able to access selected entities for Analytical Dashboards only has been removed.  
See [SAP SuccessFactors Connections](39df02030d4b411487bacecf9afea4e8.html).  

### 2023-01-11 · Intelligent Lookup: Improved Sorting and Filtering of Lookup Records in Unmatched and Multiple Match Results

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Preparation · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.2 · SAP-ID 590833549*

You can now sort and filter by multiple columns in the right-hand table of lookup entity records on the Multiple and Unmatched tabs in the intelligent lookup rule data preview pane.  
See [Process Multiple Match Results](cb2b78a037c346ccad0ade79c6e85c54.html) and [Process Unmatched Results](35fbb44d62e7498bb54461d3a4d460d5.html).  

### 2023-01-11 · Email Notification of Task Chain Runs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.2 · SAP-ID 590833937*

When creating or editing a task chain, you can now set up email notification for deployed task chains to notify selected users of task chain run completion. You can pick users to be notified from a list of tenant member users or specify the email addresses of any user within the same domain as the tenant owner.  
See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html).  

### 2023-01-11 · Correlation ID in Error Messages

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.2 · SAP-ID 590833829*

Users of Data Marketplace will now find a correlation ID and an error code in error messages. This additional technical information can help SAP support engineers to resolve incidents.  

### 2023-01-11 · <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Consumption APIs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.2 · SAP-ID 590833799*

The ODATA APIs have been renamed toSAP Datasphere Consumption APIs.  
On the SAP API Business Hub, the APIs have been split into two packages: one for Catalog and one for Consumption.  
See [SAP API Business Hub](https://api.sap.com/package/sapdatawarehousecloud/overview).  

### 2023-01-09 · X.509 Client Certificate Support for <span class="ph uicontrol">Cloud Data Integration</span> and <span class="ph uicontrol">SAP S/4HANA Cloud</span> Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.1 · SAP-ID 590833800*

You can now use X.509 client certificates or certificate chains for authentication when creating connections to Cloud Data Integration endpoints and to SAP S/4HANA Cloud in a more secure way including encryption. For new connections, X.509 Client Certificate becomes the default authentication type.  
See:  
- [Cloud Data Integration Connections](cd33107246f446628f9baff56faf5a1b.html)  
- [SAP S/4HANA Cloud Connections](a98e5ffdf47c44d9a845dca01a18bd82.html)  

### 2023-01-09 · Sharing TCUR* Tables To Other Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.1 · SAP-ID 590834287*

You can now share the TCUR* currency conversion tables to other spaces using the already existing dialog box that allows to create currency conversion objects. Once the tables have been shared to other spaces, these spaces will see your space as an alternative source to create the currency conversion views.  
See [Enabling Currency Conversion with TCUR* Tables and Views](b462239ffb644d9baab4442a10a72edf.html).  

### 2023-01-09 · Searching for Objects in the Impact and Lineage Analysis Diagram

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.1 · SAP-ID 590833938*

Diagrams can contain a large number or objects, and searching for them is made easy thanks to the new search bar present in the dialog toolbar.  
See [Impact and Lineage Analysis](9da4892cb0e4427ab80ad8d89e6676b8.html).  

### 2023-01-09 · Export and Import Intelligent Lookups as JSON Files or via the Transport App

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Preparation · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.1 · SAP-ID 590834126*

You can now export and import intelligent lookups either manually as JSON files or via the Transport app.  
See:  
- [Importing and Exporting Objects in CSN/JSON Files](f8ff0628c9fc49229740ffcd4d20e9aa.html)  
- [Transporting Content Between Tenants](df12666cf98e41248ef2251c564b0166.html)  

### 2023-01-09 · Context Management

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.1 · SAP-ID 590833830*

With this new feature data providers are now able to define and limit who can see their data products. You can now publish your data products in private contexts. Then your data products are not available publicly any longer. You can invite consumers to join your own context to be members in your private data marketplace. You can also invite other data providers to publish their data products in your context to create a wider offering.  
With the introduction of context management, all existing data providers profiles will be set to type Public Data Provider and all existing data products will be assigned to the context SAP Data Marketplace. When creating new data products you have to specify the context where they will be published.  
For data providers, see [Using Contexts to Realize Public, Private, and Internal Offers](c936499e7ef045b09139723fd0ac9e09.html).  
For consumers, see [Managing Marketplace Data Products](5d725beb848e432a91eb8de29875d7e2.html).  

### 2023-01-09 · Command Line Interface Enhancements: Reset Database User Password

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.1 · SAP-ID 590834025*

A new command dwc dbusers password reset lets administrators and space administrators reset the password of a database user.  
See [Manage Spaces and Space Access via the Command Line](5eac5b71e2d34c32b63f3d8d47a0b1d0.html).  

### 2023-01-09 · Command Line Interface Enhancements: List Spaces

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2023.1 · SAP-ID 590834026*

A new command dwc spaces list lets any user obtain a list of the spaces to which they have access on the tenant.  
See [Manage Spaces and Space Access via the Command Line](5eac5b71e2d34c32b63f3d8d47a0b1d0.html).  

### 2022-12-06 · Viewing Data in Local Tables Using Open SQL Schema As Source

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.25 · SAP-ID 590834289*

You can now use the Data Viewer and all its options to view and filter on data stored in a local table using an Open SQL Schema as a source.  

### 2022-12-06 · Providing Feedback

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.25 · SAP-ID 590834006*

You can now use the new  (Feedback) button in the shell bar to share your experience when working with SAP Data Warehouse Cloud and to provide feedback to help us improve your experience. The new button opens a separate browser tab. It is replacing the Send Feedback button that was available on the Home page.  

### 2022-12-06 · New Null and Empty Value Handling Options

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.25 · SAP-ID 590834288*

You can now choose to insert missing string values as either Empty value or Null when loading and maintaining local table data. Also, you can filter on Empty value and Null in a data preview.  
See [Load or Delete Local Table Data](870401f211f94132909bd9f2fafd91b2.html), [Maintain Local Table Data](4bd5e641be48409c8c79336df0c4a3c7.html), and [Viewing Object Data](b338e4aa7e7e494eb68c383720ebfd3a.html).  

### 2022-12-06 · Monitoring a Data Flow while the Runtime Service is Unavailable

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.25 · SAP-ID 590833941*

You are now able to use the Data Flow Monitor while the data flow runtime service is down and to see information from the latest runtime service availability. Refresh the page to see the latest data.  

### 2022-12-06 · Increased Maximum Number of Entries in IP Allowlist

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.25 · SAP-ID 590833641*

You can now add up to 250 entries in the IP allowlist.  
See [Manage IP Allowlist](a3c214514ef94e899459f68f4c1e2a23.html).  

### 2022-12-06 · Enable Time-Dependency for a Dimension or Text Entity

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.25 · SAP-ID 590833940*

You can now enable time-dependency in your dimension or text entity to define periods of validity for each row and ensure that only the appropriate dimension members with the correct names are displayed for any given time period.  
See [Enable Time-Dependency for a Dimension or Text Entity](11b2ff4179a14c379bfdf7b7b85b09a1.html).  

### 2022-12-06 · Editing Custom CSN Annotations in All Data Layer Editors

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.25 · SAP-ID 590833939*

You can now edit custom CSN Annotations in the Table Editor, Graphical View Editor, SQL View Editor, and E/R Model Editor.  
See [Edit Custom CSN Annotations in a View or Table](820d01351cd444be893af8326c1d2a9c.html).  

### 2022-12-06 · Changing the Maximum Number of Records for Expensive Statements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.25 · SAP-ID 590834170*

Up to now, the maximum number of records for expensive statements that were stored in the monitoring tables was 30 000. An administrator can now change this number, which will impact the views dedicated to monitoring and information related to statements in the System Monitor. For example, you may want to increase the number of records in order to trace more data in the System Monitor.  
See [Configure Monitoring](9cd0691c44a74f2aa47b52f615f74433.html).  

### 2022-11-17 · View Objects Shared to Your Spaces in the Repository Explorer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.24 · SAP-ID 590834308*

In the Repository Explorer, objects that are shared to a space you are a member of are now listed, even if you are not a member of the space that shared them.  
See [Repository Explorer](f8ce0b4a24fe473a962176c8aa3cad42.html).  

### 2022-11-17 · Retry of Failed Task Chain Runs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.24 · SAP-ID 590834229*

The Data Integration Monitor now allows you to retry execution of a failed task chain run. When the last task chain run fails, you can choose the Retry Run option to retry execution of the failed task chain, including child or subtasks (if any are defined), starting at the point at which the task chain run failed.  
See [Monitoring Task Chains](4142201ec1aa49faad89a688a2f1852c.html).  

### 2022-11-17 · Remote Table Statistics Move to the <span class="ph uicontrol">Remote Query Monitor</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.24 · SAP-ID 590833801*

The remote tables statistics creation has been moved from the Remote Table Monitor to the Remote Query Monitor.  
See [Creating Statistics for Your Remote Tables](e4120bbb98e44994aa1e0b32ff3f209d.html).  

### 2022-11-17 · Process Variant ADSOOLR in SAP BW Bridge Cockpit

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.24 · SAP-ID 590833580*

The SAP BW Bridge Cockpit offers process variant ADSOOLR in the Process Variant Editor - Edit Variant app. You can use this process variant to automatically remove overlapping requests from a DataStore object.  
See [Process Variant ADSOOLR](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/d32c096532114e23b7ccc1d293743c7e.html?locale=en-US) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2022-11-17 · Improvements for the <span class="ph uicontrol">Impact and Lineage Analysis</span> Dialog

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.24 · SAP-ID 590833942*

The Impact and Lineage Analysis dialog has been improved:  
- Get a better understanding of the impact and lineage of the metadata dependencies going through the diagram with the new Dependency Analysis diagram.  
- Display a new analysis around any object present on the diagram.  
- Navigate the diagram with ease by choosing to only display information relevant to you. Display only the lineage, the impact, or both. You can also display the full branch of any object.  
See [Impact and Lineage Analysis](9da4892cb0e4427ab80ad8d89e6676b8.html).  

### 2022-11-17 · Improved Import of Objects into the Business Builder

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.24 · SAP-ID 590833471*

The import of objects into the Business Builder is now executed in parallel and is therefore faster.  

### 2022-11-17 · Display of NULL Values

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.24 · SAP-ID 590834049*

You can now choose on measure level if null values shall be treated as NULL or zero in the data preview and story consumption.  
See [Define Measures](903acb8d378d4984af8ad4f694d56529.html).  

### 2022-10-19 · Revised Data Preview in Graphical and SQL View Editor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.22 · SAP-ID 590833943*

A number of enhancements are available in the data preview:  
- There is no limit to a maximum of 1,000 lines anymore. Instead, the preview header indicates the total number of rows that can be displayed by scrolling down.  
- You can now show, hide, sort, and filter on multiple columns by clicking  (Columns Settings).  
- Handling of input parameters has improved in the Graphical View editor, Table Editor, and SQL View editor. You can click the input parameter strip to change the input parameter values.  
- To avoid long loading times, you will now receive a warning message for non-replicating remote tables.  
See [Viewing Object Data](b338e4aa7e7e494eb68c383720ebfd3a.html).  

### 2022-10-19 · Locking Spaces to Avoid Audit Logs from Consuming Too Much Disk Storage

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.22 · SAP-ID 590834027*

Up to now, a space was automatically locked when it exceeded its allocations of in-memory or disk storage. In addition, certain spaces are now automatically locked when audit logs consume a high amount of GB of the tenant disk.  
Also, if you have a Space Administrator role, you can now manually lock a space.  
See [Unlock a Locked Space](c05b6a6d06db427dbdd3041d61fd5840.html).  

### 2022-10-19 · Displayed Number of Remaining Days Before Expiration of Free-Plan Tenant

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.22 · SAP-ID 590834171*

When you’re using a consumption-based tenant with a free plan, the number of remaining days before the 90-day expiration is now displayed in the top panel of SAP Data Warehouse Cloud. This enables you to know how many days remain before you can update your free-plan tenant to a standard plan.  
See [Creating and Configuring Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](2f80b57359254b5cbb4faac794ade90c.html) and [Update Your Free Plan to Standard Plan in SAP BTP](f173be27e9cb4c758c0f1fa133d9edda.html).  

### 2022-10-19 · Data Preview Timeout

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.22 · SAP-ID 590834290*

To provide a more consistent experience and reduce loads on remote systems, the Data Builder data preview now times out if it is unable to return data within three minutes. When previewing a remote table or view, guidance is given to the user to improve response times by enabling replication or persistence, removing unnecessary columns, and enabling filters.  
See [Viewing Object Data](b338e4aa7e7e494eb68c383720ebfd3a.html).  

### 2022-10-19 · Additional substatus log message information provided for failed task runs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.22 · SAP-ID 590833802*

In addition to monitor user interface enhancements provided in previous releases, the monitor tab displays for remote table replication, view persistency, data flow, and task chain runs now provide additional substatus log message detail for failed task runs.  
See [Navigating in the Monitor Tabs](5d4af7620a214872a4dc5286f5e9e997.html).  

### 2022-10-05 · Refresh Remote Tables Structure

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.21 · SAP-ID 590834291*

By refreshing your remote table structure from the Data Builder, if new columns exist in the data source, you can select those that need to be added to your remote tables.  
See [Process Source Changes in the Table Editor](622328b59afa40fd810b44ac84d6afd3.html).  

### 2022-10-05 · New section <span class="ph uicontrol">Usage Overview</span> on the data product's details page

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.21 · SAP-ID 590833832*

In the new section Usage Overview data providers can find recent and overall numbers on active and expired licenses, activations and updates. The numbers are helpful to data providers when they want to know more about the usage of their data products. Data providers need these number also when they want to clean their product portfolio and delete data products from it.  
See [Managing your Data Product's Lifecycle](c400001264094f8c89da104db72514ad.html).  

### 2022-10-05 · New Product Lifecycle States

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.21 · SAP-ID 590834243*

With the new lifecycle functions Deactivate and Delete the following product lifecycle states are introduced as well: Deactivated and Deleted.  
SAP has the option to block products from the Data Marketplace in cases of non-compliance or pending disputes. Products that are affected by this action are indicated with the status Blocked.  
See [Managing your Data Product's Lifecycle](c400001264094f8c89da104db72514ad.html).  

### 2022-10-05 · Language Support (Latvian and Estonian)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.21 · SAP-ID 590834007*

SAP Datasphere now supports Latvian and Estonian in its menus, buttons, messages, and other elements of the user interface.  
See [System Requirements and Technical Prerequisites](70ffed417b054b4692458edbd56c90f5.html).  

### 2022-10-05 · Deactivating and Deleting Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.21 · SAP-ID 590833831*

It is now possible for data providers to deactivate and delete data products if they fulfill certain conditions. Before it was only possible to delist them.  
See [Deleting a Data Product](c0463f0978204df0b267ff4990edde70.html).  

### 2022-10-05 · Connection Type for SAP HANA, Data Lake Files

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.21 · SAP-ID 590834230*

You can now create connections to access data from the Files component of standalone SAP HANA, data lake and use these connections in data flows.  
See [SAP HANA Cloud, Data Lake Files Connections](356e41e880e54255891b702d2afefeb3.html).  

### 2022-10-05 · Command Line Interface Enhancements: OAuth Client Login and Syntax Changes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.21 · SAP-ID 590834028*

You can now log into an OAuth client with the dwc command line interface and run multiple commands without needing to provide passcodes.  
In addition the following syntax changes affect existing options and commands:  
- -s (--space) is now capitalized to -S  
- -sd (--definitions) is now capitalized to -D  
- cache-init is changed to cache init and the other cache-* commands also have their hyphens replaced by spaces.  
See [Manage Spaces and Space Access via the Command Line](5eac5b71e2d34c32b63f3d8d47a0b1d0.html).  

### 2022-09-20 · Setting Compound Keys

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.20 · SAP-ID 590834292*

Compound keys are now supported and can be set in the Data Builder editors such as the Table Editor, the Graphical View editor, the E/R Model editor, and the SQL View editor. Compound keys are also supported in parent/child hierarchies.  
See [Set Key Columns to Uniquely Identify Records](d9ef2c91f6d647e584bad51999e441cd.html) and [Create an External Hierarchy for Drill-Down](dbac7a862b3744d8a71d268644aac389.html).  

### 2022-09-20 · Mass Deployment of Objects in the Data Builder and the Repository Explorer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.20 · SAP-ID 590833944*

Deploy large quantities of tables, graphical views, SQL views, Data Access Controls, and Analytic Models with the deploy option in the Data Builder and Repository Explorer.  
See [Repository Explorer](f8ce0b4a24fe473a962176c8aa3cad42.html) and [Modeling Data](5c1e3d4a49554fcd8fcf199d664d1109.html).  

### 2022-09-20 · Enable Batch Query in <span class="ph uicontrol">Data Flow</span> to Fetch Large Odata Source Files

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.20 · SAP-ID 590833945*

To add large Odata source files in a Data Flow, enable the Batch Query option in the Advanced properties section of an odata node. It will fetch the data batch by batch according to the fetch size defined.  
See [Add a Source to a Data Flow](7b50e8e304244ced9ff1e62e2f2fe919.html).  

### 2022-09-20 · Creating an <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Data Warehouse Cloud</span> Service Instance in SAP BTP

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.20 · SAP-ID 590833642*

If you've signed the Cloud Platform Enterprise Agreement (CPEA), you can now directly create SAP Data Warehouse Cloud service instances (tenants) in SAP BTP.  
See [Creating and Configuring Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](2f80b57359254b5cbb4faac794ade90c.html).  

### 2022-09-20 · Browse Data via the <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Datasphere</span> Public APIs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.20 · SAP-ID 590833803*

You can use the OData APIs to search and replicate data following the OData version 4 standard. With these APIs, you can extend existing integration capabilities with SAP Analytics Cloud, and you are given the freedom to build your own solutions.  
See [Consuming Data via the OData API](7a453609c8694b029493e7d87e0de60a.html).  

### 2022-09-07 · Replication Error Messages in Remote Table Monitor and Table Editor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.19 · SAP-ID 590834231*

For remote tables with a replication error (real-time or snapshot), you can now open error messages by clicking on the error status in the Remote Table Monitor, on the monitor details page, or in the table editor. The messages provide contextual information including where and at which date and time the error occurred. If appropriate, they let you open applications that help solving the error, for example the monitor details page or the Connections or Configuration application to be able to fix issues with the connection or the Data Provisioning agent. If applicable, they offer the Retry action to resume real-time replication.  

### 2022-09-07 · New Flexible Column Layout, Search, Sort, and Filter Controls

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.19 · SAP-ID 590834232*

The Data Integration Monitor ( ) now provides a simpler method of resizing column or panel layouts in remote table replication, view persistency, data flow, and task chain run monitors. In addition, each of the monitors now provides the ability to sort and filter column displays as well as search log messages for individual task runs.  
See [Managing and Monitoring Data Integration](4cbf7c7fc64645bfa364332827557267.html).  

### 2022-09-07 · Modeling After an Aggregation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.19 · SAP-ID 590833946*

In the Graphical View editor, you can now add any node after an Aggregation node.  
See [Aggregate Data in a Graphical View](7733250f9d2a46b690a5288634d269f0.html).  

### 2022-09-07 · Improved Usability in Connections List

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.19 · SAP-ID 590833804*

A number of usability enhancements are available in the list of connections:  
- You can now use  (Select Columns) to select the columns that you want to display and to decide on the order in which they are displayed in the list.  
- You can additionally display the Technical Name via the column selection, and you can sort the connections list on the new column. Along with adding the Technical Name column, the Name column has been renamed to Business Name.  
- Search now considers Technical Name, Real-Time Replication Status, and Owner in addition to the Business Name.  

### 2022-09-07 · Importing Hierarchies

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.19 · SAP-ID 590833582*

You can now import hierarchies from the SAP BW bridge system into SAP Data Warehouse Cloud. For every InfoObject with hierarchies, the system creates the required views: the hierarchy directory view, the hierarchy directory text view, and the hierarchy view.  
See :  
- [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP BW∕4HANA or SAP BW Bridge](7bcd3218b4064b898ca367b47ff246e2.html)  
- [Add a Hierarchy to a Dimension](218b7e6bd60846dda2f03b789b389cb0.html)  
- [Hierarchy](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/4a245e22b1160456e10000000a421937.html?version=latest) in the SAP BW bridge documentation  

### 2022-09-07 · Create, Read, Update, and Delete Data Access Controls using the <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP Data Warehouse Cloud</span> Command Line Interface

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.19 · SAP-ID 590834029*

It is now possible to create, read, update, and delete data access controls using the SAP Data Warehouse Cloud command line interface.  
See [The Space Definition File Format](3fcbf619f2774b849fa7df58163b3609.html).  

### 2022-09-07 · CompositeProvider Usability Enhancement

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.19 · SAP-ID 590833581*

You can now filter by attribute name when selecting navigation attributes in a CompositeProvider.  
See [Activating Navigation Attributes](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/943825c209ca4cbfb18c86ac8bf9fc15.html?locale=en-US&version=latest) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2022-09-07 · Changing Technical Content InfoObjects

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.19 · SAP-ID 590834141*

You can now enhance or change technical content InfoObjects by assigning them to one of your transportable packages.  
See [Changing Technical Content InfoObjects](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/f116ce7d83a940e7a66f6550409ba22c.html?locale=en-US&version=latest) in the SAP BW bridge documentation.  

### 2022-08-24 · UI Settings in Import Wizard

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.18 · SAP-ID 590833583*

In the import wizard, the description of the InfoAreas in the InfoArea tree is shown if the UI setting is set accordingly. For master data, the type is now displayed.  
See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP BW∕4HANA or SAP BW Bridge](7bcd3218b4064b898ca367b47ff246e2.html).  

### 2022-08-24 · Terminology Changes in Data Access Control

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.18 · SAP-ID 590833989*

Data entity is renamed to permissions entity and output is renamed to criteria.  
See [Create a "Single Values" Data Access Control](5246328ec59045cb9c2aa693daee2557.html).  

### 2022-08-24 · New Geo-Coordinates Layer: Choropleth / Drill

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.18 · SAP-ID 590833948*

When using the geo-coordinates column in an SAP Analytics Cloud geo map, the Choropleth / Drill type of layer is supported.  
See [Create a Geo-Coordinates Column in a Graphical View](6f3ffbd077294cfaa6ae159f45d130b7.html).  

### 2022-08-24 · GDPR Data Privacy Compliance

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.18 · SAP-ID 590833988*

By default, SAP Data Warehouse Cloud keeps track of objects you’ve accessed, so you can quickly locate those objects again. It also saves previous searches you executed, so you may repeat them. You can now opt out of this object tracking and saving of previous search queries with Privacy settings you can specify in your user profile.  
See [Changing SAP Datasphere Settings](1084796d09464e78870f32cab8584dfc.html).  

### 2022-08-24 · Explore Your Views with View Analyzer

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.18 · SAP-ID 590833805*

From the View Persistency Monitor details screen, use the new View Analyzer to analyze the entities that compose your data model. You can then use this information to decide on some improvements.  
See [Exploring Views with View Analyzer](8921e5acf2ad4c8a98073edae4c214c7.html).  

### 2022-08-24 · Easy Access to the <span class="ph uicontrol">Impact and Lineage Analysis</span> Diagram

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.18 · SAP-ID 590833947*

You can now access the Impact and Lineage diagram from any editor in the Data Builder. This allows you to get a better understanding of the impact and lineage of your data object on the spot, without having to leave the editor you are working in.  
See [Creating a Local Table](2509fe4d86aa472b9858164b55b38077.html), [Creating a Graphical View](27efb479c4814252964d3fbc6ca2dfc3.html), [Creating an SQL View](81920e4d583f45fd8761c662d3c8abab.html), [Creating an Entity-Relationship Model](a91c042549fb497384e756d5f5c71fde.html).  

### 2022-08-18 · Searching Objects in the Repository

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.17 · SAP-ID 590833949*

The performances of the Repository search have been enhanced for all editors. Loading repository objects is faster and limited to 500 objects.  

### 2022-08-18 · More Granular Control of Data Access

*Action: Required · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.17 · SAP-ID 590834302*

A SAP Data Warehouse Cloud user must be a member of a space to access the data in that space. You can now restrict data access based on user roles with the Data Warehouse Consumption privilege (-RU-E---), which defines three levels of permission:  
- R (Read) - Access only data that is exposed for consumption.  
- U (Update) - Upload data from CSV files to local tables and delete data from local tables.  
- E (Execute) - Access all data and edit data in local tables.  
The standard application roles have been updated to include appropriate levels of DW Consumption permission: Role DW Consumption Permissions Can Access DW Administrator -RU-E--- All data DW Space Administrator -RU-E--- All data in space DW Integrator -------- No data DW Modeler -RU-E--- All data in space DW Viewer -R------ Only data exposed for consumption DW Consumer -RU----- Only data exposed for consumption  
The main effect of these changes is for users with the DW Viewer role, who will continue to be able to access SAP Data Warehouse Cloud and open any object in their space but will no longer be able to preview data, except the output nodes of views that are exposed for consumption.Note  
If you have created custom roles, you will need to add the DW Consumption privilege with the appropriate level of permission to allow them to continue accessing data. You should set the minimum level of permission necessary and, if appropriate, you may be able to move users from a modeler or viewer-like role to a consumer-like role.  
See [Privileges and Permissions](d7350c6823a14733a7a5727bad8371aa.html) and [Privileges by App, Tool, Object, and Task](2d8b7d04dcae402f911d119437ce0a74.html).  

### 2022-08-18 · Cancel a Running Execution of a View Persistency

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.17 · SAP-ID 590834233*

From the View Persistency Monitor, you can now cancel a running execution of a view persistency using the Cancel Run button. This will turn the status of the task into failed.  
See [Persisting and Monitoring Views](9af04c990f294fd28c00f46763dd8b0d.html).  

### 2022-07-28 · New Cross-Space <span class="ph uicontrol">System Monitor</span> Tool

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.16 · SAP-ID 590833643*

The new System Monitor tool allows administrators to monitor the performance of their system and identify storage, task, out-of-memory, and other issues across all spaces. For example, you can see:  
- All the errors (such as failed tasks and out-of-memory errors) that occurred yesterday.  
- The top five statements with the highest peak memory consumption.  
- An overview of all tasks across spaces.  
See [Monitoring SAP Datasphere](d39b8652994846f9ab80b32fc5b4d671.html).  

### 2022-07-28 · Increased Length of Technical Names for Data Builder Objects

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.16 · SAP-ID 590833950*

The maximum length of technical names for Data Builder objects (graphical and SQL views, tables, E/R models) is now 50 characters.  

### 2022-07-28 · External Hierarchy

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.16 · SAP-ID 590834050*

You can now add one or more external parent-child hierarchies which have been defined in the source view in the Data Builder.  
See [Add an External Hierarchy](1ec95b0f18824586bc330b8c445278a8.html).  

### 2022-07-28 · Enabling External License Key Purchase

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.16 · SAP-ID 590834244*

With this version you as the Data Provider can add a URL to your external website where consumers can purchase a license key for your data products. Consumers will find the button Get License on the details page of the data product which will lead them to this website. You find the new parameter URL for License Key Purchase in the Create Data Product function.  
See [Creating Marketplace Data Products](bbcbf42b0cb541529e63628d95f394c8.html).  

### 2022-07-13 · Load Balancing for ABAP-based Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.15 · SAP-ID 590833806*

You can now leverage load balancing by connecting to an RFC message server rather than directly connecting to specific application servers. Load Balancing is supported for SAP ABAP, SAP BW, SAP ECC, and SAP S/4HANA (on-premise) connection types.  
See [SAP ABAP Connections](a75c1aacf951449ba3b740c7e46da3a9.html), [SAP BW Connections](e589041e80264f43b6c209c407336376.html), [SAP ECC Connections](e546ccd61af54bf49a0f531a43fe0961.html), and [SAP S/4HANA On-Premise Connections](a49a1e3cc50f4af89711d8306bdd8f26.html).  

### 2022-06-28 · Task Chain Monitoring

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.14 · SAP-ID 590833808*

Monitoring of task chain execution has been added to the Data Integration Monitor. You can view the status and progress of task chains that are scheduled to run, are currently running, or have already been run. In addition, you can view more detailed information on task chain runs, including sub-task execution log messages .  
See [Monitoring Task Chains](4142201ec1aa49faad89a688a2f1852c.html).  

### 2022-06-28 · Task Chain Creation and Scheduling

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.14 · SAP-ID 590833951*

You can now create and schedule task chains, which allow you to define a group or series of tasks that are executed in sequence. You can run task chains manually or define a schedule for their execution. Tasks chains may include execution of Remote Table Replication, View Persistency, and Data Flow runs.  
See [Creating a Task Chain](d1afbc2b9ee84d44a00b0b777ac243e1.html).  

### 2022-06-28 · Simplified Workload Management Area

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.14 · SAP-ID 590834313*

The area where you can manage space workload with workload classes has been simplified. You can now either leave the default configuration for your space or select the custom configuration and enter statement limits.  
Note that the section Workload Class has been renamed to Workload Management and the Statement Limits section has been renamed to Workload Configuration.  
See [Set Priorities and Statement Limits for Spaces or Groups](d66ac1efb5054068a104c4559b72d272.html).  

### 2022-06-28 · One Replication per Source Object for Remote Tables Connected via <span class="ph pname">SAP HANA Smart Data Access</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.14 · SAP-ID 590833807*

Remote tables connected via SAP HANA smart data access must have only one remote table per remote source object in data access Replicated. If several remote tables point to the same source object, they must use the same replicated data.  
See [Monitoring Remote Tables](4dd95d7bff1f48b399c8b55dbdd34b9e.html).  

### 2022-06-28 · Intelligent Lookup Usability Enhancements

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Preparation · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.14 · SAP-ID 590833551*

A number of usability enhancements are available in the intelligent lookup editor:  
- The Preview panel is now bigger, by default, when opened and will remember its size if you resize it. In addition:  
- The Review tab now supports selecting multiple pairing groups before clicking Confirm or Reject.  
- The Multiple tab now lists match candidates in descending order of match score, and includes a Reject button to allow you to select a pairing group for which none of the proposed match candidates is appropriate and move the records to the Unmatched tab.  
- You can now filter and sort the left and right tables on the Multiple and Unmatched tabs independently, and the filter is persisted after matching or rejecting a pairing group.  
- The Unprocessed tab is moved to the penultimate position (before the Error) tab.  
- A new Include Review Records option on the output node is selected by default, but can be disabled to exclude records on the Review tab from your output view.  
See [Creating an Intelligent Lookup](8f29f801faea4d48816d0339777f9d16.html).  

### 2022-06-28 · Auto-Select the Best Match Candidate in Intelligent Lookup Fuzzy Match Results

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Preparation · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.14 · SAP-ID 590833550*

A new option, Multiple Match Processing, will now auto-select among multiple matches when one match candidate scores conclusively higher than any other. You can disable this default behavior (and review each of your multiple matches manually) by setting this option to Always Review Match Candidates.  
See [Create a Fuzzy Match Rule](b063518d7a684051b66cc6d9681c8753.html).  

### 2022-06-03 · Simulating DTPs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590834143*

You can now simulate the data update of a DTP request to help you to analyze an incorrect DTP request. You can also simulate a transformation prior to the actual data transfer.  

### 2022-06-03 · Scheduling of process chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833587*

You can now schedule and unschedule process chains.  
See [Scheduling or Unscheduling](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/d430a3fa15324145821f0a7554a0ce75.html?locale=en-US&version=latest).  

### 2022-06-03 · Remote Conversion

*Action: Required · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833584*

Using remote conversion, you can now transfer data models with data from SAP BW systems to SAP BW bridge. The Transfer Cockpit supports you in migrating selected data models into an SAP BW bridge tenant and to perform a remote data transfer into that tenant.  
As a prerequisite, you need to install the DMIS add-on your on-premise SAP BW or SAP BW∕4HANA system.  

### 2022-06-03 · Modify Column Data Type for JSON and Excel files

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833954*

If a source in your data flow is a JSON or Excel file, you can now change the data type of a column and modify its corresponding properties.  
See [Add a Source to a Data Flow](7b50e8e304244ced9ff1e62e2f2fe919.html).  

### 2022-06-03 · Modeling after a union

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833953*

Your modeling capabilities have been enhanced as you can now add any node(s) after a union node in the Graphical View editor.  
See [Creating a Graphical View](27efb479c4814252964d3fbc6ca2dfc3.html).  

### 2022-06-03 · Mass activation of objects in BW Modelling Tools

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833585*

In the BW Modeling Tools, you can now activate multiple objects at once without requiring individual activation steps and sequences. The following objects are supported:  
- InfoObjects  
- DataStore objects  
- CompositeProviders  
- Transformations  
- Data transfer processes  

### 2022-06-03 · Improved SAC Time-Series Chart: Time Dimensions' Day Names Become Day Numbers

*Action: Recommended · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833952*

Time dimensions' day names are replaced by corresponding day numbers of the month. For example, the time dimension for Monday, the 7th of November 2022 will show "7" instead of "Monday". This increases SAP Data Warehouse Cloud's alignment with SAP Analytics Cloud. You will still be able to show day names in SAP Analytics Cloud via table's properties. Existing time dimensions won't be affected by the change and will need to be refreshed.  

### 2022-06-03 · Fail on String Truncation in Data Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590834293*

You can now choose to fail a data flow run if string truncation is detected while fetching the source columns.  
See [Add a Source to a Data Flow](7b50e8e304244ced9ff1e62e2f2fe919.html).  

### 2022-06-03 · Enhanced Space Selection

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833833*

This release simplifies the selection of a space. Data Providers will find this enhanced function in the Create Data Product screen, while consumers will find it in the Load Data Product screen.  
When you click on the new icon which you can find next to the space input field, you will get a list with all spaces you have access to. Now you can select the space easily from the list. If you prefer to type the name of the space, you will receive auto-suggests while typing.  
See:  
- [Creating Marketplace Data Products](bbcbf42b0cb541529e63628d95f394c8.html)  
- [Installing Marketplace Data Products](92c35efd6a4945a1a78250539aee9a51.html)  

### 2022-06-03 · Download of task logs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833809*

In the Data Integration Monitor, task logs have been enhanced: A download link will appear against a message only in case it contains more information that can’t be displayed in the monitor. You can then download the log to perform deeper analysis on what has happened with your task.  

### 2022-06-03 · DTP Monitor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590834142*

The DTP Monitor app offers you new functions. You can now get more information on red packages.  
See [Monitoring Data Transfer Process](https://help.sap.com/docs/SAP_BW_BRIDGE/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/11965da3103249338f519d139ac3a993.html?locale=en-US&version=latest).  

### 2022-06-03 · Custom process type in process chains

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833586*

You can now implement your own simplified process type. This enable you to execute your own ABAP code using a process chain.  
See [Execute Customer Process](https://help.sap.com/viewer/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/latest/en-US/28d42e503f9b4472a8052da75412f6d4.html).  

### 2022-06-03 · Currency Conversion

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.12 · SAP-ID 590833472*

In the Business Builder, you can now convert currency values into another currency for business entities and consumption models.  
See [Use Currency Conversion](1ba4554f0aaf40f7b5c0e430eb2da510.html).  

### 2022-05-17 · Troubleshooting Guide

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.11 · SAP-ID 590833588*

For SAP BW bridge, there is now a troubleshooting guide available. It explains how to identify configuration issues in the connectivity of on-premise source systems to SAP BW bridge.  
See [Troubleshooting](21ab1e7bfece4d22a3e58c296abd4340.html).  

### 2022-05-17 · Import/Export of Data Flow JSON from One Space to Another

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.11 · SAP-ID 590833810*

When you create a connection, the space name is no longer hardcoded as a prefix in the connection technical name. This change lets you export data flows from one space to a JSON file and then import the file into another space that contains a connection with the same name.  
See [Importing Objects from a CSN/JSON File](23599e6347fb4c9e9a71c82f62449875.html).  

### 2022-05-17 · Enhanced Import of Structure Changes for Remote Data Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.11 · SAP-ID 590833955*
