Support for import and refresh of data structure changes has been added for remote sources connected via SAP HANA smart data access, in addition to existing support for connections via SAP HANA smart data integration.  
See [Process Source Changes in the Table Editor](622328b59afa40fd810b44ac84d6afd3.html) and [Review and Edit Imported Table Properties](75cea7b1cbeb4c9dba0ac13605c527b3.html).  

### 2022-05-17 · Enforcing Row-Level Security Following View Source Changes

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.11 · SAP-ID 590834294*

A view’s persisted data will now be removed if any changes are made and deployed to data access controls applied to any of its sources. This change ensures that the row-level security provided by your data access controls is kept up-to-date at each level in the stack whenever changes are made.  
See [Persisted Views and Data Access Control](7a4a983611cc4efb9415e6f3db310eaa.html).  

### 2022-05-17 · Enable Currency Conversion with TCUR* Tables and Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.11 · SAP-ID 590833956*

You can now generate currency conversion views and the necessary supporting objects from an SAP connection or manually.  
See [Enabling Currency Conversion with TCUR* Tables and Views](b462239ffb644d9baab4442a10a72edf.html).  

### 2022-05-17 · Display Name Applied to Data Integration Monitor Objects

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2022.11 · SAP-ID 590834309*

The display name chosen in your profile settings - business or technical name – now also applies to the objects of the Data Integration Monitor.  
See [Changing SAP Datasphere Settings](1084796d09464e78870f32cab8584dfc.html).  

### 2022-05-06 · New Impact and Lineage Diagram

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.10 · SAP-ID 590834295*

The Impact and Lineage Analysis has been completely rebuilt to provide a new look and feel and the following enhancements:  
- Now access the analysis from the Data Builder start page (as well as the Repository Explorer and the data access control editor).  
- See each object in the context of its space.  
- Explore and tailor the diagram to your needs by showing or hiding individual branches of lineage or impacts.  
- See the deployment status of objects, whether they contain persisted/replicated data, and whether they are exposed for consumption directly on their diagram symbols.  
- See a preview of any object’s properties and open it in its editor.  
See [Impact and Lineage Analysis](9da4892cb0e4427ab80ad8d89e6676b8.html).  

### 2022-05-06 · Maintenance Activity for Uploaded Drivers

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.10 · SAP-ID 590833644*

With the first login after your tenant has been updated to version 2022.10, we'll perform some maintenance activity for your uploaded third-party ODBC drivers. Your existing connections and data flows won't be affected by this activity. In the exceptional case of an issue, you can re-upload the required drivers.  
See [Upload Third-Party ODBC Drivers (Required for Data Flows)](b9b5579054df48c39381d5b17286bf21.html).  

### 2022-05-06 · Identify Tables as Hierarchies

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.10 · SAP-ID 590833957*

You can now set the semantic usage of a table to Hierarchy directly. Previously, only views could be identified as external hierarchies.  
See [Create an External Hierarchy for Drill-Down](dbac7a862b3744d8a71d268644aac389.html).  

### 2022-04-19 · Schedule Daily Data Integration Tasks Without Reset on the First of Each Month

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.9 · SAP-ID 590833811*

When you create a simple schedule to run daily tasks, the tasks are run at a fixed frequency that is by default now not reset on the first day of each month. You can however ask that the run cycle is reset every month, using the new option Reset Every Month.  
For example, if you schedule a daily task to run every two days and the last run of the month is January 31, the next run will be:  
- on February 1 if you select the option Reset Every Month, as the run cycle is reset on the first of each month  
- on February 2 if you do not select the option Reset Every Month  
Note  
The new option is selected by default for the schedules that you've created before version 2022.9.  
See [Schedule a Data Integration Task (Simple Schedule)](7c11059ed3314e1fb753736b7867512c.html).  

### 2022-04-19 · Language Support (Lithuanian)

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.9 · SAP-ID 590834009*

SAP Data Warehouse Cloud now supports Lithuanian in its menus, buttons, messages, and other elements of the user interface.  
See [System Requirements and Technical Prerequisites](70ffed417b054b4692458edbd56c90f5.html).  

### 2022-04-19 · Increase the Size of Your Tenant

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2022.9 · SAP-ID 590833645*

If you haven’t yet allocated all your capacity units or if you’ve acquired additional capacity units, you can allocate them and increase the sizes of your SAP HANA storage and compute, data lake, and (if no size has yet been allocated) SAP BW Bridge.  
See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2022-04-19 · Deploy a wide selection of imported objects

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2022.9 · SAP-ID 590834008*

The Content Network allows you to deploy many imported objects in your Space. You can now chose to deploy newly imported objects or all imported objects in your Space via the Transport application available in the side navigation area.  
See [Importing Content from Another Tenant](b607a12931d74c4a93506ea64c55ab4e.html).  

### 2022-04-19 · Calculated Measures

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.9 · SAP-ID 590833473*

For calculated measures, there are now more complex functions available.  

### 2022-04-05 · New Privilege Allowing Login

*Action: Recommended · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.8 · SAP-ID 590834172*

The privilege Data Warehouse General allows users to log onto SAP Data Warehouse Cloud and is included in all roles except for DW Consumer.  
Note  
If you have created custom roles in an earlier version, we recommend that you adjust them and include the Data Warehouse General privilege. However, if you do not do this, note that users with custom roles including at least one the following privileges are still able to connect onto SAP Data Warehouse Cloud: Data Warehouse Data Builder, Data Warehouse Business Builder, Spaces, Data Warehouse Cloud Data Access Control, Data Warehouse Remote Connection, or Data Warehouse Cloud Data Integration.  
See [Privileges and Permissions](d7350c6823a14733a7a5727bad8371aa.html).  

### 2022-04-05 · New DW Consumer Role

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.8 · SAP-ID 590833646*

The new DW Consumer role allows users to consume data from spaces of which they are members, but does not allow them to log onto SAP Data Warehouse Cloud.  
This role is intended for business analysts and other users whose only point of contact with SAP Data Warehouse Cloud is using views that are exposed for consumption as sources in SAP Analytics Cloud and other analytics clients.  
See [Standard Roles Delivered with SAP Datasphere](a50a51d80d5746c9b805a2aacbb7e4ee.html).  

### 2022-04-05 · Monitor Memory Consumption While Persisting Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.8 · SAP-ID 590834234*

Task logs for persisted views have been enhanced in the View Persistency Monitor as memory consumption while persisting views can be checked using monitoring views.  
See [Persisting and Monitoring Views](9af04c990f294fd28c00f46763dd8b0d.html).  

### 2022-04-05 · Monitor Data Integration Tasks With Views From the Content Network in New Dedicated Space

*Action: Recommended · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.8 · SAP-ID 590834173*

You can now monitor data integration tasks using pre-configured views provided as business content by SAP via the Content Network.  
As a pre-requisite, you'll create the new dedicated monitoring space (with the space ID <SAP_ADMIN> and the space name <Administration (SAP)>) and then import the <Technical Content: Task Monitoring> package from the Content Network.  
See [Configure Monitoring](9cd0691c44a74f2aa47b52f615f74433.html) and [Working with SAP HANA Monitoring Views](4ab45090c5684ebf8765757a1dfc4e5d.html).  

### 2022-04-05 · Lock Your Partitions to Avoid Unnecessary Data Load

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.8 · SAP-ID 590833812*

When you think the data will no longer change, or no change is expected for a while, you can check the option Locked when you define a partition, and avoid unnecessary data load while persisting a view.  
See [Creating Partitions for Your Persisted Views](9b1b595054c34da5b67ac07595093f82.html).  

### 2022-03-22 · Use the <samp class="ph codeph">dwc</samp> Command Line Interface to Import and Export Entities

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.7 · SAP-ID 590833958*

The dwc command line interface, which could previously only be used by Administrators and Space Administrators, is now available to Modelers who want to import and export entities to and from their space.  
See [Importing and Exporting Objects via the Command Line](649465700ff14cd3ab7aebd72f370115.html).  

### 2022-03-22 · Save a Copy of an Intelligent Lookup

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Preparation · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.7 · SAP-ID 590833552*

The Save  Save As command is now available in the intelligent lookup editor.  
See [Saving and Deploying Objects](7c0b560e2cb94eea86219d78d87f9623.html).  

### 2022-03-22 · Modify Views with Input Parameters

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.7 · SAP-ID 590833959*

You can now modify views containing input parameters including if they are used as sources by other views.  
See [Modifying Objects That Have Dependent Objects](f315863264db489593c7f54f1f7fd83e.html).  

### 2022-03-22 · Copy Associations From View Sources

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.7 · SAP-ID 590833960*

In the graphical view editor, the new Copy From Source… option in the Associations list lets you select from among the associations that are defined on your view’s sources and recreate them in your view.  
See [Create an Association to Define a Semantic Relationship Between Entities](66c6998af9974dac8f54a46515777560.html).  

### 2022-03-22 · Column Selection and Metadata Refresh for Remote Sources in Data Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.7 · SAP-ID 590833961*

You can control the data being read from a remote source by adding/deleting columns from source and by adding new/removed columns from the source based on latest metadata.  
See [Add a Source to a Data Flow](7b50e8e304244ced9ff1e62e2f2fe919.html).  

### 2022-03-22 · Apply Data Access Controls to Views with Input Parameters

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.7 · SAP-ID 590833990*

It is now possible to apply one or more data access controls to a view that contains input parameters.  
See [Create an Input Parameter in a Graphical View](53fa99ad58c04a1ba3bb87288756dabc.html) and [Apply a Data Access Control to a Graphical or SQL View](8f79fc80d6134a89a03837a205d340cd.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Use OAuth 2.0 Authorization for Secure Access to OData Services

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590833813*

You can now use authentication type OAuth 2.0 with Generic OData and SAP SuccessFactors for Analytical Dashboard connections to increase security when authorizing SAP Datasphere to connect to OData services. Generic OData supports the OAuth 2.0 client credentials flow whereas SAP SuccessFactors for Analytical Dashboards supports the SAML bearer assertion flow. For new connections, OAuth 2.0 becomes the default authentication type.  
See:  
- [Generic OData Connections](5d36f1aae68a4e59989c424a66d948c9.html)  
- [SAP SuccessFactors Connections](39df02030d4b411487bacecf9afea4e8.html)  

### 2022-03-08 · Uploading Exchange Rates for Currencies

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590833590*

You can now manually upload exchange rates for currencies from another SAP system to your SAP BW bridge system.  
See [Transferring Exchange Rates for Currencies from SAP Systems](https://help.sap.com/viewer/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/latest/en-US/4a27a96a81661d10e10000000a42189b.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Transferring Currencies, Units, and Fiscal Year Variants

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590833589*

You can now manually upload currencies, units and fiscal year variants from another SAP system to your SAP BW bridge system.  
See [Transferring Global Settings for Currencies, Measures and Fiscal Year Variants from an SAP System](https://help.sap.com/viewer/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/latest/en-US/4a27adea81661d10e10000000a42189b.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Support of Microsoft Edge Browsers Based on Chromium

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590834010*

You can now work with SAP Data Warehouse Cloud in the latest version of the Microsoft Edge browser based on Chromium.  
See [System Requirements and Technical Prerequisites](70ffed417b054b4692458edbd56c90f5.html) .  

### 2022-03-08 · Space Management Redesign

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590834030*

The Space Management page has been refreshed and now loads more quickly and provides more pertinent information, which includes:  
- Redesign of the space tiles - You now click Edit on a tile to open a space and you can select several spaces using a checkbox to delete them in one click.  
- Used Disk bar - Provides a breakdown of the different types of data consuming disk storage.  
- The space status Hot has been renamed to Critical, Green to Healthy, and the status Hibernated has been removed.  
See [Creating Spaces and Allocating Resources](2ace657356d54199b0b87d2327b1a70b.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Shell Conversion

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590834144*

To ease the transition from SAP BW to the SAP Data Warehouse Cloud, you can now transfer data models from SAP BW or SAP BW∕4HANA to SAP BW bridge via shell conversion. This is supported for SAP BW 7.3x, 7.40, and 7.50 and for SAP BW∕4HANA 2021. Shell conversion can be used to transfer metadata such as DataStore objects, InfoObjects, transformations, and Data Transfer Processes. It does not include the transfer of existing data sets. After the transfer, you can load data from the original source systems, the SAP BW system, or the SAP BW∕4HANA system.  
See [3141688](https://me.sap.com/notes/3141688 ).  

### 2022-03-08 · Restrict Data Loads on Remote table

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590834296*

You can restrict data loads on remote tables that use an SDI ABAP or SDI CDI Adapter.  
See [Restrict Remote Table Data Loads](bd1ece5c9f78444c87708ef11eed0a31.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Replicating DataSources

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: SAP BW Bridge · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590834145*

For replicating source systems, you can now replicate the DataSources in the BW Modeling Tools.  
See [Executing Replication](https://help.sap.com/viewer/107a6e8a38b74ede94c833ca3b7b6f51/latest/en-US/78bbe157705f4c27aa5c25e341f3e1cf.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Python 3.9 Support for Script Operator in Data Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590833963*

The Python script operator is now offering support for data manipulation and vector operations via support for the builtins (version 3.9), NumPy (version 1.21.5), and Pandas (version 1.2.5) modules.  
See [Script Operator Python Reference](73e8ba1a69cd4eeba722b458a253779d.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Naming Rules and Reserved Prefixes for Technical Names

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590834011*

Rules and restrictions for technical names in SAP Data Warehouse Cloud have been revised. Certain prefixes are reserved.  
See [Rules for Technical Names](982f9a30d4ab49c8b019cfaf3dc08391.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Modify Permission Entities and Data Access Controls

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590833991*

You can now modify tables and views that are used by data access controls and modify data access controls that are attached to views.  
See [Modifying Objects That Have Dependent Objects](f315863264db489593c7f54f1f7fd83e.html) and [Process Source Changes in the Data Access Control Editor](3c470e82554145658a5029d7a1dca4a0.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Import SAP S/4HANA Entities

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590833962*

You can now import existing entities from SAP S/4HANA Cloud into SAP Data Warehouse Cloud in order to build new models on top of them or enhance them.  
See [Importing Entities with Semantics from SAP S/4HANA](845fedbd28574aa8b84239df848936f6.html).  

### 2022-03-08 · Enable Model Import for <span class="ph uicontrol">SAP S/4HANA Cloud</span> Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.6 · SAP-ID 590833814*

To allow importing entities from SAP S/4HANA Cloud with the new import wizard, you can now enable the Model Import feature for SAP S/4HANA Cloud connections.  
See [SAP S/4HANA Cloud Connections](a98e5ffdf47c44d9a845dca01a18bd82.html).  

### 2022-02-16 · Input Parameter Default Values

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.4 · SAP-ID 590833964*

You can now set a default value for each of your input parameters. When a user is required to enter a value for a parameter, they can accept the default value or override it with their own chosen value.  
See [Create an Input Parameter in a Graphical View](53fa99ad58c04a1ba3bb87288756dabc.html).  

### 2022-02-16 · Guide renamed

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2022.4 · SAP-ID 590834012*

The former Welcome Guide for SAP Data Warehouse Cloud, SAP BW Bridge is renamed to [Getting Started With SAP Datasphere, SAP BW Bridge](32b4861ce7d94ebd9f5abd854691582f.html)  

### 2022-02-16 · Create Partitions For Your Remote Tables Connected With an <span class="ph pname">SAP HANA Smart Data Access</span> Adapter

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.4 · SAP-ID 590833815*

From the Remote Table Monitor – Detail screen, you now have the possibility to split your remote table data into smaller partitions, and thus enable more efficient data transfer of large datasets from SAP HANA smart data access sources.  
See [Partitioning Remote Table Data Loads](a218d2715fd54b71a0b2060f4f97cf20.html).  

### 2022-02-16 · Configure the Size of Your New Tenant

*Action: Required · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.4 · SAP-ID 590833647*

When you log into your new SAP Data Warehouse Cloud tenant for the first time, you need to allocate the capacity units to the storage and compute resources to obtain a configuration that fits your exact needs. You do this in a dedicated page of SAP Data Warehouse Cloud.  
See [Configure the Size of Your SAP Datasphere Tenant](33f8ef4ec359409fb75925a68c23ebc3.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · Support for Associations and Shared Objects as Sources in SQLScript Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590834299*

You can now create associations in a SQLScript view. You can also, now, use shared objects as sources in a SQLScript view. When a shared object is added to the code, it's added in the form "spaceName"."objectName" and can directly be accessed, avoiding the need to duplicate the object in your space.  
See [Creating an SQL View](81920e4d583f45fd8761c662d3c8abab.html)  

### 2022-01-29 · Scheduled automatic Task Logs Deletion

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590833648*

Deleting old task logs that are no longer needed can be useful to release storage space. This is why SAP Data Warehouse Cloud has now a log deletion schedule activated by default. You can change this schedule defining your own criteria or decide to take immediate deletion actions.  
See [Delete Task Logs to Reduce Storage Consumption](c6902024ecd74956b4ba2d1c67ccb073.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · Quickly Maintain Data with a URL Access

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590834297*

Maintaining an object's data is facilitated with an URL link access. You can:  
- share the URL link to other users with the proper editing rights  
- bookmark the URL link in your browser for frequent use  
The URL link brings you directly in the object's data editor and allows you to quickly maintain data contained in the object.See [Maintain Local Table Data](4bd5e641be48409c8c79336df0c4a3c7.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · New Guidance for Keys and Associations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590833965*

New validation messages help you set appropriate key columns for your entities based on their semantic usage and to map the necessary key columns when creating associations.  
See [Set Key Columns to Uniquely Identify Records](d9ef2c91f6d647e584bad51999e441cd.html) and [Create an Association to Define a Semantic Relationship Between Entities](66c6998af9974dac8f54a46515777560.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · New <cite class="cite">Integrating Data and Managing Spaces</cite> Guide

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590834013*

To provide more role-focused information, the former Administration Guide has been split into two guides:  
- [Administering SAP Datasphere](70ee87c19d3b47459ac7f2c8d4fea48a.html) - Providing documentation for Administrators.  
- [Integrating Data and Managing Spaces in SAP Datasphere](8f98d3c917f94452bafe288055b60b35.html) - Providing documentation for Space Administrators and Data Integrators.  

### 2022-01-29 · Make a Local Copy of Your Object with the <span class="ph uicontrol">Save As</span> Function

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590833966*

You can now create local copies of objects with the Save As function.  
See the various Business Layer's and the Data Layer's editors' pages.  

### 2022-01-29 · Join Optimization in Data Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590834298*

You can now configure join optimization for Join operator in a data flow to be either automatic or manual.  
See [Create a Join in a Data Flow](e57633deb333431c8787f2f57b1cb08a.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · Import Remote Tables

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590833967*

Up to now, the import of remote tables has only been available indirectly through the Data Builder when creating a view or a E/R model. Now you can import remote tables into your space directly from the Data Builder start page or the Repository Explorer.  
See [Import Remote Tables](fd04efbac29c44fb8cfeaf2166b3d882.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · Import <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP BW</span> Analysis Authorizations from <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP BW∕4HANA</span> Model Transfer Connections

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590833992*

You can now import analysis authorizations defined in SAP BW∕4HANA v2.0 and higher systems into SAP Data Warehouse Cloud from SAP BW∕4HANA Model Transfer Connections, either directly in the wizard when importing a query or in the Data Access Controls app.  
See [Importing SAP BW∕4HANA Models](a3d4a2f91bea4810ba8839ff73577dac.html) and [Import SAP BW and SAP BW∕4HANA Analysis Authorizations](f56e4271dc4943aa9f21223ce5c93873.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · Guides renamed

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: General · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: Changed · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590834014*

The former Modeling Guide is renamed to [Acquiring, Preparing, and Modeling Data with SAP Datasphere](b4a5d02cefdf45478e7376860c985202.html).  
The former Welcome Guide is renamed to [Getting Started with SAP Datasphere](2377bb6efedb42d9a6796f0d7d68f1a4.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · Dynamic Memory Allocation for Data Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590833968*

You can now manually allocate the memory usage for your data flow if your data flow run is facing out-of-memory failures.  
See [Creating a Data Flow](e30fd1417e954577baae3246ea470c3f.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · Create Consumption Model from Data Preview

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590833474*

For business entities and fact models you can save the data preview as a consumption model.  
See [Previewing Data in Business Builder Objects](3c58d6eb6b8b49e99e25a795f65e7173.html).  

### 2022-01-29 · Automatic Restart of Data Flows

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.3 · SAP-ID 590833969*

You can now enable automatic restart of data flows in case of any failures or system upgrade.  
See [Creating a Data Flow](e30fd1417e954577baae3246ea470c3f.html).  

### 2022-01-11 · Schedule Data Flow in Data Flow Builder

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590833971*

You can now schedule a data flow from the data flow builder. You can also modify the existing schedule of a data flow or delete it.  
See [Creating a Data Flow](e30fd1417e954577baae3246ea470c3f.html).  

### 2022-01-11 · Remove Unnecessary Remote Table Columns to Reduce Data Loads

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590834300*

You can now reduce the volume of data loaded from your source systems and improve performance by removing unnecessary columns from the definition of a remote table.  
See [Restrict Remote Table Data Loads](bd1ece5c9f78444c87708ef11eed0a31.html).  

### 2022-01-11 · Read and Write Entities in Spaces from the Command Line

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590834031*

You can now read entity (table and view) definitions (in the standard CSN syntax) from a space with the dwc command line interface, using the -d option. If you include entity definitions in your space definition file when creating or updating a space, then the entities are added to the space.  
See [Manage Spaces and Space Access via the Command Line](5eac5b71e2d34c32b63f3d8d47a0b1d0.html).  

### 2022-01-11 · Provide and Sell Data Products with the Data Sharing Cockpit

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590833835*

The Data Sharing Cockpit is the tool you use as a data provider. As a data provider, your main objective is to offer valuable data products to the users of the Data Marketplace in SAP Data Warehouse Cloud. To manage your products and licenses, to publish new releases, and to maintain your data provider profile page, the Data Sharing Cockpit offers you tailored apps for each task and workflow.  
See [Data Sharing Cockpit - Data Provider's Guide](e479b7b4c95741c7a7a1d42397984c7e.html).  

### 2022-01-11 · Import <span class="ph pname" translate="no">SAP BW</span> Analysis Authorizations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590833993*

You can now import analysis authorizations defined in SAP BW 7.5 SP16 and higher systems into SAP Data Warehouse Cloud.  
See [Import SAP BW and SAP BW∕4HANA Analysis Authorizations](f56e4271dc4943aa9f21223ce5c93873.html).  

### 2022-01-11 · General Availability of Data Marketplace

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590833834*

Data Marketplace is the new easy-to-use platform to exchange diverse, fine-grain data in SAP Data Warehouse Cloud. Each customer of SAP Data Warehouse Cloud can now become their own data publisher and monetize their existing data.  
Note that Data Marketplace and the Data Sharing Cockpit are only available in SAP Data Warehouse Cloud tenants provisioned since January 25, 2021 (see [3148585](https://me.sap.com/notes/3148585)).  

### 2022-01-11 · Find and Acquire Data Products

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Marketplace · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590834245*

The landing page for Data Marketplace targeted at consumers, enables you to find and acquire the right data products:  
- You can search and browse for data products and data providers.  
- You can use filter criteria to limit the search results.  
- After you evaluated the data products, by downloading and testing free sample data for example you can acquire a product using different ways. A data product can be free of charge, be available on request, or require a license key.  
See [Installing Marketplace Data Products](92c35efd6a4945a1a78250539aee9a51.html).  

### 2022-01-11 · Delete Audit Logs

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Privacy and Protection · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590833994*

You can now delete audit logs. Audit logs can consume a large quantity of GB of disk. Deleting them will free up space in your tenant database.  
See [Audit Logging](c78a7c2a3cec4b0897db294d74e00d9b.html).  

### 2022-01-11 · Convert Currencies in a Graphical View

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.2 · SAP-ID 590833970*

Calculate values in different currencies with a Currency Conversion column. You can create a Currency Conversion column via the Calculated Column node in a graphical view.  
See [Create a Currency Conversion Column in a Graphical View](6e3d8bed7ece4c27ba10e2cc523915fe.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Text Associations

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833475*

Business entities now support the definition of attributes with texts and language dependent texts. This enables you to display your data with ID and text, or with a language dependent text, when you preview or analyze your data.  
See [Define Attributes](270bb3d19df04d96adca12d182bafdfb.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Remote Table Data and View Persistency

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833973*

From the Data Builder, you can now act on your remote table data and view persistency directly without switching back to the Data Integration Monitor:  
- Remote Table:  
Load new snapshot of data, remove replicated data and enable real-time access directly from the table editor. You can also define a schedule to automate these actions.  
See [Importing Tables and Views from Sources](7c4acd33e39a451e99c87f0661772443.html).  
- View Persistency:  
Automate the view persistency actions defining a schedule.  
See [Persist Data in a Graphical or SQL View](9bd12cf116ae40e09cdba8b60cf75e11.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Processing Source Changes in the Table Editor

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833974*

The structure of tables that you import from an Open SQL schema or an HDI container can change over time, with columns added or being removed, for example. You can now click Refresh in the table editor to check for and import these structural changes, with validation messages being displayed if the changes risk impacting objects that use the table as a source.  
See [Process Source Changes in the Table Editor](622328b59afa40fd810b44ac84d6afd3.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Intelligent Lookup

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Preparation · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833553*

When combining data, there may be no column in your primary entity that contains data to uniquely identify a record in the other entity, and which would thus allow the creation of a standard join. Or, if such a column (a foreign key) exists, its data may be incomplete or unreliable. This can be particularly common when one of the entities comes from outside your organization. It may require a lot of manual work in spreadsheets to join the entities, and the results of this work may not be easy to reuse when new data arrives.  
Intelligent lookup is a business-centric, interactive data harmonization environment for subject matter experts, which lets you iteratively join entities by defining rules to match records and then reviewing the results.  
See [Creating an Intelligent Lookup](8f29f801faea4d48816d0339777f9d16.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Information Message and Disabled Features when Run-Time Database is Not Available

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590834032*

On rare occasions when the run-time database is not available, an information message is displayed and certain features that depend on the run-time are disabled.  
See [Save and Deploy Your Space](95973c709373415ebf42f6446a909924.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Improved Visibility for Input Parameters in Graphical Views

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833976*

The number of input parameters in a view is now displayed on its symbol (as a source or output) in the graphical view editor. In addition, when you select an input parameter in the output node side panel, each node in which it is used and, if appropriate, its source is highlighted.  
See [Create an Input Parameter in a Graphical View](53fa99ad58c04a1ba3bb87288756dabc.html) and [Visualize Column and Input Parameter Lineages in a Graphical View](a2426b7597c64d5fbfcaeb67415787bd.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Exception Aggregation

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Business Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833476*

Beside the standard aggregation types (SUM, MIN, MAX, AVG) you can now also use exception aggregation for measures.  
See [Define Measures](903acb8d378d4984af8ad4f694d56529.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Excel File as a Source in Data Flow

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833972*

You can now import Excel files (.xlsx, .xls) from a remote file storage to your data flow and configure the worksheet to be used.  
See [Creating a Data Flow](e30fd1417e954577baae3246ea470c3f.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Controlling the Visibility of Measures and Attributes in Stories

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Modeling · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833975*

You can hide or show measures and attributes in Stories in a graphical view.  
See [Specify Measures to Analyze](33f7f291538a44a293a89f6f1cf1fa81.html) and [Specify Attributes as Keys, Units, and Other Characteristics](cedc59c994cf488bb6b43b105e72c4d3.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Connections Removed from Space Management

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Space Management · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590834314*

Starting with this version, you no longer create and manage connections in spaces but directly in the new dedicated app Connections. You can still open the new app from the Connections section in Space Management.  
See [Create a Connection](c2165842082c43fc85bad9f0c97572bb.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Connections App

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590834235*

To improve usability and provide instant access to creating and managing connections, they have been moved from Space Management into a separate Connections app that you can open from the side navigation area with the  (Connections) button. You can still open the app from the Connections section in Space Management.  
See [Create a Connection](c2165842082c43fc85bad9f0c97572bb.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Connection Type <span class="ph uicontrol">Microsoft Azure Blob Storage</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590834237*

With the new connection type, you can connect to Microsoft Azure Blob Storage and use the connection in data flows.  
See [Microsoft Azure Blob Storage Connections](df5a7c56ac21472c973f6fab341f3991.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · Connection Type <span class="ph uicontrol">Generic SFTP</span>

*Action: Info only · Area: Other · Category: Data Integration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590834236*

With the new connection type, you can connect to SFTP-based file shares in your local network to consume CSV, JSON, and other files in data flows.  
See [Generic SFTP Connections](b645de78a8374c24871ab6169be40d35.html).  

### 2021-12-01 · <samp class="ph codeph">dwc</samp> Command Line Interface Update

*Action: Recommended · Area: Other · Category: Administration · Component: SAP Datasphere · Environment: Cloud Foundry · Type: New · Version: 2022.1 · SAP-ID 590833649*

A new version of the dwc command line interface is released with various technical improvements. To update to the latest version, run npm install -g @sap/dwc-cli  
See [Install or Update the SAP Datasphere Command Line Interface](f7d5eddf20a34a1aa48d8e2c68a44e28.html).
